However, computing the exact attention mass requires reading every key, so the problem of block selection ultimately reduces to approximating this mass from a compact summary instead of the full keys. Via a cumulant expansion, we show why existing methods falter: their selection strategies attempt to estimate the attention mass, but are confined to a first-order approximation. Therefore, we propose COBS (Cumulant Order Block Sparse Attention), an attention method that builds on NSA, incorporating a novel selector that stores a compressed second-order statistic per block. On the 32k RULER long-context retrieval benchmark, COBS raises the NSA baseline's mean score from 0.2999 to 0.8195, approaching dense attention at 0.9040 and closing about 86% of the gap, while using only 1.21x the KV cache read traffic of the NSA baseline and 15.15x less read traffic than dense. The same model preserves short-context behavior and attains lower position-wise negative log-likelihood (NLL) than dense attention in our comparison.

</details>


### [37] [EvoLP: Self-Evolving Latency Predictor for Model Compression in Real-Time Edge Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09063)
*Shuo Huai,Hao Kong,Shiqing Li,Xiangzhong Luo,Ravi Subramaniam,Christian Makaya,Qian Lin,Weichen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoLP框架，一种能够在边缘设备上精确预测模型推理延迟且可在压缩过程中持续进化的预测器，在三个边缘设备和四种模型变体上优于现有方法，并能有效指导满足延迟约束的模型压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备资源有限且应用要求实时性，需要面向延迟目标的神经网络压缩，但在真实设备上测量延迟困难且昂贵，因此需要一种高效准确的延迟预测方法。

Method: 提出EvoLP框架，该延迟预测器能够在网络压缩过程中进化，不断提升延迟预测精度。

Result: 在三个边缘设备和四种模型变体上的评估表明，EvoLP优于先前最先进的方法；将其集成到模型压缩框架中，能在满足严格延迟约束的同时有效指导压缩过程以获得更高模型精度。

Conclusion: EvoLP是一种有效的边缘设备推理延迟预测框架，可进化提升预测精度，并开源在GitHub上。

Abstract: Edge devices are increasingly utilized for deploying deep learning applications on embedded systems. The real-time nature of many applications and the limited resources of edge devices necessitate latency-targeted neural network compression. However, measuring latency on real devices is challenging and expensive. Therefore, this letter presents a novel and efficient framework, named EvoLP, to accurately predict the inference latency of models on edge devices. This predictor can evolve to achieve higher latency prediction precision during the network compression process. Experimental results demonstrate that EvoLP outperforms previous state-of-the-art approaches by being evaluated on three edge devices and four model variants. Moreover, when incorporated into a model compression framework, it effectively guides the compression process for higher model accuracy while satisfying strict latency constraints. We open source EvoLP at https://github.com/ntuliuteam/EvoLP.

</details>


### [38] [Pitfalls and Remedies for Multi-Task Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09073)
*Carl Hvarfner,Sam Daulton,Max Balandat,Eytan Bakshy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多任务高斯过程在贝叶斯优化迁移学习中的根本缺陷：即使源任务与目标任务存在最简单的仿射变换关系，标准的多任务GP也会错误估计跨任务相关性。问题源于两个结构性机制：逐任务标准化引入的有限样本对齐误差，以及边际似然在非重叠设计点上对相关性的识别速率过低。作者提出三种保守修复方案：将任务均值和尺度提升为模型参数、限制任务协方差为非负、使部分源和目标设计点共位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战多任务高斯过程作为贝叶斯优化中迁移学习默认方法的有效性，指出其在最简单的仿射相关场景下也会失败，并深入剖析失败的根本原因。

Method: 通过受控实验复现标准多任务GP的失败，从逐任务标准化的有限样本对齐误差和边际似然的慢速识别率两个独立机制进行理论归因，并提出三种保守修复策略。

Result: 标准多任务GP在仿射相关任务间错误估计相关性；三种修复策略在简单场景下恢复基线性能，但在更困难场景和大多数变体中失效。

Conclusion: 多任务高斯过程作为迁移学习默认方法的可靠性存疑，尤其在非重叠设计和简单仿射场景下表现不佳，所提修复仅能缓解部分问题。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization routinely warm-starts a target experiment with data from related source tasks, and the multi-task Gaussian process is the textbook surrogate for the job. We revisit this default in a controlled setting and find that it misestimates the cross-task correlation even in the simplest non-trivial case, affinely related source and target tasks, where a working transfer learning method should obviously succeed. We trace the failure to two independent structural mechanisms. Per-task standardization, the textbook fix for the affine slice ambiguity, propagates a finite-sample alignment error into the recovered correlation. The marginal likelihood itself identifies the correlation only at a per-sample rate that a Gaussian process at non-overlapping designs further dilutes. We propose three conservative remedies that follow from the analysis: promoting per-task means and scales to model parameters, restricting the task covariance to non-negative correlations, and co-locating part of the source and target designs. Across synthetic multi-task problems and surrogate-based hyperparameter tuning transfer, these remedies recover the target-only baseline on the simple instances, while the broader failure persists on harder instances and across most rank-based and latent-context variants.

</details>


### [39] [A Survey on the Green Development of Large Models: From Resource-Efficient Architectures to Hardware-Software Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09084)
*Linhui Xiao,Guiping Cao,Mingyue Guo,Xianchao Guan,Fan Yang,Ming Tao,Xin Li,Yuxin Peng,Yaowei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了大模型绿色发展的全栈软硬件协同设计路径，涵盖高效架构（注意力优化、线性复杂度模型、稀疏化与合并）、训练部署策略（数据高效学习、参数高效微调、计算压缩）、节能硬件（AI芯片、内存优化、跨平台部署）以及大模型在可持续发展关键领域的应用（DeepSeek、遥感、国家级基础设施等），并讨论了持续学习、存算一体硬件和标准化评估等未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模AI模型的快速扩张带来了显著性能突破，但同时引发了计算成本、能耗和环境可持续性的严峻问题。本文旨在提供一条通往可持续、可扩展、社会责任感的大模型发展的系统性路线图。

Method: 系统性综述方法，从高效模型构建（注意力算子优化、线性复杂度架构、模型稀疏化与合并）、训练部署策略（数据高效学习、参数高效微调、计算压缩）、节能AI硬件（主流AI芯片、内存优化、跨平台部署、可持续基础设施）以及大模型在可持续关键领域的应用等多个维度进行梳理。

Result: 归纳了绿色大模型发展的多项关键技术路径，指出了持续学习范式、存算一体硬件和标准化评估协议等关键未来方向，并提供了全栈视角的技术路线图。

Conclusion: 大模型的绿色发展需要从算法、硬件到基础设施的全栈协同设计，未来应重点关注持续学习范式、以内存为中心的硬件架构以及标准化的评估体系，以实现可持续、可扩展且具有社会责任感的大模型发展。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of large-scale AI models has led to significant performance breakthroughs across diverse domains, yet it has also raised critical concerns regarding computational costs, energy consumption, and environmental sustainability. This survey provides a comprehensive overview of the green development of large models, emphasizing resource-efficient architectures and full-stack hardware-software co-design. We systematically review recent advances in efficient model construction, including attention operator optimization, linear-complexity architectures, and model sparsification and merging, as well as training and deployment strategies such as data-efficient learning, parameter-efficient fine-tuning, and computational compression. Beyond algorithmic improvements, we explore energy-efficient AI hardware, including mainstream AI chips, memory optimization, cross-platform deployment, and sustainable infrastructure. Furthermore, we examine how large models are being applied to sustainability-critical domains such as DeepSeek, remote sensing interpretation, national-scale infrastructure, and global initiatives. Finally, we discuss key challenges and future directions, highlighting the need for continual learning paradigms, memory-centric hardware, and standardized evaluation protocols. This survey aims to offer a holistic roadmap toward sustainable, scalable, and socially responsible development of large models. Paper homepage: https://cje.ejournal.org.cn/article/doi/10.23919/cje.2025.00.438

</details>


### [40] [EXHOLD: Experience-Aware Real-Time Hold Control for Large-Scale Ride-Hailing Matching at DiDi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09090)
*Xu Liu,Kai Wan,Zihao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EXHOLD是一个两阶段框架，将体验感知的司乘配对评估与等待时间执行解耦，通过决策模型分配体验等级和约束优化生成单调等待时间表，在滴滴巴西生产系统中显著提升了订单完成率、司机收入和漏斗效率，同时降低了乘客取消率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模网约车中，hold control（延迟匹配）是提升司乘体验的关键机制，但现有工业方法依赖多预测模型的启发式阈值，在非平稳流量下脆弱且难以针对多目标体验信号进行优化。

Method: 两阶段框架：Stage I学习决策模型，通过聚合匹配漏斗中的满意度信号将每对司乘分配到离散可解释的体验等级；Stage II通过经验分位数的约束优化求解单调等待时间表，显式强制执行服务护栏以限制不必要等待，同时最大化整体体验改善。

Result: 在滴滴巴西生产系统的随机A/B实验中，EXHOLD在订单完成率、司机收入、乘客取消率和漏斗效率等方面取得一致改进。消融和行为分析验证了两个阶段均不可或缺，且策略在时空异质性下做出校准决策。

Conclusion: EXHOLD已部署于巴西生产环境，有效提升了市场效率和体验，证明了所提框架在实际大规模系统中的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: In large-scale ride-hailing, hold control is a critical mechanism for improving passenger-driver experience. By selectively delaying certain driver-order pairs, the system waits for better opportunities, reduces cancellations, and mitigates wasted driver effort. However, existing industrial hold strategies often rely on heuristic thresholding over multiple predictive models, which can be brittle under non-stationary traffic and hard to optimize for multi-objective experience signals.
  We propose EXHOLD, a deployable two-stage framework decoupling experience-aware pair assessment from hold-time execution. In Stage I, we learn a decision model assigning each driver-order pair to discrete, interpretable experience tiers by optimizing a unified objective that aggregates satisfaction signals across the matching funnel. In Stage II, we solve for a monotone hold-time schedule via constrained optimization over empirical quantiles. This explicitly enforces service guardrails bounding the unnecessary holding of promising matches while maximizing overall experience improvement.
  We evaluate EXHOLD through randomized A/B experiments in DiDi's production system in Brazil. Results show consistent gains in marketplace efficiency and experience: EXHOLD increases trip completion and driver income, significantly reduces passenger cancellations, and improves funnel efficiency. Ablations and behavioral analyses confirm both stages are essential and that the policy makes calibrated decisions under spatiotemporal heterogeneity. EXHOLD is currently deployed, serving production traffic in Brazil.

</details>


### [41] [A Personalized Computational Framework for Assessing the Sufficiency of Partially Observed Data in Healthcare AI models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09165)
*Qingchu Jin,Felistas Mazhude,Jamie B. Rabb,Robert S. Kramer,Douglas B. Sawyer,Raimond L. Winslow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出特征充分性分析（FSA），用于判断现有临床特征子集是否足以达到全特征容量（FFC）的预测性能，从而避免不必要的额外数据采集，支持可信的AI辅助临床决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在临床应用中，机器学习模型所需的特征往往不完整，导致预测任务难以执行。现有方法缺乏对现有特征是否足以达到完整特征集预测性能的系统性评估。

Method: 定义全特征容量（FFC）概念，提出特征充分性分析（FSA），通过估计缺失变量在已有特征条件下的分布，进行患者级别的充分性评估，判断现有特征子集是否足以达到FFC。

Result: 通过术后持续通气预测和10年死亡率预测两个案例验证了FSA的有效性；FSA还提供了基于预测充分性的临床可解释特征排序方法，可识别内在难预测的患者群体，并支持成本感知的临床数据采集优化。

Conclusion: FSA是一种通用的计算分析方法，能够判断不完整临床信息是否足以支持可信的AI辅助临床决策，有助于促进医疗AI系统在多样化临床场景中的前瞻性部署。

Abstract: Achieving early and timely diagnosis and treatment for disease is a major challenge. Recent applications of machine learning (ML) algorithms trained on patient data have shown promise in many different settings for predicting the patient health state. A challenge often faced when applying these ML algorithms is that at any given time, not all clinical variables (features) needed as input to perform prediction tasks are available. We define the concept of full-feature-capacity (FFC) to refer to prediction performance when such algorithms make use of all features on which they were trained. We then introduce Feature Sufficiency Analysis (FSA) - an analysis for determining whether a subset of all clinical features needed by an AI model is sufficient to achieve FFC. FSA estimates the underlying distributions of missing variables conditioned on features that are available. FSA provides a patient-specific assessment of whether the existing set of measured features achieves FFC. If yes, then there is no need to acquire further inputs and a ML-based prediction. We provide two case studies: prediction of need for postoperative prolonged ventilation in patients recovering from heart surgery; 10-year mortality prediction in an outpatient cohort. We also demonstrate that FSA also provides a clinically interpretable feature-ranking methodology based on prediction sufficiency, identifies intrinsically hard-to-predict patient populations, and has the potential to perform cost-aware optimization for clinical data acquisition. FSA provides a generic computational approach for determining whether incomplete clinical information is sufficient to support trustworthy AI-assisted clinical decision-making, thereby facilitating the prospective deployment of healthcare AI systems across diverse clinical settings.

</details>

Abstract: We study the integration of variational quantum circuits (VQCs) into diffusion models through a squeeze-and-excitation (SE) channel-modulation scaffold that isolates the quantum contribution. Using a role-matched classical control and multi-seed significance testing across DDPM and latent diffusion on MNIST and CIFAR-10, with a score-based NCSN study on MNIST, we find that quantum cores achieve comparable mean FID to the classical control across DDPM and latent diffusion, while paired sampling-seed tests for EfficientSU2 detect no statistically significant difference. Although the quantum cores use $4.5$--$9\times$ fewer core parameters than the role-matched control, parameter-matched classical controls attain comparable mean FID, so the experiments do not establish a quantum parameter-efficiency advantage. We further identify a structural failure in score-based NCSN: the unbounded score target, proportional to $1/σ$, drives angle-embedding inputs far beyond the $2π$ period of rotation gates, causing phase aliasing and collapse of the quantum modulator. A bounding transformation, $θ\leftarrow π\tanh(\cdot)$, maps inputs to the non-aliasing domain and substantially improves both quantum cores. Since all circuits are classically simulated at a few-qubit scale, we do not claim quantum advantage. Instead, the study provides a fair-comparison protocol for quantum-enhanced generative models and a mechanistic account of when and why angle embeddings fail.

This paper presents a Variational Graph Autoencoder (VGAE) that detects the power flow solution feasibility, using the IEEE 118-bus case, to assess the validity of the solutions provided by AI-driven solvers.

### [44] [Interference and Retention in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09202)
*Julius Störk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将遗忘建模为任务间干扰，在冻结特征和深度网络场景下推导出干扰能量的数学形式，并基于任务支持集几何关系提出无回放、无Fisher的IGFA方法，在可分离任务上实现无损保持，在冲突任务上将不可逆遗忘转化为可恢复的塑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法依赖回放、弹性正则化或蒸馏等事后机制，这些方法并未直接建模遗忘的本质。本文认为遗忘应被直接视为任务间的干扰，从而从几何角度理解并解决持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。

Method: 首先在冻结特征回归中推导出新任务学习对旧任务的干扰能量等于遗忘量；在深度网络中通过路径平均曲率近似该量。基于任务支持集几何关系：当支持集不交叠时可结构性地消除遗忘，当支持集冲突时存在不可避免的失真下限。据此提出Interference-Gated Functional Allocation (IGFA)，通过任务感知正交化在任务对齐时共享方向、冲突时保护方向，无需回放和Fisher矩阵。

Result: IGFA在结构可分离任务上实现无损保持；在不可分离任务上将不可逆遗忘转化为可恢复的塑性；在不相似任务流上匹配最强无回放结构基线；在相似任务上优于无条件投影方法，保留迁移收益。

Conclusion: 遗忘的本质是任务间的干扰能量，通过几何分析可以设计更优的持续学习策略。IGFA方法验证了该理论的有效性，在无需回放和Fisher信息的前提下取得了领先性能。

Abstract: Continual learning commonly relies on post-hoc mechanisms such as replay, elastic regularization, or distillation. This work argues that forgetting should instead be modeled directly as interference between tasks. In the frozen-feature regime, forgetting from learning a new task is exactly the interference energy induced on the old task. In deep networks, the same quantity is recovered through path-averaged curvature with minimal additional forward passes.
  When task supports are disjoint, forgetting can be eliminated structurally and when task supports overlap in conflicting directions, a non-zero distortion floor is unavoidable. The same geometry optimally merges models through task-aware orthogonalization. From this analysis we derive Interference-Gated Functional Allocation (IGFA), a replay-free, Fisher-free method that shares directions when tasks align and protects them when they conflict. Across benchmarks, IGFA achieves lossless retention when tasks are structurally separable and moves unavoidable cost from irreversible forgetting into deferred but recoverable plasticity when they are not. It matches the strongest replay-free structural baselines on dissimilar-task streams and improves on unconditional projection when similarity makes transfer worth preserving.

</details>


### [45] [Present but Rescaled: Chat-to-Agent Transfer of Additive Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09156)
*Lucas Pinto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次系统研究加法激活引导（additive activation steering）从对话（chat）到工具使用智能体（ReAct agent）的迁移效应。发现注入方向在表示层面保持稳定，但行为耦合效果因模型和上下文而异，某些模型下拒绝绕过（refusal bypass）放大至2倍，另一些则衰减。这表明智能体部署下加法引导的安全性不可预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加法激活引导主要在单轮对话中校准，但目标模型越来越多地被部署为使用工具的ReAct智能体。这种部署环境的迁移是否影响引导效果及其安全性尚不清楚。

Method: 采用匹配信息设计：相同内容分别以纯对话和ReAct工具使用片段呈现，加入匹配范数的随机方向控制，每轮重新编码转录以排除KV-cache污染。在三个模型家族上测量表示层面的方向强度和行为层面的耦合效应，结合方向消融对比和预注册实验定位重缩放来源。

Result: 注入方向在晚期层达到接近全强度（agent/chat比0.83-1.16），但行为耦合完全重置：Qwen2.5-7B上拒绝绕过放大（T=1.45），Gemma-2-9B放大至2.00倍，Yi-1.5-9B衰减至0.43倍。方向消融不放大（T=0.93），但加法注入放大（T=1.50），二者增益差20.1个点。重缩放定位于ReAct格式框架本身，而非观察边界。

Conclusion: 智能体部署下加法引导的安全性不可预测：某些模型拒绝绕过放大至2.00倍，另一些则衰减。部署不能假设给定模型在加法引导下是安全的。

Abstract: Additive activation steering (injecting a scaled residual-stream direction during generation) is calibrated almost entirely in single-turn chat, yet the models it targets are increasingly deployed as tool-using ReAct agents. We present the first systematic chat-to-agent transfer study of additive steering, coupling behavioral measurement with a representation read-out in a matched-information design: the same items rendered as plain chat or as a ReAct tool-use episode, with matched-norm random-direction controls and the transcript re-encoded every turn to exclude KV-cache contamination. Transfer is real but rescaled, and the right description is a dissociation: the injected direction reaches the late layers at near-full strength in every setting and model tested (install-site agent-over-chat ratios 0.83-1.16 across three families), while the behavioral coupling is reset per model and context. On Qwen2.5-7B a refusal bypass vector amplifies in the agent (T = 1.45, CI [1.20, 1.78], N = 300); across a powered uniform-protocol distribution the coupling spans amplification (Gemma-2-9B T = 2.00) to attenuation (Yi-1.5-9B T = 0.43, CI [0.29, 0.60]), with no universal constant and a single clean attenuator against a universal sign. Directional ablation of the same axis does not amplify (T = 0.93, CI including 1) while additive injection amplifies (T = 1.50), a 20.1-point gain difference (CI [13.4, 26.8]) that identifies an additive-specific mechanism. Two pre-registered instruments converge to localize the rescaling to the ReAct format scaffold, before any tool observation, rather than to the observation boundary where a dilution account would predict it. The safety implication is immediate and unpredictable: agentic deployment amplifies steering-based refusal bypass by up to 2.00x on some models while others attenuate, so a deployment cannot assume a given model is safe under additive steering.

</details>


### [46] [All you need is SAMPAT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09235)
*Jayadeva,Madhur Aswani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为SAMPAT的三层神经网络架构，通过多元多项式和解析变换实现平滑函数的可证明逼近，生成的封闭代数表达式具有完全可解释性，在保持竞争力的同时提供模型家族选择优化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI/ML最先进方法依赖于深度神经架构，但普遍缺乏可解释性。在实验数据分析中，科学家不仅需要定量预测，还需要从数据中获取洞察，因此可解释性至关重要。

Method: 提出SAMPAT三层神经架构，通过平滑逼近（Smooth Approximation via Multivariate Polynomials and Analytic Transformations）实现。该架构可学习连续可微函数，逼近任意光滑函数，输出为封闭紧凑的代数解析表达式。通过限制神经元连接可生成特定形式逼近（如多项式、三角函数、高斯混合等），无限制时自主学习合适结构。添加跳跃连接后，4-6层SAMPAT可表示主流AI/ML方法。

Result: 在合成数据和基准数据集上，SAMPAT以更简洁的表示达到了有竞争力的性能。许多任务中两层SAMPAT已足够。可分解多项式、建模非线性系统。支持在训练过程中同时优化模型家族选择和参数。

Conclusion: SAMPAT通过可解释的代数表达式实现了平滑函数逼近，在保持性能的同时提供了完全可解释性，并允许在训练过程中优化模型家族选择，为可解释AI/ML提供了新范式。

Abstract: The current state of the art in AI/ML rests on deep neural architectures, which, in general, suffer from a lack of interpretability. Interpretability is crucial to gleaning insights while analyzing experimental data, where quantitative predictions may not be adequate for a scientist. We present a three layer neural architecture, SAMPAT (Smooth Approximation via Multivariate Polynomials and Analytic Transformations), that can provably learn a continuous, everywhere differentiable function, that can approximate any smooth function arbitrarily closely. SAMPAT's approximant can be expressed as a closed and compact algebraic, analytic expression, providing complete interpretability. Experiments on synthetic and benchmark datasets indicate that SAMPAT yields competitive performance with simpler representations. For many tasks, a two layer SAMPAT suffices. By imposing restrictions on the connectivity between neurons, SAMPAT may be used to provide a range of approximants, including regular and trigonometric polynomials, rational expressions, Gaussians, mixtures of Gaussians, as well as arbitrary combinations of the same; without restrictions, it learns a suitable structure. SAMPAT may be used to factorize polynomials and model nonlinear systems. With the addition of skip connections, a 4 to 6 layer SAMPAT is adequate to represent a substantive range of methods widely used in AI/ML, allowing the choice of a model's family, not just its parameters, to also be optimized as part of the learning process.

</details>


### [47] [COAST: Context-Aware Differential Learning for Gene Expression Prediction in Spatial Transcriptomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09166)
*Keunho Byeon,Sunhong Park,Jeewoo Lim,Jin Tae Kwak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COAST是一个基于上下文感知差分学习的框架，利用H&E组织病理学图像预测空间基因表达，通过联合优化绝对表达回归和符号差分回归，在多个数据集上取得了一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学成本高、通量低，现有方法主要监督绝对表达，而忽略了空间点之间相对表达关系这一有价值的信息。

Method: COAST包含三部分：(1) 局部和全局上下文特征的类型特异性调制；(2) Transformer编码器聚合目标与上下文点令牌以捕获细粒度局部模式和切片级结构；(3) 联合训练目标，结合绝对表达回归与目标-上下文点之间的符号差分回归。

Result: 在多个空间转录组数据集上，COAST在相关性指标和分布指标上均取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 上下文感知的差分学习能够有效提升基于组织病理学图像的空间基因表达预测性能，表明了相对表达关系作为监督信号的潜在价值。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics enables profiling of spatial gene expression but is limited by high cost and low throughput, motivating prediction from H&E histopathology images. Existing context-aware methods mainly supervise absolute expression, while relative expression relationships between spots are rarely used explicitly. We propose COAST, a context-aware differential learning framework for spatial gene expression prediction. COAST conditions the local and global context features with type-specific modulation and aggregates the target and context spot tokens using a Transformer encoder to capture both fine-grained local patterns and slide-level structure. It is trained with a joint objective that combines absolute expression regression with signed differential regression between the target and context spots. Experiments on multiple spatial transcriptomics datasets show consistent improvements in correlation- and distribution-based metrics, demonstrating the effectiveness of context-aware differential learning for histology-based spatial gene expression prediction.

</details>


### [48] [Risk-Aware General-Utility Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09298)
*Pedro P. Santos,Fábio Vital,Alberto Sardinha,Francisco S. Melo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了风险感知的通用效用马尔可夫决策过程（GUMDPs），提出基于熵风险度量（ERM）的框架，并采用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）方法进行在线规划求解，实验验证了该方法在多种任务（标准MDPs、最大状态熵探索、模仿学习、多目标MDPs）中优化风险感知行为的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUMDPs框架缺乏对风险感知目标的考虑，而实际决策中需要在期望性能与风险规避之间进行权衡。作者旨在将风险度量引入GUMDPs，使代理能够在最大化期望性能的同时控制风险。

Method: 首先形式化定义了风险感知GUMDPs框架，聚焦于熵风险度量（ERM）；然后利用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）实现在线规划求解，可证明收敛到任意期望精度；最后通过多种任务进行实验验证。

Result: 所提出的MCTS方法能够在标准MDPs、最大状态熵探索、模仿学习和多目标MDPs等多种任务中成功优化不同风险偏好程度的行为，展示了框架的通用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 风险感知GUMDPs框架能有效融合风险规避与丰富目标函数，基于MCTS的在线规划方法可精确求解此类问题，为风险感知决策提供了统一的解决方案。

Abstract: We study general-utility Markov decision processes (GUMDPs) with risk-aware objectives. In this framework, an agent aims to optimize a risk measure of the distribution of objective values, where the objective function depends on the frequency of visitation of states induced by the agent's policy. First, we motivate, propose, and formalize risk-aware GUMDPs, which enable agents and decision makers to trade off expected performance by risk aversion while benefiting from the rich set of objectives that can be cast under the framework of GUMDPs. We focus our attention on the entropic risk measure (ERM). Second, we show how we can solve risk-aware GUMDPs with ERM objectives by resorting to online planning techniques. In particular, we propose an approach based on Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to provably solve risk-aware GUMDPs up to any desired accuracy. Third, we provide a set of experimental results showcasing that our approach is successful when optimizing for a spectrum of risk-aware behaviors in the context of GUMDPs under diverse tasks (standard MDPs, maximum state entropy exploration, imitation learning, and multi-objective MDPs).

</details>


### [49] [Understanding Schedule-Free Methods in Nonconvex Optimization: Rate Guarantees and Escaping Saddles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09167)
*Jiseok Chae,Donghwan Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对Schedule-Free梯度下降法和随机梯度下降法在非凸优化中的收敛性进行了最坏情况分析，证明了它们能达到一阶方法的最优收敛率，并通过连续时间ODE的Lyapunov分析以及非自治动力系统框架下的严格鞍点规避分析，为其优异性能提供了理论支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schedule-Free方法在无需学习率调度器的情况下展现出与甚至超越带调优调度器的优化器的性能，但其在非凸优化中的收敛理论尚属空白。本文旨在填补这一理论空缺，为Schedule-Free方法的强实证表现提供严格的数学解释。

Method: 基于连续时间极限常微分方程（ODE）建立Lyapunov分析框架，对标准形式的Schedule-Free梯度下降和随机梯度下降进行最坏情况收敛率分析；同时将Schedule-Free梯度下降建模为非自治动力系统，证明在任意小的一次性扰动下能避免严格鞍点。

Result: 证明了Schedule-Free梯度下降和随机梯度下降在光滑但可能非凸的目标函数上达到一阶方法的最优最坏情况收敛率；证明了Schedule-Free梯度下降在非自治动力系统框架下具有严格鞍点规避能力。

Conclusion: 本文为Schedule-Free方法提供了完整的非凸优化收敛理论，从最优收敛率和鞍点规避两个关键角度解释了其强性能表现，为这类方法的进一步发展和应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Schedule-Free methods have attracted growing interest for alleviating the burden of designing and tuning a learning rate scheduler, while matching and sometimes even outperforming optimizers with tuned schedulers. Despite their strong empirical results, their convergence theory in nonconvex optimization, where modern machine learning objectives typically arise, has remained largely unexplored. In this paper, we provide worst-case analyses of Schedule-Free gradient descent and Schedule-Free stochastic gradient descent, in their standard form and without auxiliary modifications or restrictive conditions, for smooth but possibly nonconvex objectives. Based on a Lyapunov analysis derived from the continuous-time limiting ordinary differential equation associated with these methods, we show that Schedule-Free gradient descent and Schedule-Free stochastic gradient descent achieve the optimal worst-case convergence rates attainable among first-order methods. We further formulate Schedule-Free gradient descent as a nonautonomous dynamical system and prove strict-saddle avoidance under an arbitrarily small one-time perturbation. These theoretical results provide a better understanding of the strong performance that Schedule-Free methods demonstrate.

</details>


### [50] [Shortcut Trajectory Planning for Efficient Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09336)
*Guanquan Wang,Yoshimasa Tsuruoka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STP是一种单阶段训练的快捷轨迹规划框架，通过步长条件化实现一步/少步推理，在D4RL多个任务上取得强性能，同时简化了训练流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的轨迹规划器推理成本高，基于一致性的规划器虽减少了采样步数，但依赖两阶段教师-学生蒸馏，增加了训练成本且可能引入不稳定性。

Method: 提出STP框架：单阶段训练条件性快捷轨迹模型，通过步长条件化支持可调节的一步和少步推理，并使用带有可行性感知校正的评论家选择候选规划。

Result: 在D4RL基准的 locomotion、navigation、manipulation 和 dexterous control 任务上均取得了强性能。

Conclusion: STP在实现强性能的同时简化了快速生成式规划的训练流程。

Abstract: Diffusion-based trajectory planners have shown strong performance in offline reinforcement learning, but their iterative denoising process often incurs high inference cost. Consistency-based planners reduce the number of sampling steps, yet they typically rely on a two-stage teacher--student distillation pipeline that increases training cost and may introduce instability. We propose Shortcut Trajectory Planning (STP), an offline model-based reinforcement learning framework that incorporates shortcut models as efficient trajectory generators. STP trains a conditional shortcut trajectory model in a single stage, supports adjustable one-step and few-step inference through step-size conditioning, and selects candidate plans using a critic augmented with feasibility-aware correction. Across standard D4RL benchmarks, including locomotion, navigation, manipulation, and dexterous control tasks, STP achieves strong performance while simplifying the training pipeline for fast generative planning.

</details>


### [51] [Application of machine learning to monster level prediction in tabletop RPG game design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09196)
*Jolanta Śliwa,Jakub Adamczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了如何利用机器学习模型从怪物的数值属性中预测其等级（Level），将任务建模为表格序数回归问题，基于《开拓者第二版》公开数据构建了首个TTRPG怪物等级预测数据集，比较了多种回归与神经网络方法，发现基于树的集成模型表现最佳，且与人类直觉和游戏规则一致，可作为有效的怪物平衡辅助工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在桌面角色扮演游戏（TTRPG）开发中，设计平衡的怪物是一项核心但劳动密集型的任务，每个怪物由多维数值属性描述，共同决定其战力等级。目前缺乏自动化工具来辅助这一设计过程，因此作者希望探索机器学习能否通过预测怪物等级来支持设计师的工作。

Method: 将怪物等级预测形式化为表格序数回归问题；构建了首个专门用于TTRPG怪物等级预测的数据集（基于《开拓者第二版》公开数据）；比较了经典回归模型（含舍入方案）、专用表格序数回归算法以及带序数感知损失的神经网络；在时序与扩展窗口协议下使用多种互补指标进行评估，并进行了可解释AI分析（特征重要性、误差分布）。

Result: 基于树的集成模型（如梯度提升树）在序数排序和预测准确度上均优于线性模型和神经网络方法，达到近乎完美的序数排序和高预测精度；可解释性分析表明模型与人类直觉一致，遵循游戏规则中的模式。

Conclusion: 机器学习能够可靠地近似设计师的判断，可作为怪物平衡及更广泛TTRPG系统设计的有效计算机辅助工具。

Abstract: Designing balanced adversaries is a central but labor-intensive task in tabletop role-playing game (TTRPG) development. In systems such as Pathfinder, each monster is described by many numerical attributes that jointly determine its power, summarized as an ordinal level. We investigate whether machine learning can support designers by predicting this level from a monster's attributes, framing the task as tabular ordinal regression. We introduce what is, to our knowledge, the first dataset built specifically for TTRPG monster-level prediction, derived from publicly available Pathfinder Second Edition data. Using it, we compare classical regression models with rounding schemes, dedicated tabular ordinal regression algorithms, and neural networks with ordinal-aware losses. To mirror real design workflows, we evaluate all models under chronological and expanding-window protocols with several complementary metrics. Results show that tree-based ensembles outperform linear models and neural approaches, achieving near-perfect ordinal ranking and high predictive accuracy. Explainable AI analyses, such as feature importance and error distributions, show that the model is aligned with human intuition and follows patterns grounded in game rules. Together, these results show that machine learning can reliably approximate designer judgments and serve as an effective computer-aided tool for monster balancing and broader TTRPG system design.

</details>


### [52] [Fully Trainable Deep Differentiable Logic Gate Networks and Lookup Table Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09399)
*Wout Mommen,Lars Keuninckx,Matthias Hartmann,Werner Van Leekwijck,Piet Wambacq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对深度可微逻辑门网络(LGNs)和查找表网络(LUTNs)中连接的部分/完全优化方法，通过每个门/LUT输入引脚上的概率分布选择最优连接，同时并行学习最优门类型或LUT条目。在多个基准上，连接优化的LGN以更少逻辑门超越固定连接版本，并验证了LUT神经元描述的稳定训练能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度可微逻辑门网络和查找表网络通常采用固定连接结构，限制了模型表达能力和效率。本文旨在通过优化网络连接结构，在减少逻辑门数量的同时提升模型性能，并解决深层训练稳定性问题。

Method: 在每个门/LUT输入引脚上维护一个连接的概率分布，训练时选择具有最高权重的连接；同时并行学习最优门类型或LUT条目。采用高学习率、直通估计器(straight-through estimator)和修剪常数输出门类型来保证多达十层网络的训练稳定性。另外提出了一种LUT神经元描述以实现反向传播的稳定训练。

Result: 连接优化的LGN在Yin-Yang、MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上超越固定连接LGN，且逻辑门数量大幅减少。MNIST上：两层8000门达98.92%，单层8000门达98.45%，门数量减少约50倍。LUTN上：两层2000个6输入LUT达98.88%准确率，且参数量减少4倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的连接训练方法能有效优化LGN和LUTN结构，在显著减少门/参数数量的同时保持甚至提升准确率，且训练稳定性可达深层网络，为可微逻辑网络的实用化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We introduce a novel method for both partial and full optimization of the connections in deep differentiable logic gate networks (LGNs) and lookup table networks (LUTNs). Our training method utilizes a probability distribution over a set of connections per gate/lookup table (LUT) input pin, selecting the connection with highest merit, all whilst the optimal gate types or LUT-entries are learned in parallel. We show that the connection-optimized LGNs outperform standard fixed-connection LGNs on the Yin-Yang, MNIST Handwritten Digits and Fashion-MNIST benchmarks, while requiring only a fraction of the number of logic gates. We achieve 98.92% on the MNIST dataset with two layers of 8000 gates. With only one layer of 8000 gates, we obtain 98.45%, showing that our method requires almost 50 times fewer gates compared to fixed-connection LGNs. Training stability up to ten layers has been ensured by employing a high learning rate, straight-through estimators and trimming constant-output gate types. Additionally, we present a LUT neuron description that enables stable training with backpropagation, tested up to 6-layer deep networks. The model requires four times fewer trainable parameters and still achieves a higher accuracy compared to the fixed-connection LGN training algorithm. Our connection-training algorithm also works well for the LUTNs, achieving an accuracy of 98.88% for two layers of 2000 6-input LUTs.

</details>


### [53] [Temporal Knowledge Graph Forecasting under Distribution Shifts: A Synthetic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09232)
*Konrad Özdemir,Julia Gastinger,Lukas Kirchdorfer,Heiner Stuckenschmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用合成时序知识图谱生成器，在受控的分布漂移环境下评估七种TKG预测架构，发现模型鲁棒性高度依赖于信号类型：基于递归和周期性的规律在平稳条件下可恢复，但结构突变（尤其是潜在实体-社区结构漂移）对模型适应性构成最大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的TKG预测模型仅在经验性基准数据集上评估，无法揭示模型对分布漂移的鲁棒性。为弥补这一不足，有必要在受控的漂移环境下系统研究TKG模型的鲁棒性。

Method: 使用合成TKG生成器，编码递归性、同质性和周期性三种数据生成机制；在平稳和漂移两种条件下评估七种预测架构的性能。

Result: 基于递归和周期性的规律在平稳条件下可较好恢复；简单基于记忆的基线在递归主导数据时具有竞争力；结构突变暴露模型适应性局限，潜在实体-社区结构漂移是最具挑战性的漂移类型。

Conclusion: TKG模型在面对时序分布漂移时的鲁棒性高度依赖于数据中的信号类型，当前模型在应对结构突变方面存在明显局限性。

Abstract: Temporal knowledge graphs (TKGs) represent evolving relational systems, whose underlying data-generating processes often change over time. Yet, TKG forecasting models are commonly evaluated only on empirical benchmark datasets that provide limited insight into the models' robustness to such distribution shifts. Recognising this issue, we study TKG forecasting under controlled shift environments using a synthetic TKG generator that encodes three temporal and structural properties -- recurrence, homophily, and periodicity -- as data-generating mechanisms. This allows us to evaluate seven forecasting architectures under stationary and shifting regimes. Our experiments suggest that robustness in TKG forecasting is highly signal-dependent. Recurrence-based and periodic regularities are largely recoverable under stationary conditions, and simple memory-based baselines can be competitive when recurrence dominates the data. However, structural breaks reveal limitations in model adaptivity, with shifts in latent entity-community structure posing the strongest challenge in our study. Overall, our findings improve the understanding of the capabilities and limitations of current TKG models confronted with temporal distribution shifts.

</details>


### [54] [On-Device Adaptive Battery Power Prediction for Electric Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09400)
*Avik Bhatnagar,Anton Paule,Tobias Schuermann,Sebastian Reiter,Oliver Bringmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向资源受限电动汽车系统的设备端学习方法，通过将预训练电池预测模型转化为可自适应版本，实现对新数据分布的持续适应，在线和离线自适应策略分别降低平均绝对误差达7.49%和14.88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动汽车的功率管理需要精准预测，但深度学习模型在面对训练数据之外的数据分布时性能会下降，而电动汽车系统资源受限，需要一种能在设备端持续适应新数据的方法。

Method: 利用现有预训练模型，将其转化为保留初始训练关键超参数知识的可自适应版本；综合研究了在线适应和离线适应两种模型自适应策略，在资源受限的EV系统中实现设备端持续学习。

Result: 在线自适应技术使平均绝对误差降低7.49%，离线自适应技术降低14.88%，多种模型和时间范围上的预测性能均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 设备端自适应相比未适应的模型部署在实际EV场景中能显著增强电池功率预测性能，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Adaptive power management in Electric Vehicles (EVs) requires accurate power prediction. Although deep learning models have emerged as highly effective for time-series forecasting in this domain, their performance is prone to degradation when exposed to data with distributions different from the training data. We introduce a novel approach that enables on-device learning in resource-constrained EV systems to continuously adapt pretrained battery prediction models to new, unseen data. We leverage existing pretrained models by transforming them into adaptable versions that retain critical hyperparameter knowledge from their initial training. We comprehensively investigate both online and offline model adaptation strategies. Our results demonstrate significant improvements in forecasting performance across various models and time horizons, achieving mean absolute error reductions of up to 7.49\% and 14.88\% with online and offline adaptation techniques, respectively. This study highlights the substantial benefit of on-device adaptation, resulting in enhanced battery power predictions than unadapted model deployments in real-world EV scenarios.

</details>


### [55] [Forget Narrowly, Retain Broadly: Unlearning as an Asymmetric Generalization Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09236)
*Amit Peleg,Naman Deep Singh,Naama Pearl,Bibhabasu Mohapatra,Matthias Hein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SUITE是一个评估LLM机器遗忘的新协议和训练语料库，它通过细粒度标注遗忘-保留边界来解决现有基准测试中遗忘不足（遗漏泛化查询）和过度遗忘（破坏无关知识）的问题，并提出了最佳遗忘-保留权衡算法JensUn++。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM机器遗忘评估基准不可靠：一方面存在遗忘不足问题（under-forgetting），即目标知识在改写或间接查询下重新出现；另一方面存在过度遗忘问题（over-forgetting），即缺乏对语义、句法和词汇层面的探测来验证无关知识是否得以保留。这两种失败反映了一个不对称泛化问题。

Method: 提出SUITE评估协议和训练语料库，通过细粒度标注捕捉真实世界事实领域的遗忘-保留结构；基于所获洞察提出JensUn++遗忘算法。

Result: 在SUITE上训练的方法显著提升，表明训练数据与算法设计同等重要；JensUn++在三种LLM上、在顺序遗忘和联合遗忘设置下均取得了最佳遗忘-保留效用权衡。

Conclusion: 机器遗忘需要更严格的评估体系解决不对称泛化问题，SUITE提供了这一框架，而JensUn++验证了该框架对算法设计的指导价值。

Abstract: Machine unlearning in LLMs is the targeted removal of specific knowledge while preserving all other capabilities, critical for privacy and safety. Yet existing benchmarks measure it unreliably. They miss knowledge that resurfaces under paraphrased or indirect queries, a failure we call under-forgetting, and lack the semantic, syntactic, and lexical probes needed to verify that unrelated knowledge is preserved, a failure we call over-forgetting. Both failures reflect an asymmetric generalization problem. Forget evaluation must cover diverse query formulations of the same target facts, testing whether forgetting holds beyond exact training prompts. Retain evaluation must probe a far larger and implicitly defined set, namely every fact disjoint from the forget target. The retain set thus defines the effective forget set, yet current datasets provide no fine-grained annotation of this forget-retain boundary. We address this with SUITE, an evaluation protocol and training corpus that captures forget-retain structure for real-world factual domains. Methods trained on SUITE improve substantially, showing that training data is as important as algorithmic design. Building on the obtained insights, we introduce JensUn++, an unlearning algorithm that achieves the best forget-retain utility trade-off across three LLMs, in both sequential and joint unlearning settings. Code and datasets are available at https://amitpeleg.github.io/forget-narrowly-retain-broadly

</details>


### [56] [LionVote: Per-Layer Learning Rate Adaptation for Lion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09266)
*Kris Atallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LionVote，一种逐层学习率调节机制，通过梯度方向稳定性和动量健康两个诊断指标，为每个参数张量维护复合层级并定期更新学习率。在ViT-Tiny/CIFAR-100上达到69.7% top-1准确率，优于Lion的69.0%和AdamW的68.8%。诊断揭示Lion在注意力/MLP层学习率偏高2.6-2.8倍，归一化层偏高约2倍。逐层自适应在CNN架构上效果有限，在ViT上效果依赖具体任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化器如Lion使用全局学习率，但不同层类型（注意力、MLP、归一化）对学习率的敏感度存在显著差异，导致单一全局学习率无法最优适配所有层。作者通过诊断发现Lion在不同层上有效学习率偏高的幅度不一致（注意力/MLP层2.6-2.8倍 vs 归一化层2倍），这种跨层类型的差异无法通过单个全局学习率解决。

Method: 提出LionVote：为每个参数张量维护一个复合层级（persistent integer），每c个epoch通过两个诊断指标（梯度方向稳定性和动量健康）进行投票更新，当投票出现平局时由验证损失裁决。投票阈值基于几何恒等式、EMA时间常数和噪声底限估计推导得出；更新节奏通过结构约束和消融实验确定。

Result: 在ViT-Tiny/CIFAR-100上，LionVote达到69.7% top-1准确率，显著优于Lion的69.0%（p<0.02, Welch's t-test）和AdamW的68.8%。诊断显示Lion的注意力/MLP层有效学习率偏高2.6-2.8倍，归一化层偏高约2倍，且32%的跨层类型差异无法通过单一全局学习率复现。

Conclusion: 逐层学习率自适应（LionVote）能够有效提升模型性能，但其增益取决于两个因素：架构异质性和任务特性。在均匀CNN架构上，配合余弦退火的SGD仍占主导地位；在ViT架构上，增益呈现任务依赖性。

Abstract: Per-layer diagnostics reveal that, at the prescribed learning rate, Lion's effective scale is 2.6-2.8x too high for attention and MLP parameters and ~2x too high for normalization layers on ViT-Tiny/CIFAR-100; this 32% cross-layer-type disparity cannot be reproduced by a single global rate. The measurement comes from LionVote, a per-layer learning rate mechanism in which each parameter tensor maintains a compound level, a persistent integer updated every c epochs by two diagnostics (gradient direction stability and momentum health) resolved by a validation loss tiebreaker. Voting thresholds derive from geometric identities, the EMA time constant, and a noise-floor estimate; cadence is bounded structurally and selected by ablation. On ViT-Tiny/CIFAR-100, LionVote achieves 69.7% top-1 accuracy vs. Lion's 69.0% (p < 0.02, Welch's t-test) and AdamW's 68.8%. Per-layer adaptation value depends on both architectural heterogeneity and task; on uniform CNN architectures tuned SGD with cosine annealing remains dominant, and on ViT architectures gains are task-dependent.

</details>


### [57] [Autoregressive latent diffusion for 3D molecule generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09277)
*Federico Ottomano,Gaopeng Ren,Yingzhen Li,Kim E. Jelfs,Alex M. Ganose*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KRONOS是一种潜在自回归扩散框架，在预训练自编码器的潜在空间中生成分子，联合建模分子图拓扑和几何结构，并采用填充中间（FIM）混合训练策略，在单一自回归模型中同时支持无条件生成和片段条件生成。在QM9和GEOM-Drugs数据集上，该方法在自回归方法中达到领先的无条件生成性能，且与扩散模型竞争力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D分子生成方法存在两难：扩散模型生成质量高但需预先指定分子尺寸；自回归方法支持变长生成和条件生成但难以平衡无条件生成与上下文条件生成。KRONOS旨在统一两者的优势。

Method: 提出KRONOS框架，在预训练自编码器的潜在空间中执行自回归扩散生成，联合建模分子图拓扑和几何结构；引入基于Fill-in-the-Middle（FIM）范式的混合训练策略，使单一从左到右的自回归模型同时支持无条件生成和片段条件生成。

Result: 在QM9和GEOM-Drugs数据集上，KRONOS在自回归方法中达到领先的无条件生成性能，与扩散模型竞争力相当；片段条件生成对无条件生成性能影响可忽略不计。

Conclusion: KRONOS证明了在同一架构中可以同时支持无条件生成和片段条件生成两种范式，且性能优异。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) molecule generation has been dominated by diffusion models, which achieve strong generation quality but typically require the molecular size to be specified a priori. Recent autoregressive approaches have substantially narrowed the performance gap while naturally supporting variable-length generation and conditioning on partial molecular context. However, balancing unconditional and context-conditioned generation remains challenging. We introduce KRONOS, a latent autoregressive diffusion framework that generates molecules in the latent space of a pre-trained autoencoder, jointly modeling molecular graph topology and geometry, while retaining the flexibility of autoregressive generation. We further introduce a mixed training strategy inspired by Fill-in-the Middle (FIM) paradigm, enabling both unconditional and fragment-conditioned molecular generation within a single left-to-right autoregressive model. Experiments on QM9 and GEOM-Drugs demonstrate that KRONOS achieves leading unconditional generation performance among autoregressive methods, while remaining competitive with diffusion models. Moreover, fragment-conditioned generation is achieved with negligible impact on unconditional generation performance, demonstrating that both generation paradigms can be supported within a single architecture.

</details>


### [58] [Semantic Pareto-DQN: A Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning Framework for Financial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09641)
*Cláudio Lúcio do Val Lopes,Lucca Machado da Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出语义Pareto-DQN，一种多目标强化学习框架，通过大语言模型将异构交易特征编码为自然语言叙事作为状态表示，并利用向量化奖励优化帕累托前沿，在不进行数据重采样的前提下解决金融异常检测中的极端类别不平衡和"欺诈崩溃"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单目标算法在金融异常检测中因极端类别不平衡而出现"欺诈崩溃"现象（默认预测多数类），且无法在异常拦截与客户摩擦之间取得平衡，而现有数据重采样方法具有扭曲性。

Method: 1) 利用大语言模型将异构交易特征合成为自然语言叙事，生成鲁棒、尺度不变的状态表示；2) 设计向量化奖励函数，显式解耦金融效能、操作摩擦和语义发现三个目标；3) 通过连续帕累托前沿映射，动态导航漏报与误报的非对称成本。

Result: 在电商欺诈和UCI Credit数据集上，语义Pareto-DQN打破了零召回陷阱，相比标量化基线方法实现了更高的少数类召回率，并以可控的操作摩擦代价换取了金融异常发现能力的提升。

Conclusion: 多目标强化学习结合语义状态表示能有效解决类别极端不平衡下的异常检测问题，为平衡拦截效果与用户体验提供了无需数据重采样的新范式。

Abstract: Financial anomaly detection suffers from extreme class imbalance, causing traditional single-objective algorithms to exhibit ``fraud collapse'', defaulting to the majority class and failing to balance anomaly interdiction with customer friction. To overcome this without distortive data resampling, we propose the Semantic Pareto-DQN, a multi-objective reinforcement learning framework. Our approach synthesizes heterogeneous transaction features into cohesive natural-language narratives, encoded by large language models, thereby producing a robust, scale-invariant state representation. The agent optimizes a vectorial reward that explicitly decouples financial efficacy, operational friction, and semantic discovery. By mapping the continuous Pareto frontier, the system dynamically navigates the asymmetric costs of missed anomalies versus false positives. Empirical evaluations across E-Commerce fraud and UCI Credit datasets show that semantic Pareto-DQN successfully shatters the zero-recall trap. It achieves superior minority-class recall compared to scalarized baselines, providing an alternative to trade bounded operational friction for financial anomaly discovery.

</details>


### [59] [Super-Tuning: From Activation-Aware Pruning to Sparse Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09287)
*Ivan Ilin,Philip Zmushko,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Super（基于Wanda剪枝分数的稀疏PEFT）和Supra（Super+LoRA混合适配器），通过固定稀疏支持集实现高效微调，在Llama-3.2-1B和Meta-Llama-3-8B的Math17K算术任务上取得高精度，证明剪枝启发式排序可为PEFT提供有效固定稀疏支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM全参数微调成本高昂（内存、计算、存储），探索能否复用剪枝中的显著性信号来选择模型应适应的参数子集，实现稀疏参数高效微调。

Method: 提出Super：使用Wanda风格的激活加权幅度分数（基于校准pass计算）固定一个小的可训练支持集进行稀疏PEFT。提出Supra：混合适配器，将稀疏更新与LoRA结合，通过简单的预算分割规则保持匹配的可训练参数预算。对比PaFi纯幅度基线。

Result: 在Llama-3.2-1B和Meta-Llama-3-8B的Math17K算术实验中，Super/Supra变体在测试的调度选择适配器配置中达到最高平均准确率；低分数支持集在幅度和Wanda排序下均有效。

Conclusion: 简单的剪枝启发式排序可以为PEFT提供有用的固定稀疏支持集，尤其是与低秩适配器（LoRA）结合时效果更佳。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain expensive to fine-tune because full-parameter updates require substantial memory, compute, and per-task storage. We study whether saliency signals originally developed for pruning can be reused to choose where a model should adapt. We propose Super, a sparse parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) method that fixes a small trainable support using a Wanda-style activation-weighted magnitude score [Sun et al., 2023] computed from a calibration pass. We then introduce Supra, a hybrid adapter that combines this sparse update with LoRA while preserving a matched trainable-parameter budget through a simple budget-splitting rule. In single-seed Math17K arithmetic experiments on Llama-3.2-1B and Meta-Llama-3-8B, the best Super/Supra variants achieve the highest average accuracy among the tested schedule-selected adapter configurations. We also include a PaFi-style magnitude-only support as a closest training-free sparse baseline and find that low-score supports under both magnitude and Wanda-style orderings can be effective. These results suggest that simple pruning-inspired orderings can provide useful fixed sparse supports for PEFT, especially when combined with low-rank adapters.

</details>


### [60] [Graph Neural Networks for Scalable and Transferable Node Centrality Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09372)
*Samra Sana,Giorgio Mantica,Saul Imbrici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究使用图神经网络（GNN）近似计算介数中心性和紧密度中心性，提出混合分布训练策略提升跨图拓扑的迁移能力，GNN推理相比精确计算最高实现97.7倍加速，但紧密度中心性对社区结构图敏感仍是开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图论中的中心性指标（如介数中心性和紧密度中心性）在大型图上精确计算代价高昂，需要一种可扩展的近似方法。GNN提供了一种基于学习的框架，但关键在于能否学习可迁移的结构表征而非仅拟合训练分布。

Method: 将中心性近似建模为节点排序问题，使用精确中心性值作为监督信号，采用消息传递GNN进行学习。训练策略包括单一分布训练（Erdos-Renyi图）和混合分布训练（Erdos-Renyi、Barabasi-Albert、Gaussian Random Partition图），评估指标采用Kendall's tau秩相关系数。

Result: 在未见过的Erdos-Renyi图上，介数中心性tau=0.851，紧密度中心性tau=0.894；大规模模型（N=5000）介数中心性tau=0.938；混合分布训练提升了介数中心性跨图族迁移能力；GNN推理加速比达97.7倍；紧密度中心性在社区结构图上迁移效果较差。

Conclusion: 混合分布训练可有效提升GNN中心性近似的结构迁移能力，方法具备良好的可扩展性。但紧密度中心性对不同拓扑结构（尤其是社区结构）敏感，迁移至真实世界图时效果受限，这是未来需要解决的关键问题。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) provide a learning-based framework for approximating graph quantities that are expensive to compute exactly. This paper investigates GNNs for scalable approximation of betweenness and closeness centrality, formulated as a node-ranking problem. Exact centrality values are used as supervision, and ranking quality is evaluated using Kendall's tau rank correlation. We study whether message-passing GNNs can learn transferable structural representations across different graph topologies rather than only fitting the distribution used during training. On unseen Erdos renyi graphs, the proposed models achieve tau = 0.851 for betweenness and tau = 0.894 for closeness. A large-scale betweenness model trained on graphs with N = 5,000 nodes achieves tau = 0.938, demonstrating scalability. Mixed-distribution training on Erdos renyi, Barabasi-Albert, and Gaussian Random Partition graphs improves betweenness transfer across graph families. In contrast, closeness centrality remains more sensitive to community-structured graphs and shows reduced transfer to real-world topologies. Finally, GNN inference achieves up to a 97.7x speedup over exact computation. These results show that mixed-distribution training can improve structural transfer in GNN-based centrality approximation, while identifying closeness centrality's sensitivity to topology as an open challenge.

</details>


### [61] [Mach-Mind-4-Flash Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09375)
*Foundation Model Team*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Mach-Mind-4-Flash，一个35B参数（3B激活）的MoE智能体模型，仅通过后训练优化即达到与100B参数模型相当或更优的性能。采用三阶段流水线：统一RL/OPD训练框架、多专家并行训练与MOPD融合、HMPO令牌效率优化，在多项基准上领先或匹配10-30倍激活参数规模的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不扩展预训练计算的前提下，通过后训练优化使小规模MoE模型达到百亿参数级模型的性能水平，同时大幅降低推理成本。

Method: 三阶段后训练流水线：(1) 统一RL/OPD训练基础设施，含动态多教师调度和算子级加速，实现17%端到端加速；(2) 在推理、通用和智能体三条轨道上并行训练领域专用RL专家，通过多教师同策略蒸馏（MOPD）融合为通用模型，使用路由反向KL散度消除混合奖励的跷跷板退化；(3) 混合中长策略优化（HMPO），单阶段令牌效率方法，压缩推理链19-46%且准确率损失≤0.7个百分点。

Result: AIME'26: 92.70, IFBench: 82.82, Behavioral-SafetyBench: 80.74, BFCL-v4: 75.80, BrowseComp-zh: 72.31, ClawBench: 84.20，领先或匹配10-30倍激活参数规模的模型。

Conclusion: 通过创新的后训练优化流水线，小模型可以在不增加预训练计算的情况下达到大模型性能，为高效部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We present Mach-Mind-4-Flash, a 35B-parameter Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) agentic model with 3B activated parameters. Through post-training optimization alone without scaling pre-training compute, the model achieves performance on par with or surpassing that of 100B-parameter-class models. By introducing scalable agentic interaction environments for large-scale reinforcement learning, the model attains significant performance gains on real-world application tasks. Our pipeline comprises three stages: (1) a unified RL/OPD training infrastructure with dynamic multi-teacher scheduling and operator-level acceleration, delivering 17\% end-to-end training speedup; (2) multiple domain-specific RL experts trained in parallel across Reasoning, General, and Agent tracks, then fused into a single generalist via Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (MOPD) -- a routed reverse-KL objective that eliminates the see-saw degradation of mixed-reward RL; (3) Hybrid Median-length Policy Optimization (HMPO), a single-stage token-efficiency method that compresses reasoning chains by 19--46\% with $\le$0.7 percentage-point accuracy loss. Mach-Mind-4-Flash scores 92.70 on AIME'26, 82.82 on IFBench, 80.74 on Behavioral-SafetyBench, 75.80 on BFCL-v4, 72.31 on BrowseComp-zh, and 84.20 on ClawBench -- leading or matching models with 10--30$\times$ its activated size at a fraction of the inference cost.

</details>


### [62] [Learning Physics-Informed Surrogate Model of Linear Elastic Displacement Fields from Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09382)
*Rodolphe Barlogis,Ferhat Tamssaouet,Quentin Falcoz,Stéphane Grieu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息的DeepONet框架，用于含裂缝弹性域的结构健康监测实时代理建模，该框架通过专用编码策略处理裂缝几何，利用局部罚函数弱施加裂缝边界无牵引条件，无需有限元生成训练数据，并通过数值示例验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种快速且物理一致的代理模型，用于含裂缝弹性域的实时结构健康监测，以克服传统有限元方法计算成本高、难以满足实时监测需求的局限性。

Method: 采用物理信息DeepONet框架，通过专用编码策略表示裂缝几何，将裂缝边界上的无牵引条件以局部罚函数形式弱施加，模型输入同时包含边界条件和裂缝几何信息，无需依赖有限元生成的训练数据。

Result: 针对单一代表性裂缝几何的数值示例表明，该公式是可行的，能够预测位移场，为扩展到多种裂缝几何的代理建模奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 该方法在实时结构健康监测中具有潜力，未来工作可扩展至更广泛的裂缝几何形状的代理建模。

Abstract: This work aims to develop a fast and physically consistent surrogate model for real-time structural health monitoring of fractured elastic domains. We propose a physics-informed DeepONet framework that predicts displacement fields from both boundary conditions and fracture geometry, using a dedicated encoding strategy for the latter and without relying on finite-element-generated training data. The traction-free condition on the fracture boundary is imposed weakly through a localized penalty term. The presented numerical example focuses on one representative fracture geometry, demonstrating the feasibility of the formulation and laying the groundwork for extensions to surrogate modeling across diverse fracture geometries.

</details>


### [63] [Data-Efficient Deep Learning: Empirical Guidelines for Training Set Size Estimation in Inertial Sensor Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09402)
*Ofir Kruzel,Itzik Klien*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过系统实验发现惯性传感器分类任务中学习曲线呈对数增长模式，提出一个通用框架来估计达到指定精度所需的最小样本量，并引入稳定性点指标，证明模型在远少于传统经验所需样本量时即可达到稳定性能，从而将惯性传感领域从追求数据规模转向数据效率优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在惯性传感器分类任务（如人类活动识别、智能手机位置识别）中依赖大规模数据集，但数据采集耗时费力且难以规模化扩展，目前缺乏确定所需最小样本量的数据驱动准则。

Method: 提出统一的经验公式框架，分析二分类和多分类场景下学习曲线的收敛速率；在6个不同真实世界数据集（总计102.7小时惯性测量数据）上进行系统评估，提出稳定性点指标，定义为学习曲线在预定平均绝对百分比偏差内趋于渐近最大值所需的样本量。

Result: 精度呈一致的对数增长模式，与任务复杂度无关；模型在远少于传统启发式方法建议的样本量时即可达到实际稳定性。

Conclusion: 提供了一个通用框架，可从少量预实验样本外推总数据需求，优化记录投入与模型可靠性之间的权衡，推动惯性传感领域从最大化数据量转向优化数据效率。

Abstract: Deep learning models dependency on large-scale inertial datasets presents a significant bottleneck in inertial sensor-based classification tasks, such as human activity recognition and smartphone location recognition. In these domains, data collection requires massive recording campaigns that are complex, time-consuming, and difficult to scale. Currently, data-driven guidelines for determining the minimum sample size required to reach a desired accuracy level do not exist. To address this gap, this study presents a systematic empirical evaluation of learning curve convergence rates in inertial classification. We introduce a unified framework that analyzes classification performance under both binary and multi-class scenarios, and derive an empirical formula to estimate performance relative to dataset size. Testing across six diverse, real-world datasets totaling 102.7 hours of inertial measurements demonstrates that accuracy follows a consistent logarithmic growth pattern, regardless of task complexity. Leveraging this finding, we propose a quantitative stability point metric, defined as the sample size required for the learning curve to stabilize within a predefined mean absolute percentage deviation of its asymptotic maximum. Our analysis reveals that models often reach practical stability with substantially fewer samples than traditional heuristics suggest. Ultimately, we offer a generalizable framework to extrapolate total data requirements from small-scale pilot studies, optimizing the tradeoff between recording effort and model reliability. These findings shift the prevailing paradigm from maximizing data volume toward optimizing data efficiency, offering concrete, data-backed guidelines for planning recording campaigns in inertial sensing applications.

</details>


### [64] [SYNRARE: Synthetic Rare Disease EHR Generation for ML Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09404)
*Nicolai Dinh Khang Truong,Richard Röttger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SYNRARE是一个基于Synthea框架的图形用户界面，用于生成模拟罕见病患者的合成电子健康记录，支持定义患者与常见疾病患者的差异程度，从而在可控技术条件下对机器学习算法进行基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见病诊断常因症状与常见疾病相似而延迟，机器学习算法有望加速诊断，但法律和隐私问题构成障碍。合成数据生成是获取电子健康记录的替代方法，但现有方法缺乏对生成可定义差异程度的罕见病亚组患者的支持。

Method: 基于Synthea框架开发了SYNRARE图形用户界面，使研究人员能够方便地修改和生成罕见病患者的合成电子健康记录，这些记录仅在与常见疾病患者的可定义差异程度上有所不同。

Result: SYNRARE使研究人员能够在可控技术条件下快速对各种场景下的机器学习算法进行基准测试。

Conclusion: SYNRARE通过生成差异程度可定义的合成罕见病患者数据，解决了现有合成数据生成方法对罕见病模拟支持不足的问题，为机器学习算法的基准测试和开发提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Motivation: Rare disease (RD) diagnosis is frequently delayed due to the similarities in symptoms to common disease variants. Machine Learning Algorithms applied to Electronic Health Records show promise for accelerating the diagnosis; however, legal and privacy concerns pose significant barriers. To address these issues, Synthetic Data Generation is an alternative method for obtaining Electronic Health Records and can be applied with any Machine Learning algorithm for benchmarking and development purposes. Despite the availability of Synthetic Data Generation algorithms, support for generating a subset of patients that differ in a definable degree from the majority to simulate patients with RD is often lacking.
  Results: We present SYNRARE, a graphical user interface based on the Synthea framework that enables easier modification and generation of synthetic Electronic Health Records of RD patients, which differ only to a definable degree from patients with common diseases, thereby enabling the benchmarking and testing of algorithms under controlled technical conditions. SYNRARE enables researchers to rapidly benchmark their Machine Learning algorithms across any scenario.
  Availability and implementation: SYNRARE, including detailed instructions for installing, is available at https://gitlab.sdu.dk/screen4care/synrare.

</details>


### [65] [Similarity search generalisation in contrastive learning with InfoNCE loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09405)
*Nick Whiteley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明InfoNCE损失函数的总体风险与理想化softmax相似度搜索之间的差距为O(1/k)，并通过Gâteaux微分推导出新的连续性边界，表明增加负样本数量k可稳定泛化误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比学习理论多关注k→∞极限下的互信息解释和embedding对齐均匀性，但缺乏对有限负样本情况下InfoNCE损失函数与下游相似度搜索任务之间关系的定量分析，以及泛化性能的严格刻画。

Method: 1) 证明InfoNCE的总体风险与期望交叉熵之间的差距为O(1/k)；2) 通过Gâteaux微分推导InfoNCE损失的连续性边界，该边界保留了负样本平均结构并引入可调逆温度参数；3) 对Lipschitz嵌入函数，展示k个负样本的平均效应随k增长而稳定泛化误差。

Result: InfoNCE损失在k个负样本下的总体风险与理想softmax搜索的偏差以O(1/k)速度收敛；提出的连续性边界表明负样本平均机制能有效抑制泛化误差的波动。

Conclusion: 该工作从有限负样本角度完善了InfoNCE的理论理解，将损失函数与下游搜索任务直接关联，并提供了可操作的泛化误差控制手段。

Abstract: Similarity search is a primary application of embedding models trained by contrastive learning. For one of the most popular contrastive learning loss functions, InfoNCE, we show that the population risk with $k$ negative samples is $O(1/k)$ close to an expected cross-entropy which quantifies deviation between i) a softmax similarity search over unseen data using the learned embedding function, and ii) an idealised softmax search over the same data but using similarity implicitly represented in the positive sample generator. This complements existing interpretations of InfoNCE in the $k\to\infty$ limit which are phrased in terms of mutual information, and alignment versus uniformity in embeddings. To quantify generalisation performance, we introduce a new continuity bound for the InfoNCE loss, obtained via Gâteaux differentiation. The bound preserves the structure of averaging over negative samples present in the loss function and features an ``inverse temperature'' parameter which can be tuned to account for the algorithmic temperature. For embedding functions which are Lipschitz in a parameter, this yields a simple demonstration that the averaging effect of $k$ negative samples in the InfoNCE loss carries over to stabilisation of the generalisation error as $k$ grows.

</details>


### [66] [Action-Factored Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Scalable Quantum Device Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09422)
*Edwin De Nicolo,Rahul Marchand,Cornelius Carlsson,Pranav Vaidhyanathan,Natalia Ares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QADAPT框架，通过在线学习的动作空间分解表示解耦多智能体，实现量子点阵列调谐的零样本泛化与恒定收敛步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静电定义量子点阵列的调谐中存在参数串扰导致的非平稳环境问题，既影响多智能体强化学习也困扰人工调谐，亟需可扩展的快速校准方案。

Method: 采用因子化动作空间表示（在线学习）解耦智能体；基于局部观测和奖励学习共享策略的QADAPT框架。

Result: 实现对未见量子器件尺寸的零样本泛化；达到目标区域所需的收敛步数近似恒定。

Conclusion: 为大规模量子处理器的快速校准提供了可扩展的路线。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning is well suited to problems with large parameter spaces and exploitable local structure, such as the tuning of electrostatically-defined quantum-dot arrays. However, if parameter cross-talk is strong, a non-stationary environment from the perspective of any individual agent can destabilize learning - the same effect that plagues manual tuning of such systems. We propose using a factored representation of the action space, learned online, to decouple agents and minimize their interference. Our framework, QADAPT, uses this factorization to efficiently learn shared policies based on local measurements and rewards. With this modular strategy, we achieve zero-shot generalization to unseen quantum device sizes and maintain an approximately constant number of convergence steps to reach target regimes. This work provides a scalable route toward the rapid calibration of large-scale quantum processors.

</details>


### [67] [Active rejection enables reliable generalization of universal machine-learning interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09456)
*Mingxiang Luo,Xinnan Mao,Lu Wang,Lei Bai,Feng Ding,Yuqiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应多教师路由(ATR)方法，利用少量真实r²SCAN标签(仅0.2%)校准多个预训练通用机器学习原子间势(uMLIP)，通过结构描述符、教师身份和教师间分歧评估结构-教师对的可靠性，选择高置信度预测生成289万个可追踪的伪标签用于预训练，在保留测试和有限温度分子动力学中均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器学习原子间势(uMLIPs)在高精度计算(如r²SCAN)上的成本限制了训练数据集规模，且平均基准性能无法保证每个结构的能量-力预测可靠性。需要一种可扩展且可靠的高保真数据构建方法。

Method: 将高保真数据构建重构为不确定性下的结构级决策问题：用少量真实r²SCAN标签校准多个预训练uMLIP教师，结合结构描述符、教师身份和教师间不一致性估计每个结构-教师对的可靠性；选择高置信度预测生成伪标签，拒绝无足够可靠教师的结构。

Result: 仅用0.2%候选结构的真实r²SCAN标签，生成289万可追踪的r²SCAN级伪标签。在保留测试和MP-r²SCAN基准上，用ATR生成数据集训练的轻量CHGNet持续优于基线。有限温度分子动力学显示ATR提升多个材料系统的动力学鲁棒性，避免灾难性结构坍塌。

Conclusion: 主动拒绝机制是将多个预训练uMLIP转化为可扩展、可靠的高保真数据构建系统的有效方法，为构建高性能uMLIP提供了新范式。

Abstract: Universal machine learning interatomic potentials (uMLIPs) bridge quantum-mechanical accuracy and large-scale molecular dynamics, but the cost of high-accuracy calculations such as r$^2$SCAN limits training to datasets that remain small relative to the open materials space. Strong average benchmark performance also does not guarantee reliable energy--force predictions for every structure. We propose Adaptive Multi-Teacher Routing (ATR), which reformulates high-fidelity data construction as a structure-wise decision problem under uncertainty. Using a small set of real r$^2$SCAN labels, ATR calibrates multiple pretrained uMLIP teachers and combines structural descriptors, teacher identity, and inter-teacher disagreement to estimate the reliability of each structure--teacher pair. It selects high-confidence predictions for pseudo-label generation and rejects structures for which no teacher is sufficiently reliable. With real r$^2$SCAN labels for only 0.2\% of candidate structures, ATR distils 2.89 million traceable r$^2$SCAN-level pseudo-labels for pretraining. On held-out r$^2$SCAN structures and the MP-r$^2$SCAN benchmark, a lightweight CHGNet trained on the ATR-generated dataset consistently outperforms the baseline and non-routed controls. Finite-temperature molecular dynamics further shows that ATR improves dynamical robustness across multiple material systems, maintaining stable trajectories where baseline simulations undergo catastrophic structural collapse. These results establish active rejection as an effective mechanism for converting multiple pretrained uMLIPs into a scalable and reliable data-construction system for high-fidelity uMLIPs.

</details>


### [68] [Neural Collapse Is Forbidden: Information Floors in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09487)
*Bruno Abrahao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证语言模型表示中的类内方差并非不完全神经坍缩，而是分配的信息存储，且服从特定规律：类别结构仅承载4-12%的表示方差，而上下文内标记承载79-91%；类别分散度与条件互信息I(token; context | category)成正比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠正将语言模型表示中的类内方差简单视为不完全神经坍缩的常见观点，证明其实际上是有结构的信息分配存储。

Method: 通过一行居中恒等式推翻等角紧框架假设；在14个模型上分析无量纲方差份额；采用权重衰减理论分析将next-token预测简化为不平衡K类问题；对二元类别证明下界，建立类内分散度与条件互信息的关系。

Result: 宏观类别结构仅占4-12%表示方差，上下文内标记占79-91%，在100倍参数范围内稳定；类别分散度与条件互信息成正比，该关系跨模型成立，甚至一个模型的信息可预测另一模型的分散度。

Conclusion: 语言模型表示中起主导作用的是上下文标记信息而非类别结构，类内方差是信息的结构化存储。

Abstract: Within-class variance in language-model representations is commonly read as incomplete neural collapse. We argue it is allocated information storage, and that the allocation obeys a law. A one-line centering identity voids a family of simplex equiangular-tight-frame claims, including our own earlier ones; in dimensionless variance shares across 14 models, macro-category structure carries only 4-12% of representational variance and within-token context carries 79-91%, stable across a 100x parameter range. On the theory side, token-level weight decay penalizes a category in proportion to its type count, not its occurrence mass, reducing next-token prediction to an imbalanced K-class problem whose optimum orders category norms by type count. A converse floor, proved for binary categories, forces within-category dispersion to be at least proportional to the conditional mutual information I(token; context | category). The law holds: identity dispersion, not total variance, tracks this information across every tested model and partition, under a model-free estimate and even across models, where one model's information predicts another's dispersion; and over pretraining the category share overshoots, decays, and partially recovers, because the information it must carry never left.

</details>


### [69] [TSAI-MetaFraud: A Benchmark Dataset for Financial Fraud Transaction and Behavioral Risk Detection in Metaverse Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09528)
*Refat Ishrak Hemel,Ehsan Hallaji,Roozbeh Razavi-Far*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TSAI-MetaFraud是一个面向元宇宙虚拟经济的多模态、多任务欺诈检测基准数据集，整合了行为、交易和图结构信息，支持交易欺诈检测、跨模态节点分类、时序链接预测和弱监督欺诈检测等任务，并提供了机器学习和图神经网络的基线评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集通常孤立地关注用户行为、身份验证或金融交易，缺乏多模态集成数据，限制了可复现的多模态欺诈检测方法评估。为填补这一空白，作者构建了TSAI-MetaFraud基准数据集。

Method: 整合行为数据、交易数据和图结构信息，构建多模态多任务基准数据集；定义交易欺诈检测、跨模态节点分类、时序链接预测和弱监督欺诈检测四项基准任务；使用机器学习模型和图神经网络进行基线评估。

Result: 成功构建了统一虚拟经济场景下的多模态基准数据集，并提供了多类欺诈检测任务的基线评估结果。

Conclusion: TSAI-MetaFraud为元宇宙虚拟经济中的多模态学习、图挖掘、欺诈分析和可信AI研究提供了标准化的基准平台。

Abstract: The emergence of metaverse platforms has created virtual economies that introduce new challenges related to fraud, bot activity, and illicit financial behavior. Despite growing interest in trustworthy metaverse analytics, existing datasets typically focus on user behavior, authentication, or financial transactions in isolation, limiting the development and reproducible evaluation of multimodal fraud detection methods. To address this gap, we present TSAI-MetaFraud, a multimodal, multi-task benchmark dataset for fraud analytics in virtual economies. TSAI-MetaFraud integrates behavioral, transactional, and graph-structured information while incorporating realistic fraud and automated bot scenarios. We define benchmark tasks including transaction fraud detection, cross-modal node classification, temporal link prediction, and weakly supervised fraud detection, and provide baseline evaluations using machine learning models and graph neural networks. By jointly capturing behavioral activity, financial interactions, and relational structure within a unified virtual economy, TSAI-MetaFraud provides a benchmark for advancing multimodal learning, graph mining, fraud analytics, and trustworthy AI in emerging metaverse ecosystems.

</details>


### [70] [Statistically Undetectable Backdoors in Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09532)
*Andrej Bogdanov,Alon Rosen,Neekon Vafa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在深度前馈神经网络中，攻击者可以植入统计上不可检测的后门（即使在白盒设置下，后门模型与诚实训练模型的总变差距离仍然很小），该后门能为每个输入提供基于不变性的对抗样本，将远端输入映射到异常接近的输出；同时证明在没有后门的情况下，基于标准密码学假设，任何多项式时间内生成此类对抗样本都是不可能的，揭示了模型训练者与使用者之间根本性的能力不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示深度神经网络中存在的安全漏洞，特别是模型训练者能够植入在统计上无法检测的后门，从而在模型使用阶段产生对抗样本，这种能力不对称性在理论和实践层面都具有重要意义。

Method: 利用密码学假设和统计总变差距离理论，构建了在白盒设置下统计不可检测的后门植入方法；通过理论证明和初步实验验证，展示了后门模型与诚实模型在权重层面的不可区分性，以及后门提供的对抗样本生成能力。

Result: 后门模型与诚实模型在总变差距离上不可区分；后门能够为每个输入生成基于不变性的对抗样本；在没有后门的情况下，基于标准密码学假设，多项式时间内无法生成任何此类对抗样本。

Conclusion: 模型训练者与使用者之间存在根本性的能力不对称：训练者可以植入统计学上不可检测的后门并利用其生成对抗样本，而使用者即使完全掌握模型权重也无法在多项式时间内做到同样的事。这提醒我们需要对模型训练过程建立更强的信任机制和安全保障。

Abstract: We show how an adversarial model trainer can plant backdoors in a large class of deep, feedforward neural networks. These backdoors are statistically undetectable in the white-box setting, meaning that the backdoored and honestly trained models are close in total variation distance, even given the full descriptions of the models (e.g., all of the weights). The backdoor provides access to invariance-based adversarial examples for every input, mapping distant inputs to unusually close outputs. However, without the backdoor, it is provably impossible (under standard cryptographic assumptions) to generate any such adversarial examples in polynomial time. Our theoretical and preliminary empirical findings demonstrate a fundamental power asymmetry between model trainers and model users.

</details>


### [71] [GatedLinear: Adaptive Routing of Complementary Linear Bases for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09537)
*Qitai Tan,Ruiwen Gu,Yilin Su,Mo Li,Xu Lin,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GatedLinear框架，通过三因子融合门（Tri-Factorized Fusion Gate）自适应路由三种互补线性基（全局趋势-季节基、差分增量基、相位对齐循环基），以轻量级方式解决时间序列预测中多样且互斥的动态模式建模问题，在标准基准上达到SOTA或高度竞争性精度，同时提供可解释路由模式和更小参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型通常通过单一计算骨干（如自注意力或谱滤波）施加固定的算法归纳偏置来处理时间序列，难以应对真实序列中不同变量和预测 horizon 需要根本不同预测处理的异质性。需要一种能自适应路由不同预测机制的方法。

Method: GatedLinear包含三个核心组件：(1) 三种专用线性机制池：全局趋势-季节基（平滑投影）、差分增量基（非平稳漂移）、相位对齐循环基（显式周期复用）；(2) Tri-Factorized Fusion Gate：将路由决策解耦为通道偏好、horizon感知偏移和基于已知未来时间标记的相位索引偏置，实现细粒度逐点软路由；(3) 轻量级架构，避免堆叠重型神经模块。

Result: 在标准基准上达到SOTA或高度竞争性精度，优于近期复杂基础模型，同时具有更小的参数规模和显式的可解释路由模式。

Conclusion: 通过自适应路由互补线性基而非单一机制，可以有效处理时间序列中多样化的动态模式；三因子解耦的门控设计实现了高效且可解释的机制选择，验证了轻量级线性方法在复杂时序预测中的潜力。

Abstract: Time series forecasting requires models to capture diverse, often mutually exclusive, temporal dynamics, from smooth trend continuation to nonstationary drift and strict phase-aligned recurrence. While recent deep learning models have improved accuracy, they typically force these diverse patterns through a single computational backbone governed by fixed algorithmic inductive biases (e.g., self-attention or spectral filtering). This single-mechanism approach often struggles with the profound heterogeneity of real-world series, where different variables and forecast horizons necessitate fundamentally different predictive treatments. To address this, we propose GatedLinear: a lightweight framework that frames forecasting as the adaptive routing of complementary linear bases. GatedLinear leverages a pool of three specialized mechanisms: a global trend-seasonal basis for smooth projection, a difference-based incremental basis for nonstationary drift, and a phase-aligned recurrence basis for explicit cyclic reuse. To dynamically orchestrate these distinct behaviors, we introduce a Tri-Factorized Fusion Gate that disentangles routing decisions into channel-specific preferences, horizon-aware offsets, and phase-indexed biases derived from known future time marks. This design allows the model to perform highly granular, point-wise soft routing across different predictive regimes without stacking computationally heavy neural modules. Experiments on standard benchmarks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art or highly competitive accuracy against recent complex foundational models, while offering explicitly interpretable routing patterns and operating with a substantially smaller parameter footprint.

</details>


### [72] [CoCoT-EEG: Contrastive-Pretrained Multiscale Convolutional Transformer for EEG Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09543)
*Gabriel Mahuas,Victoria Shevchenko,Ugo Tanielian,Yassir Bendou,Richard Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCoT模型，采用对比预训练策略和多尺度时间卷积+Transformer编码器架构，在EEG解码任务中超越基于掩码重建预训练的最先进模型，且从头训练即可媲美预训练模型，展示了对比学习在EEG基础模型中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于掩码重建预训练的EEG基础模型因EEG信号噪声高、信息集中于有限维度（如窄频带）而表现次优，亟需探索更适合EEG特性的预训练策略。

Method: 提出CoCoT模型：采用多尺度时间卷积层作为输入层提取多尺度特征，结合Transformer编码器进行全局建模；使用对比学习预训练目标替代传统的掩码重建预训练。

Result: 在多种异构电极配置的EEG解码基准任务中，CoCoT匹配或超越现有基于重建预训练的最优模型；从头训练的CoCoT甚至超越先前的单任务解码模型，媲美预训练模型。

Conclusion: 对比学习策略在构建EEG基础模型中具有可行性，架构设计和预训练目标的选择至关重要，为探索替代性大规模预训练策略提供了新方向。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretrained foundation models (FM) have shown early promise for non-invasive electroencephalogram (EEG) decoding applications. Many recent large-scale models converged on the approach of tokenizing raw EEG followed by masked reconstruction pretraining. However, this recipe has been shown to be suboptimal for data, like EEG, with high noise amplitude and information confined to limited dimensions such as narrow frequency bands. Building on this insight, we develop a novel contrastive-pretrained EEG model with multiscale temporal convolution input layers and Transformer encoder blocks (CoCoT). CoCoT matches or beats state-of-the-art reconstruction-pretrained EEG models on extensive benchmark decoding tasks with heterogeneous electrode configurations. Furthermore, CoCoT trained from scratch outperforms previous single-task decoding models and even rivals pretrained models, showcasing the architecture's flexibility and data efficiency. Through systematic ablations, including model architecture and pretraining objective, we demonstrate the viability of contrastive learning for building EEG FMs while suggesting key architectural design considerations, prompting further investigations in alternative large-scale pretraining strategies.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [73] [Interval Certifications for Multilayered Perceptrons via Lattice Traversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08773)
*Merkouris Papamichail,Konstantinos Varsos,Giorgos Flouris,João Marques-Silva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种严格的理论框架，将AI安全中的对抗鲁棒性问题归结为格点遍历问题。通过定义区间（轴对齐超矩形）的可靠认证与完全认证，开发了基于格点遍历算子的迭代细化验证方案，并揭示了两种认证在优化问题上的不对称性：完全认证可在多项式时间内求解，而可靠认证具有强难解性。此外，针对对称区间（ℓ∞球）提出了对数级算法，并通过ParallelepipedoNN系统进行了实证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决AI安全中对抗鲁棒性的基础理论问题，特别是为多层感知器分类器建立严格的认证框架，并弥补文献中缺乏的完全认证研究。

Method: 将对抗鲁棒性问题归约为格点遍历问题，定义可靠认证和完全认证两种区间认证类型；开发格点遍历算子并应用"细化与验证"迭代方案；利用形式化MLP验证器保证最大可靠性与最小完全性；通过优化问题分析揭示计算复杂度不对称性。

Result: 完全认证的最小解可通过多项式次Oracle调用获得；可靠认证被证明是强难解的；在对称区间（ℓ∞球）上提出了对数级算法。

Conclusion: 对抗鲁棒性问题可纳入格点遍历的理论框架，可靠认证与完全认证在优化复杂度上存在本质不对称性，为AI安全认证提供了新的理论基础和算法工具。

Abstract: In this work we present a rigorous theoretical framework to a foundational problem of AI safety, namely adversarial robustness. In particular, we show that the adversarial robustness problem can be reduced to a lattice traversal problem. Each element of this lattice corresponds to an interval, i.e., an axis-aligned hyper-rectangle, containing an input point $\mathbf{x}$. Consider a multilayered perceptron classifier (MLP). An interval $I$ constitutes a sound certification if $\mathbf{x} \in I$ and $\mathbf{x}$ can be freely perturbed in $I$ without changing the MLP's prediction. Complementarily, an interval $I$ constitutes a complete certification if $\mathbf{x} \in I$ and when $\mathbf{x}$ moves outside of $I$ the MLP's prediction is guaranteed to change. While the sound certification problem corresponds to the well-studied adversarial robustness, complete certifications have not been examined in the literature. We develop lattice traversal operators, which we apply in a refine & verify iterative scheme. Using formal MLP verifiers, sound maximality and complete minimality are guaranteed. Moreover, we examine objective optimization problems. There we discover some interesting asymmetries. For complete certifications, the minimum solution is obtained in polynomial oracle calls. This does not hold for sound certifications, where we prove strong intractability results. Additionally, we examine optimization problems in symmetric intervals (i.e., $\ell_\infty$-spheres), where we provide logarithmic algorithms. Finally, we present an empirical evaluation, using the novel ParallelepipedoNN system.

</details>


### [74] [CogniConsole: Externalizing Inference-Time Control as a Formal Abstraction for Reliable LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08774)
*Vanessa Figueiredo,Wilter Franceschi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CogniConsole架构，通过推理时的结构化控制（程序化协调+提示推理）在固定模型下显著降低输出方差和失败率，证明许多LLM故障源于控制不足而非能力不足，主张将推理时控制作为一等抽象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战将LLM可靠性仅归因于模型能力的传统观点，论证推理时控制（任务框架与上下文选择）对可靠性的显著影响。

Method: 提出CogniConsole架构，将控制外部化为程序化协调与有界提示推理相结合的结构化接口；通过489个可控性导向探针在多步交互环境中进行实验。

Result: 从非结构化到全结构化的脚手架递增，系统性地降低了输出方差和失败率；上下文漂移和约束不一致等故障模式源于控制不足而非能力不足。

Conclusion: 推理时控制应作为一等抽象，为超越单纯规模扩展的LLM系统设计与评估开辟新方向。

Abstract: Reliability in large language model (LLM) systems is typically framed as a function of model capability. We challenge this by demonstrating that reliability is significantly influenced by \emph{inference-time control} -- the computational layer governing task framing and context selection. We introduce \emph{CogniConsole}, an architectural instantiation that externalizes this control into a structured interface combining programmatic coordination with bounded prompt-based reasoning. Through \emph{controllability-oriented probes} ($N=489$) in a multi-step interactive environment, we show that increasing structural scaffolding -- from unstructured to fully scaffolded -- \textbf{systematically reduces output variance and failure rates under a fixed model architecture}. Our results indicate that many observed failure modes, such as context drift and inconsistent constraint adherence, arise from under-specified control rather than insufficient capability. This work provides an empirical basis for treating inference-time control as a first-class abstraction, opening new directions for designing and evaluating LLM systems beyond scaling alone.

</details>


### [75] [GATS: Graph-Augmented Tree Search with Layered World Models for Efficient Agent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08894)
*Maureese Williams,Dymitr Nowicki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GATS是一个结合UCB1树搜索与三层世界模型的规划框架，在规划阶段无需LLM调用即可实现100%任务成功率，显著优于依赖LLM推理的LATS和ReAct方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体规划方法（如LATS、ReAct）在推理过程中高度依赖LLM，导致计算成本高昂且行为随机不稳定。

Method: 提出GATS框架，核心包括：(1) 基于UCB1的系统性树搜索；(2) 三层世界模型——L1精确符号动作匹配、L2执行日志统计学习、L3未知动作的LLM预测；(3) 规划阶段零LLM调用。

Result: 合成任务上GATS成功率100%（LATS 92%, ReAct 64%）；12项挑战性场景中GATS保持100%成功率（LATS 88.9%, ReAct 23.9%）；每任务零LLM调用（LATS需37次）；输出确定性零方差计划。

Conclusion: 系统性的搜索结合学习到的世界模型，在智能体规划任务上能显著超越依赖LLM引导的探索方法。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have shown promise in multi-step planning tasks, but existing approaches like LATS (Language Agent Tree Search) and ReAct rely heavily on LLM inference during planning, leading to high computational costs and stochastic behavior.
