We present \textbf{GATS} (Graph-Augmented Tree Search), a planning framework that combines systematic UCB1-based tree search with a layered world model to eliminate LLM calls during inference while achieving superior planning performance. Our three-layer world model integrates: (L1) exact symbolic action matching, (L2) statistics learned from execution logs, and (L3) LLM-based prediction for unknown actions. On synthetic planning tasks with branching paths and dead-ends, GATS achieves \textbf{100\% success rate} compared to 92 % for LATS and 64\% for ReAct. On a comprehensive stress test spanning 12 challenging scenarios -- including coding workflows, web navigation, and long-horizon tasks -- GATS maintains \textbf{100\% success} while LATS drops to 88.9 % and ReAct to 23.9%. GATS requires \textbf{zero LLM calls per task} during planning (vs. 37 per task for LATS) and produces deterministic plans with zero variance across runs. Our results demonstrate that systematic search with learned world models can substantially outperform LLM-guided exploration for agent planning.

</details>


### [76] [Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench: Testing the Limits of Agents on Long-Horizon Terminal Tasks with Dense Reward-Based Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08964)
*Zongxia Li,Zhongzhi Li,Yucheng Shi,Ruhan Wang,Junyao Yang,Zhichao Liu,Xiyang Wu,Anhao Li,Yue Yu,Ninghao Liu,Lichao Sun,Haotao Mi,LeoweiLiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench（LHTB），一个包含46个长时域任务的终端基准测试，涵盖实验复现、软件工程、多模态分析等9个类别。每个任务被分解为细粒度计步子任务，支持密集中间奖励和部分评分。评估15个前沿模型发现，最强模型在0.95部分奖励阈值下仅达15.2% pass@1，在完美奖励下为10.9%，平均通过率分别为4.3%和1.7%，揭示出巨大改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终端基准测试主要关注几分钟内完成的简单问题，仅评估最终结果，忽略了中间进度和部分解决方案，导致奖励信号稀疏且无法全面评估智能体能力。

Method: 设计46个长时域任务，分解为细粒度计步子任务，采用参考解或仿真引擎设置，支持密集中间奖励和部分学分评估。

Result: 智能体平均每任务消耗990万token、约231个episode、85.3分钟执行时间。最强模型在0.95阈值下pass@1为15.2%，1.0阈值下为10.9%；所有模型平均通过率分别为4.3%和1.7%。

Conclusion: LHTB比现有终端基准测试更具挑战性，当前模型表现仍有巨大改进空间，该基准将支持未来长时域终端智能体的研究进展。

Abstract: AI agents have become capable of autonomously completing short, well-specified tasks. However, existing terminal benchmarks largely focus on simple problems that finish within minutes and are evaluated only by their final outcome. This setup overlooks intermediate progress and partial solutions, yielding sparse reward signals and an incomplete picture of agent capability. We introduce Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench, a terminal benchmark of 46 long-horizon tasks spanning nine categories, including experiment reproduction, software engineering, multimodal analysis, interactive games, and scientific computing. Each task follows a Terminal-Bench-style setup with a reference solution or simulation engine, but is further decomposed into fine-grained graded subtasks. This design enables dense intermediate rewards and partial credit, allowing evaluation to capture not only whether an agent reaches the final goal, but also how far it progresses on open-ended workflows. Tasks in Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench typically require hundreds of episodes and minutes to hours of execution, stressing long-horizon planning, long-context management, and iterative debugging rather than one-shot problem solving. We evaluate 15 frontier models and find that agents consume on average 9.9M tokens per task, with roughly 231 episodes and 85.3 minutes of execution time per run, making Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench more demanding than prior terminal-based benchmarks. Even the strongest tested model achieves 15.2% pass@1 at a partial-reward threshold of 0.95 and 10.9% at a perfect-reward threshold of 1.0, while the mean pass rate across models is 4.3% and 1.7% under the two thresholds, respectively. These results reveal headroom for improvement. We further analyze failure modes and error patterns, and release Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench to support future progress on long-horizon terminal agents.

</details>


### [77] [A Formalization of the Mean-Field Derivation of the Vlasov Equation: AI-Assisted Lean Formalization as a Strategy Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08986)
*Joseph K. Miller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将数学定理形式化的新方法：由数学家指导AI系统，将LaTeX文档转化为Lean 4证明助手中的可编译、无漏洞、仅依赖基础公理的完整代码。以非线性Vlasov方程适定性问题为案例（存在性、唯一性、稳定性估计、平均场极限及Lagrangian解原理），在约一周完成主定理、一个月完成全部开发。其中最优传输相关结果（Wasserstein-1度量、Kantorovich-Rubinstein对偶）可分离为独立于Mathlib的自包含层（299个声明中49个，22个声明接口，无逆向依赖）。人类角色是指导而非编写证明：定义范围、分解策略、填补库缺口；AI执行。该形式化验证了每个陈述的证明，但陈述是否为预期定理仍需数学家判断。方法框架不限定具体系统，旨在超越任何单一工具的生命周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种由数学家指导AI系统进行定理形式化的新范式，将形式化过程框架化为一个"形式化游戏"，以降低形式化验证的门槛，使数学家无需深入掌握证明助手的实现细节即可验证复杂定理。

Method: 采用"形式化游戏"框架：数学家扮演指导者（定义范围、分解策略、评估库缺口），AI系统扮演执行者（编写证明代码）。目标是将LaTeX文档转化为Lean 4代码，要求编译通过、无sorry、依赖仅限Lean基础公理。通过"可重用性"检查评估结果是否能作为自包含的通用数学层被更广库吸收。以非线性Vlasov方程的Dobrushin平均场路线为案例进行完整形式化。

Result: 完成了非线性Vlasov方程适定性问题的完整形式化（存在性、唯一性、稳定性估计、平均场极限、Lagrangian解原理）。主定理约一周完成，全部开发约一个月。其中最优传输相关结果（Wasserstein-1度量、Kantorovich-Rubinstein对偶）分离为自包含层：299个声明中49个，22个声明接口，无逆向依赖，可独立编译于Mathlib。

Conclusion: 人类指导+AI执行的协作范式可有效加速定理形式化进程。形式化验证了每个陈述的证明正确性，但陈述是否为预期定理仍需数学家判断。方法框架不限定具体系统，具有超越当前工具的持久性。定量结果作为单次游戏的观察而非普适规律。

Abstract: We formalize a research result in the Lean 4 proof assistant by having a mathematician direct an AI system, and frame the activity as a formalization game. The objective is to turn a LaTeX document into Lean. The game is won when the development compiles, contains no sorry, and a machine check shows the target theorems rest on Lean's foundational axioms alone. Reuse is a second check, by a definition we introduce: whether the development yields a self-contained layer of general mathematics the wider library could absorb. The case study is a complete, axiom-clean formalization of well-posedness for the nonlinear Vlasov equation via Dobrushin's mean-field route -- existence, uniqueness, the stability estimate and mean-field limit, and a short-window superposition principle (weak solutions are Lagrangian). The human's role was to direct, not to write proofs: to scope the definitions, steer the decompositions, and triage the library's gaps; the AI agent executed. The formalization certifies the proof of each statement as written; whether the written statement is the intended theorem stays the mathematician's judgment. The optimal-transport machinery that fell out of the build (in particular, properties of the Wasserstein-1 metric and the Kantorovich-Rubinstein duality theorem) separates into a self-contained layer that compiles against Mathlib alone: about a sixth of the development (49 of 299 declarations), behind a 22-declaration interface with no reverse dependency. The headline theorems ran in about a week, the full development in about a month. We report the quantitative claims as observations of one game, not as general laws. The game's rules name no particular system, so the methodological framing is meant to outlast the tools of any one run.

</details>


### [78] [ARCANA: A Reflective Multi-Agent Program Synthesis Framework for ARC-AGI-2 Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09059)
*Kunbo Zhang,Lei Fu,Zeyu Wang,Zijing Liu,Kejian Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARCANA 是一个协作式多智能体框架，用于在严格时间和硬件约束下解决 ARC AGI 2 任务。它将每个任务分解为迭代的感知、假设生成、符号执行和反思优化，通过可微黑板通信并由元控制器调度，结合结构化的程序搜索与自适应多轮修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严格的时间和硬件约束下，现有的单一模型方法难以高效解决 ARC AGI 2 中的抽象变换任务，需要一种结合结构化程序搜索与自适应多轮修正的协作式多智能体框架来提升推理效率和解决方案质量。

Method: ARCANA 包含四个核心智能体：感知基础智能体（从原始网格构建以对象为中心的场景图）、潜在程序策略（生成多样化的 DSL 程序）、符号执行器（在演示上验证候选方案）、反思智能体（综合失败驱动的反馈进行下一轮修正）。智能体通过共享的可微黑板通信，并由学习的元控制器调度。

Result: 该框架在挑战性的抽象变换任务上提升了推理效率和解决方案质量，能够有效处理时间与硬件资源的严格约束。

Conclusion: ARCANA 通过将结构化程序搜索与自适应多轮修正相结合，证明了协作式多智能体框架在解决抽象推理任务上的有效性，为在受限环境下处理 ARC AGI 类问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present ARCANA, a collaborative multi agent framework for solving ARC AGI 2 tasks under strict test time and hardware constraints. ARCANA decomposes each task into iterative perception, hypothesis generation, symbolic execution, and reflective refinement. A perceptual grounding agent builds object centric scene graphs from raw grids, a latent program policy proposes diverse DSL programs, a symbolic executor verifies candidates on demonstrations, and a reflective agent synthesizes failure driven feedback for the next turn. These agents communicate through a shared differentiable blackboard and are scheduled by a learned meta controller. The design combines structured program search with adaptive multi turn correction, improving reasoning efficiency and solution quality on challenging abstract transformation tasks.

</details>


### [79] [Neuro-Agentic Control: A Deep Learning-based LLM-Powered Agentic AI Framework for Controlling Security Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09076)
*Saroj Gopali,Bipin Chhetri,Deepika Giri,Sima Siami-Namini,Akbar Siami Namin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经代理控制框架（Neuro-Agentic Control Framework），将基于LLM的规划器（如Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite）与预训练时间序列基础模型（TimesFM）耦合，通过“反事实物理注入”机制在物理执行前检验LLM提议的干预措施，实现了物理可信的自主防御。在SWaT工业数据集上，该框架相比LSTM和TCN基线表现更优，成功阻止了33.3%的阈值越界攻击，且零次执行物理无效（幻觉）动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业物联网环境中，针对操作技术的网络攻击日益增加，传统基于规则的监控方法存在局限性。虽然LLM具备强大的语义推理能力可用于决策支持，但其幻觉特性在闭环控制中会带来不可接受的安全隐患。需要一种既能利用LLM推理能力又能确保物理安全性的架构。

Method: 提出神经代理控制框架，核心由三部分组成：1) LLM规划器（Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite）负责高层决策推理；2) 预训练时间序列基础模型（TimesFM）作为数值潜空间物理建模器；3) 反事实物理注入机制——在实际行动前，将LLM提议的干预措施注入TimesFM的数值潜空间中进行物理可行性模拟，拒绝幻觉或不安全动作。

Result: 在SWaT工业数据集上的随机攻击场景评估中：Neuro-Agentic Loop阻止了5次阈值越界（33.3%），优于LSTM（26.7%）和TCN（13.3%）；且零次执行物理无效（幻觉）动作，证明了基础模型作为确定性“哨兵”守护代理AI在关键基础设施中的有效性。

Conclusion: 将LLM的语义推理能力与时间序列基础模型的物理建模能力结合，并通过反事实物理注入机制作为安全屏障，可以有效解决LLM在工业控制中的幻觉问题，实现物理可信的自主防御。基础模型可作为确定性的“哨兵”来保障关键基础设施中代理AI的安全性。

Abstract: Cyberattacks on operational technology are increasingly causing costly downtime and physical damage, exposing the limitations of traditional rule-based monitoring in industrial IoT environments. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have strong semantic reasoning abilities to assist in decision support, their hallucinatory nature presents unacceptable safety liabilities for closed-loop control. This paper introduces a neuro-agentic control framework, a novel architecture that couples an LLM-based planner (i.e., such as Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite) with a pre-trained Time-Series Foundation Model (TimesFM), to achieve physics-grounded autonomous defense. The paper introduces a ``Counterfactual Physics Injection'' mechanism that simulates the impact of LLM-proposed interventions within the numerical latent space of the foundation model before actuation, while allowing the system to reject hallucinatory or unsafe actions. Evaluated on an industrial dataset (e.g., the Secure Water Treatment (SWaT)) in the context of stochastic attack scenarios, the framework exhibited better performance compared to LSTM and TCN baselines. The Neuro-Agentic Loop prevented five breaches (33.3%) below the threshold versus LSTM (26.7%) and TCN (13.3%), with zero physically invalid (hallucinated) actions executed. These results demonstrate the efficacy of using foundation models as deterministic ``Sentinels'' to safeguard agentic AI in critical infrastructure.

</details>


### [80] [L-MAD: A Systematic Evaluation of Multi-Agent Debate Structures in Legal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09099)
*Tan-Minh Nguyen,Hoang-Trung Nguyen,Huu-Dong Nguyen,Dinh-Truong Do,Thi-Hai-Yen Vuong,Le-Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出法律多智能体辩论框架（L-MAD），在结构化法律文本蕴含任务中系统评估不同辩论结构与聚合方法。通过分配专家角色，L-MAD相比强单智能体基线提升高达8%。研究发现增加智能体数量可降低不一致性并提升准确率，但延长讨论轮次会导致"过度讨论漂移"——智能体相互强化错误。研究划定了在高风险法律推理中部署协作多智能体系统的实际边界与安全区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体辩论（MAD）在通用推理中表现出潜力，但在高度结构化、知识密集的法律领域中的有效性尚缺乏系统探索。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出L-MAD框架，为多个智能体分配不同的专家角色（如律师、法官等），在Legal Textual Entailment任务上系统评估不同辩论结构与聚合方法，对比单智能体基线并分析辩论规模（智能体数量与讨论轮次）的影响。

Result: L-MAD相比强单智能体基线提升高达8%。增加智能体数量可降低不一致性、提升准确率；但延长讨论轮次导致"过度讨论漂移"（over-deliberation drift），即智能体相互强化错误。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了多智能体辩论在法律领域中的双重效应：规模扩大（更多智能体）有益，但深度扩展（更多轮次）有害。划定了在高风险法律推理中部署协作多智能体系统的实际边界与安全区间。

Abstract: While multi-agent debate (MAD) frameworks have shown significant potential in general reasoning, their effectiveness in highly structured, knowledge-heavy legal domains remains under-explored. In this work, we introduce the Legal Multi-Agent Debate (L-MAD) framework to systematically evaluate different debate structures and aggregation methods within Legal Textual Entailment. By assigning distinct expert personas to multiple agents, L-MAD improves upon strong single-agent baselines by up to 8\%. Furthermore, analyzing how debate scales reveals a clear trade-off: increasing the agent population reduces inconsistency and improves accuracy, whereas extending discussion rounds induces a detrimental \textit{over-deliberation drift} where agents reinforce each other's mistakes. Ultimately, our findings outline the practical boundaries and safety margins of deploying collaborative multi-agent systems in high-stakes legal reasoning environments.

</details>


### [81] [MedRealMM: A Real-World Multimodal Benchmark for Chinese Online Medical Consultation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09142)
*Runhan Shi,Quan Zhou,Yuqian Xu,Shuai Yang,Xin Wu,Zitong Zhou,Hui Liu,Bin Cha,Zheming Wang,Liya Li,Wei Wei,Haoyuan Hu,Jun Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedRealMM是一个基于真实医患交互数据构建的大规模多模态在线医疗咨询基准，包含5,620个真实多模态病例，覆盖64个临床科室。该基准通过MCCP提取框架识别临床关键时刻，并采用病例特定的评分规则进行评估。实验表明，图像信息对临床性能至关重要，当前前沿模型虽在部分正面指标上可媲美医生，但触发更多负面标准，安全敏感的误差避免仍是核心瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM医疗咨询基准存在以下问题：多基于合成对话或患者模拟器；忽略患者上传的医学图像；使用多项选择或词法重叠指标评估开放性临床回答，无法反映真实临床质量。因此需要构建一个与真实临床实践对齐的评估基准。

Method: 1) 从中国互联网医院收集脱敏的真实医患交互数据；2) 提出多模态临床挑战点(MCCP)提取框架，识别真实咨询轨迹中的临床关键时刻；3) 将每个关键时刻转换为标准化的下一轮回答生成任务，保留前序文本-图像上下文；4) 由医师构建病例特定的评分规则，奖励临床可取行为，惩罚不安全、无依据或矛盾的回答。

Result: 评估了19个通用和医学专用LLM（包括纯文本和多模态系统）。结果显示：图像信息对可靠的临床性能至关重要；当前前沿模型的性能仍低于在线医生；部分前沿模型在满足正面临床标准上可与医生媲美，但触发更多负面标准。

Conclusion: MedRealMM为评估多模态在线医疗咨询提供了真实且可复现的基准，揭示了安全敏感的误差避免是当前LLM应用于临床的核心瓶颈。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in online medical consultation, yet existing benchmarks remain poorly aligned with real clinical practice. Many rely on synthetic conversations or patient simulators, omit patient-uploaded medical images, or evaluate open-ended clinical responses using multiple-choice or lexical-overlap metrics that poorly reflect clinical quality. We introduce \textbf{MedRealMM}, a large-scale benchmark for multimodal online medical consultation built from de-identified patient-doctor interactions collected from a nationwide Chinese internet hospital. MedRealMM uses a Multimodal Clinical Challenge Point (MCCP) extraction framework to identify clinically demanding moments in authentic consultation trajectories and converts each into a standardized next-response generation task while preserving the preceding text-image context. Each instance is paired with a case-specific rubric refined by physicians that rewards clinically desirable behaviors and penalizes unsafe, unsupported, or contradictory responses. The current release contains 5,620 real-world multimodal cases spanning 64 clinical departments. We evaluate 19 general-purpose and medical-specialized LLMs, including text-only and multimodal systems. Our results show that image information is critical for reliable clinical performance and that current frontier models remain below the online physician response. Although some frontier models satisfy as many or more positive clinical criteria than physicians, they trigger more negative criteria, indicating that safety-sensitive error avoidance remains a central bottleneck. MedRealMM offers a realistic and reproducible benchmark for evaluating multimodal medical reasoning in real-world online consultation. The dataset will be publicly available on Hugging Face at https://huggingface.co/datasets/jdh-algo/MedRealMM.

</details>


### [82] [KV-PRM: Efficient Process Reward Modeling via KV-Cache Transfer for Multi-Agent Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09153)
*Peng Kuang,Haibo Jin,Xiaoyu Han,Yanli Wang,Xiaopeng Yuan,Ye Yu,Kaidi Xu,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KV-PRM提出了一种基于KV缓存的高效过程奖励模型，通过直接读取LLM生成阶段产生的KV缓存而非重新编码整个文本轨迹，将评分成本从O(L²)降至O(L)，在MATH、GSM8K和AIME基准上匹配或超越文本PRM，同时实现最高5000倍FLOPs减少、37倍延迟降低和34倍内存节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本PRM在多智能体长轨迹场景下需重新编码全部文本，评分成本随序列长度L呈二次增长，造成严重计算瓶颈，限制了PRM在长上下文场景中的应用。

Method: KV-PRM利用LLM生成阶段自然产生的KV缓存，通过处理单个"验证令牌"与已有KV缓存的交互来直接进行奖励建模，避免了文本的重新编码，并通过理论证明KV缓存比文本具有更大的信息容量。

Result: 在MATH、GSM8K和AIME基准上，KV-PRM在Beam Search、MCTS和Weighted Voting等多种TTS方法下匹配或严格优于文本PRM；评分FLOPs降低高达5000倍、延迟降低37倍、每序列内存占用降低34倍。

Conclusion: KV-PRM通过利用KV缓存实现了高效且性能优异的过程奖励建模，为PRM在长上下文场景中的实际应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Process Reward Models (PRMs) have been proven to be highly effective in guiding test-time scaling (TTS) methods, which significantly boost the capabilities of LLM-based multi-agent systems. However, existing PRMs are text-based: they re-encode the entire trajectory text from scratch. In long multi-agent rollouts, the scoring cost, growing quadratically with respect to sequence length L, creates a severe computational bottleneck, severely limiting PRMs' application in long-context scenarios. To resolve this, we introduce KV-PRM, a highly efficient process reward model that eliminates the heavy text re-encoding by directly reading the KV cache produced naturally during the LLM's generation phase. By processing a single "verify token" against the pre-existing KV cache, KV-PRM reduces the scoring cost from O(L^2) to O(L). We formally prove that the KV cache contains strictly greater information capacity than text, and is more efficient for downstream reward modeling. Empirically, across the MATH, GSM8K, and AIME benchmarks, KV-PRM matches or strictly outperforms text-PRMs under various TTS methods such as Beam Search, MCTS, and Weighted Voting, with up to a 5,000x reduction in scoring FLOPs, a 37x reduction in latency, and a 34x reduction in per-sequence memory footprint compared to text-based PRMs.

</details>


### [83] [Scoped Verification for Reliable Long-Horizon Agentic Context Evolution under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09175)
*Dan C. Hsu,Luke Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GRACE方法，将LLM agent的持久性系统指令维护为类型化语义图，通过局部邻域验证实现上下文演化，在电信agent任务上将可靠性从0.091提升至0.673，显著优于平面文本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署的LLM agent依赖持续更新的系统指令，但平面文本维护方式在长期演化中因指令积累和相互干扰导致验证困难，亟需一种结构化的上下文演化方法。

Method: GRACE将持久指令组件维护为类型化语义图，在修改节点的局部类型化邻域内验证更新，接受的图更新重建为增量文本编辑，应用于部署时的指令检查点。

Result: 在固定电信agent框架下，GRACE的pass^3可靠性从0.091提升至0.673±0.136，超越Gemini 3.1 Pro零样本基准0.242，而平面文本HCE基线仅为0.191±0.051。

Conclusion: 可靠的长期上下文演化需要两个条件：使验证局部化的结构基板，以及保持累积指令内容可用的整合机制。

Abstract: Deployed LLM agents rely on agentic context, the model-external textual control content assembled by an operational harness. In this work, the mutable component of that context is a persistent system-level instruction that is updated from operational experience while the model, tools, and harness remain fixed. Over long evolution horizons, flat-text maintenance makes verification increasingly difficult as accumulated instructions grow and interact. We propose Graph-Regularized Agentic Context Evolution (GRACE), which maintains the persistent instruction component as a typed semantic graph and validates proposed updates within the local typed neighborhoods of modified nodes. Accepted graph updates are reconstructed as incremental edits to the textual instruction checkpoint used at deployment. We evaluate GRACE within a fixed telecom agent harness derived from $τ^2$-bench under a controlled distribution-shift protocol. Across five independent replications, GRACE improves strict reliability, measured by pass^3, from the Gemini 2.5 Flash zero-shot value of 0.091 to 0.673$\pm$0.136 at the final checkpoint. This exceeds a Gemini 3.1 Pro zero-shot reference of 0.242 on the same held-out set, while the flat-text HCE baseline finishes at 0.191$\pm$0.051. These results identify two requirements for reliable long-horizon context evolution, a structural substrate that makes verification local and a consolidation mechanism that keeps accumulated instruction content usable.

</details>


### [84] [Toward Auditable AI Scientists: A Hypothesis Evolution Protocol for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09195)
*Izumi Takahara,Teruyasu Mizoguchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出假设演化协议（HEP），一种将假设生成、评估和演化作为显式、可审计操作的智能体框架，使LLM智能体在材料科学研究任务中能够执行假设-测试-证据-信念循环，实现可审计的AI科学推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的假设、测试和信念更新都隐藏在非结构化的日志中，缺乏让智能体或人类研究者审计这一过程的机制，导致科学推理过程不可追溯、不可验证。

Method: 提出假设演化协议（HEP），一种智能体框架，将假设生成、评估和演化作为显式、可审计的操作，使智能体能够执行假设-测试-证据-信念循环。

Result: 在材料科学研究任务上，配备HEP的智能体能够执行规划式智能体所缺乏的假设-测试-证据-信念循环，跨研究问题具有泛化能力，并且随着基础LLM能力的增强，能更充分地利用该协议。

Conclusion: 这项工作朝着可审计的AI科学家迈出了一步，其科学推理过程可以被检查、验证和在此基础上构建。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly expected to play a central role in AI-driven scientific discovery. Equipped with broad knowledge, flexible reasoning, and tool use, they have the potential to autonomously explore and solve scientific problems by repeatedly proposing hypotheses, testing them, and revising their beliefs in the light of the evidence. In current agents, however, these hypotheses, tests, and belief updates are buried in unstructured logs, and no mechanism lets the agent or the human researcher audit that process. Here we propose the Hypothesis Evolution Protocol (HEP), an agent harness that provides hypothesis generation, evaluation, and evolution as explicit, auditable operations. On materials-science research tasks, a HEP-equipped agent operates the hypothesis--test--evidence--belief cycle that planning-style agents lack, generalizes across research questions, and exploits the protocol more fully as the base LLM becomes more capable. These results mark a step toward auditable AI scientists, whose scientific reasoning can be inspected, verified, and built upon.

</details>


### [85] [OpenProver: Agentic and Interactive Theorem Proving with Lean 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09217)
*Matěj Kripner,Milan Straka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了OpenProver，一个基于LLM的自动化定理证明开源系统，集成了Lean 4形式化验证，采用Planner-Worker-Verifier架构，支持交互式人机协同证明搜索，并提供可重复的定量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的自动化定理证明系统缺乏开源实现和可重复评估机制，尤其是缺少自动形式化验证来确保证明的正确性；同时，人机协同在定理证明中的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 采用Planner-Worker-Verifier三层架构：Planner代理维护一个紧凑的Whiteboard草稿板和一个无界Repository存储中间发现，并将数学工作分解为多个并行Worker；系统集成Lean 4形式化验证，提供交互式终端界面支持人类操作员监控和引导证明搜索。

Result: 在ProofNet基准上对OpenProver进行了定量消融实验评估，并与简单基线进行了对比，展示了自动形式化验证在可重复实验中的价值。

Conclusion: OpenProver是一个完全开源、支持自动形式化验证和交互式人机协同的自动化定理证明系统，能够实现可重复的定量消融实验，为LLM驱动的ATP研究提供了可复现的平台。

Abstract: In this system paper, we present OpenProver, an open-source system for LLM-driven automated theorem proving (ATP) with integrated Lean 4 formal verification. OpenProver integrates a Planner-Worker-Verifier architecture inspired by recent ATP agentic systems such as Aletheia. A Planner agent maintains a compact Whiteboard scratchpad and an unbounded Repository of intermediate findings, and decomposes mathematical work into parallel Workers.
  OpenProver is fully open-source, offers reproducible evaluation through automatic formal verification of generated proofs, and provides an interactive terminal interface for human-guided proof search. In interactive mode, OpenProver allows the human operator to monitor and steer the proof search process, motivated by the established human-AI synergy in interactive code generation.
  To showcase the potential for quantitative ablation experiments enabled by automatic formal verification, we evaluate OpenProver on ProofNet and compare it with a simple baseline. OpenProver is publicly available at https://github.com/kripner/OpenProver.

</details>


### [86] [LongMedBench: Benchmarking Medical Agents for Long-Horizon Clinical Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09322)
*Yanzhen Chen,Zihan Xu,Xiaocheng Zhang,Zhiting Fan,Weiqi Zhai,Hongxia Xu,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LongMedBench是一个基于MIMIC-IV真实EHR数据的长期临床决策基准，包含335名患者、平均每名患者19.72次住院访问的经验流数据集，用于评估LLM智能体在长期、多会话临床环境中的事实QA、时间推理和长期决策能力。实验发现，当前LLM能利用显式时间戳但难以进行隐式时间推理，RAG和记忆系统可提升信息检索性能，但决策任务性能高度依赖模型的即时上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM医疗智能体评估主要关注短上下文知识问答和工具使用，但真实医疗是纵向的，临床医生需要在多次就诊、检查和治疗中聚合证据。因此，需要建立长期、多会话交互的基准来真实评估智能体的临床决策能力。

Method: 利用可复现的流水线整合MIMIC-IV入院记录和临床笔记，构建时间序列事件流和长上下文记忆数据集；设计包含事实QA、时间推理和长期决策三个维度的评估分类体系。

Result: 数据集包含335名患者，平均每人19.72次住院访问，每次访问44.91个医疗事件。实验表明：LLM能利用显式时间戳但在隐式时间推理上有困难；RAG和记忆系统可提升信息检索性能；决策任务性能高度依赖模型即时上下文。

Conclusion: 长期、多会话交互对真实评估医疗智能体至关重要，当前模型在隐式时间推理和依赖长历史上下文的决策任务上仍存在显著挑战。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce LongMedBench, a real-world EHR-based benchmark for long-horizon clinical decision-making. Prior evaluations of LLM-based medical agents have largely emphasized short-context knowledge QA and tool use. However, real-world medical care is inherently longitudinal, and clinicians must aggregate evidence across repeated visits, tests, and evolving treatments. Therefore, long-horizon interaction is essential for realistic assessment. LongMedBench is constructed via a reproducible pipeline that integrates MIMIC-IV admission records and clinical notes into time-series event streams and long-context memory datasets, enabling long-horizon, multi-session interactions between agents and a clinical environment. It comprises 335 patients, with 19.72 inpatient visits per patient on average and 44.91 medical events per visit. Guided by the long-horizon decision process, we propose an evaluation taxonomy with three suites: fact-based QA, temporal reasoning, and long-horizon decision-making. This taxonomy measures how agents understand and leverage historical patient information over extended horizons. Our experiments show that while recent LLMs can make good use of explicit timestamps, they have challenges in implicit time inference; The RAG and agent memory system can improve the performance of information retrieval tasks, but the performance of decision-making tasks is highly dependent on the model's immediate context.

</details>


### [87] [Communication-Efficient Digital-Twin Coordination for Heterogeneous LLM Embodied Agents over Computing Power Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09330)
*Nuocheng Yang,Sihua Wang,Zihan Chen,Tony Q. S. Quek,Changchuan Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LDT-Coord框架，利用轻量级数字孪生解耦异构LLM智能体团队的协调性能与自然语言推理能力，通过结构化时间约束和规则化调度算法解决冲突，并采用C-POMDP与PPO-Lagrangian控制通信开销，在保持任务成功率的同时降低通信开销70倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多轮自然语言对话的异构LLM智能体协调框架面临三大耦合挑战：通信开销随团队规模快速增长、协调质量受限于LLM异构能力、智能体因迭代协商遭受动作延迟。

Method: 1) 每个智能体独立选择意图动作，向DT服务器报告动作决策及共享资源上的结构化时间约束；2) DT运行免训练规则化编排算法解决跨智能体冲突并返回协调指令；3) 将智能体报告控制建模为C-POMDP，采用PPO-Lagrangian算法求解以降低通信开销。

Result: LDT-Coord在保持与传统方法相当的任务成功率的同时，将通信开销降低70倍以上，并在LLM异构性下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过数字孪生解耦协调与语言推理能力，结合结构化约束与规则化调度，可有效解决异构LLM智能体团队协调中的通信、异构能力与延迟挑战。

Abstract: Embodied agent teams powered by heterogeneous large language models (LLMs) are being widely deployed in physical artificial intelligence such as smart factories, warehouses, and service robotics. To enable collaboration among such an agent team, efficient coordination mechanisms that operate reliably under limited network resources are required. However, existing heterogeneous LLM-agent coordination frameworks that rely on multi-round natural-language-based conversations introduce three coupled challenges. First, inter-agent dialogue incurs communication overhead that grows rapidly with team size. Second, the quality of coordination is constrained by the heterogeneous capabilities of the agent team's LLMs. Third, agents may suffer from action delays due to iterative negotiation. To address these challenges, we propose LDT-Coord, a networked coordination framework built upon a lightweight digital twin (DT). Specifically, each agent independently selects its intended action and reports both the action decision and a structured temporal constraint over shared resources to the DT server, thereby decoupling coordination performance from natural-language reasoning ability. Then, DT executes a training-free, rule-based orchestrator algorithm to resolve cross-agent conflicts and returns coordination instructions to prevent such conflicts. To further reduce communication overhead, we formulate agent reporting control as a constrained partially observable Markov decision process (C-POMDP) and solve it with the PPO-Lagrangian algorithm. Simulation results show that LDT-Coord achieves a task success rate comparable to conventional coordination methods while reducing communication overhead by more than 70x and maintaining robustness under LLM heterogeneity.

</details>


### [88] [Fictional Worldbuilding: Multi-Agent LLM Collaboration with Hierarchical Context Compression and Iterative Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09403)
*Jingbo Chen,He Wang,Wei Yuan,Yuqiao Lai,Zhenyan Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AutoWorldBuilder多智能体协作系统，通过结构化概念网络、DAG调度器、四层上下文压缩（~90% token缩减）、迭代审核机制和技能驱动架构，解决LLM应用于世界构建时的上下文爆炸、创意多样性与一致性矛盾、缺乏自动质量保证三大挑战。在20个任务上达到95%成功率，每个世界生成56-103个自洽概念，零冲突交付。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在自动世界构建中面临三个核心挑战：构建过程线性增长的上下文爆炸、创意多样性与内容一致性之间的张力、缺乏自动化质量保证机制。

Method: 提出AutoWorldBuilder系统，包含五个集成组件：结构化概念网络（含冲突检测）、基于DAG的混合批调度器（按语义局部性分组任务）、四层上下文压缩机制（实现约90% token缩减）、迭代审核系统（含专门Auditor智能体，提案通过率从42%提升至85%以上）、技能驱动智能体架构（支持零代码扩展及差异化温度配置）。

Result: 在20个多样化世界构建任务上，使用GPT-OSS 120B和DeepSeek v3.2作为LLM后端，达到95.0%成功率。每个世界生成56-103个自洽概念，耗时18-31分钟，零冲突交付。

Conclusion: 验证了层即预算压缩、语义局部性调度、生成与审核分离等架构模式的有效性，这些模式可迁移至更广泛的知识密集型多智能体LLM应用。

Abstract: Worldbuilding, the construction of coherent fictional worlds, is a foundational task in game design and literary creation. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer new possibilities for automated content generation, but their application to worldbuilding faces three challenges: context explosion that grows linearly with the building process, the tension between creative diversity and content consistency, and the absence of automated quality assurance. This paper presents AutoWorldBuilder, a multi-agent collaborative system that addresses these challenges through five integrated components: a structured concept network with conflict detection; a DAG-based hybrid batch scheduler that groups tasks by semantic locality; a four-layer context compression mechanism achieving approximately 90% token reduction; an iterative review system with specialized Auditor agents that improves proposal pass rates from 42% to over 85%; and a skill-driven agent architecture supporting zero-code extension with differentiated temperature configuration. Two experiments across 20 diverse worldbuilding tasks, using GPT-OSS 120B and DeepSeek v3.2 as LLM backends, demonstrate a 95.0% success rate. The system generated 56-103 self-consistent concepts per world in 18-31 minutes with zero-conflict delivery. The architectural patterns validated here, including layer-as-budget compression, semantic-locality scheduling, and separation of generation and review, transfer to the broader class of knowledge-intensive, multi-agent LLM applications.

</details>


### [89] [How Does Bayesian Causal Discovery Fail? Characterising Structural Consequences in Linear Gaussian Networks under Latent Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09449)
*Debargha Ghosh,Silja Renooij,Anna Kononova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了线性高斯因果模型中，潜在混淆变量在恰好两个观测变量间加性混淆时，后验分布如何响应。推导出评分函数偏好含虚假边图的关键相关阈值，该阈值随样本量增加而降低，并刻画了两种后验失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅指出混淆会破坏可识别性，但未刻画后验分布在混淆条件下的具体行为模式。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 在线性高斯因果模型框架下，聚焦恰好两个观测变量间的加性潜在混淆，推导评分函数偏好的相关阈值，并结合精确后验计算对多种图结构进行验证。

Result: 发现了影响虚假边被偏好的相关阈值，且该阈值随样本量增加而降低；在阈值之上，根据混淆变量局部结构的不同，存在两种截然不同的后验失效模式。

Conclusion: 潜在混淆会导致贝叶斯因果发现的后验分布出现系统性偏差，具体表现为虚假边的偏好增强及两种失效模式，为后续鲁棒性方法设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Bayesian causal discovery is widely used for its ability to quantify epistemic uncertainty over directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) through posterior inference. However, its behaviour under latent confounding remains poorly understood, as existing work typically notes that confounding breaks identifiability without characterising how the posterior distribution over DAGs responds. In this work, we analyse posterior behaviour under latent confounding in linear Gaussian causal models, focusing on additive latent confounding between exactly two observed variables. We derive a critical correlation threshold above which the score function favours graphs with a spurious edge between the confounded variables, and show that this threshold decreases with sample size -- more data lowers the correlation required for the spurious edge to be favoured. Beyond this threshold, we characterize two distinct posterior failure regimes determined by the local structure around the confounded variables. Our findings are supported by exact posterior computations on multiple graph structures, demonstrating both the predicted failure regimes.

</details>


### [90] [ProofCouncil: An LLM Agent for Solving Open Mathematical Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09474)
*Johannes Schmitt,Tim Gehrunger,Jasper Dekoninck,Gergely Bérczi,Uri Kreitner,Liam Price,David Holmes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProofCouncil是一个数学智能体，采用作者-评论家架构解决开放数学问题。在FirstProof挑战的第二批（10个问题）中，有6个提交被评审认为最多只需微小修改即可正确，在参赛团队中表现最佳。在30个来自数学研究者的开放问题中，21个收到反馈，其中5个完全正确，2个有希望，8个包含有用的部分进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在解决开放数学问题方面展现出潜力，但通过针对真实数学实践的智能体工作流可以进一步提升其性能。

Method: 采用作者-评论家(author-critic)架构设计数学智能体ProofCouncil。

Result: 在FirstProof挑战的10个问题中6个提交被评审认为基本正确，表现最佳。在30个开放问题中，21个获得反馈，5个完全正确，2个有希望，8个包含有用部分进展。

Conclusion: ProofCouncil展示了作者-评论家架构在自主解决开放数学问题上的有效性，并开源了构建该智能体的库。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown increasing promise in solving open problems in mathematics. However, their performance can be further improved through agentic workflows tailored to real-world mathematical practice. To this end, we introduce ProofCouncil, a mathematical agent that is designed to tackle open problems using an author-critic architecture. ProofCouncil served as a submission to the second batch of FirstProof, a challenge consisting of 10 real-world mathematical problems that agents must solve autonomously. Its submissions for 6 of the 10 problems were judged by the referees to be correct up to at most minor revisions, showing the best performance among participating teams. We also evaluate ProofCouncil on 30 open problems collected from mathematical researchers. Among the 21 solutions that received human feedback, 5 were judged completely correct, 2 more were judged promising pending final verification, and a further 8 contained useful partial progress. In this short paper, we describe the development of ProofCouncil and the agent-building library used to create it, which we release as open source to the community.

</details>


### [91] [Ceci n'est pas une pipe: AI systems as semantic abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09489)
*Jade Alglave,Patrick Cousot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个语义框架，用于区分AI系统输出中哪些由领域知识证明、哪些来自引用来源、哪些是系统当前可用的，从而精确定义外推、被驳斥断言、来源与知识不匹配等常见故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统的输出并非它所描述的事实或世界状态，而是一种工程化的表征。需要建立一个语义框架来检验这种表征的正确性。

Method: 区分三类内容：被公认领域知识所证明的、引用来源所说的、系统当前可用的。基于此给出常见故障的精确定义。

Result: 给出了外推、被驳斥或未经支持的断言、来源与知识不匹配、过时或被驳斥的来源、添加假设、未经支持的使用等故障的精确定义。

Conclusion: 该框架为规范和检查AI系统提供了有用的词汇，使AI系统的输出、引用、工具调用和改变世界的行为必须由可靠的主张和明确的权威来证明，而非表面上的流畅性。

Abstract: An AI system's output is not the fact or world state it appears to describe, but rather an engineered representation. We propose a semantic framework to describe AI systems, to be able to examine the correctness of such representations. To do so, we distinguish what is justified by accepted domain knowledge, what reference sources say, and what the system can currently use. This allows us to give precise definitions to common failures: extrapolation, refuted or unsupported assertion, sources versus knowledge mismatch, stale or refuted source, added hypotheses, unsupported use... We hope our framework gives a useful vocabulary for specifying and checking AI systems whose outputs, citations, tool calls, and world-changing actions must be justified by reliable claims and explicit authority rather than apparent fluency.

</details>


### [92] [Multimodal Reward Hacking in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09492)
*Jiayu Yao,Yiwei Wang,Anmeng Zhang,Zhe Sun,Songsong Wang,Lingrui Mei,Yuyao Ge,Shenghua Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多模态大语言模型在强化学习对齐中的奖励破解问题，发现基于结果的奖励会导致高达48.1%的破解率，且强化学习会创造新错误而非仅继承旧错误。模型扩展可缓解但无法消除该问题，不同算法（GRPO最鲁棒、RLOO最脆弱）和奖励设计（视觉语义验证有效、关键词检查加剧破解）的影响各异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习越来越多地被用于对齐多模态大语言模型，但更高奖励并不总是意味着更好的任务表现。当视觉证据被纯文本或弱化奖励评估时，这种风险被放大。现有研究缺乏对多模态场景下奖励破解问题的系统性分析。

Method: 在安全VQA、图表VQA和压力测试三种场景下，对比不同奖励设计、数据歧义性、模型规模（2B-32B）以及RL算法（GRPO, RLOO, DAPO）对奖励破解的影响。引入新指标NRFR（新奖励失败率）衡量在代理奖励提升的样本中实际失败的比例。

Result: 基于结果的奖励导致高达48.1%的奖励破解率（RHR）；NRFR超过RHR表明RL创造了新失败而非仅继承旧失败；模型扩展可降低但无法消除破解，32B模型在结果奖励下仍有54.9%的更差率；GRPO最鲁棒、RLOO最脆弱、DAPO从2B到8B显著提升；关键词视觉检查增加破解，VLM作为判断的语义验证可减少破解。

Conclusion: 多模态奖励破解是优化不完美奖励的系统性结果，鲁棒对齐需要能够在优化压力下保持可靠的奖励和验证器。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly used to align multimodal large language models (MLLMs), but higher rewards do not always imply better task performance. This risk is amplified when visual evidence is evaluated by text-only or weakly grounded rewards. We study reward hacking in MLLM RL across safety VQA, chart VQA, and stress-test settings, varying reward design, data ambiguity, model scale (2B-32B), and RL algorithm (GRPO, RLOO, DAPO). We introduce Newly Rewarded Failure Rate (NRFR), which measures failures among samples whose proxy reward improves over the SFT baseline. Outcome-only rewards cause severe hacking, reaching 48.1% Reward Hacking Rate (RHR), while NRFR exceeding RHR shows that RL creates new failures rather than merely inheriting them. Scaling reduces but does not eliminate hacking: even the 32B model retains a 54.9% worse rate under outcome-only rewards, whereas answer-aware rewards improve the oracle trend at every scale. Robustness is also algorithm- and scale-dependent: GRPO is consistently most resistant, RLOO remains vulnerable, and DAPO improves substantially from 2B to 8B. Visual-evidence rewards help only with reliable verification: keyword-based checks increase hacking, while VLM-as-judge semantic verification reduces it. Overall, multimodal reward hacking is a systematic result of optimizing imperfect rewards, and robust alignment requires rewards and verifiers that remain reliable under optimization pressure.

</details>


### [93] [Shared Selective Persistent Memory for Agentic LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09493)
*Sanjana Pedada,Aditya Dhavala,Neelraj Patil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出共享选择性持久记忆（Shared Selective Persistent Memory）架构，通过保留四类可复用上下文（任务规范、数据模式、工具配置、输出约束）并丢弃会话特定推理轨迹，结合零token数据刷新机制，在协作工作空间中实现高效的多轮代码生成。在三类企业场景中任务完成率达96%，相比无记忆（79%）和全历史记忆（71%）显著提升；零token刷新减少14倍任务时间，摘要驱动生成降低97倍token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic LLM系统在多轮工具使用中面临上下文断裂问题：每次会话从零开始，丢失之前的配置选择、领域约束、数据模式和使用模式。简单持久化完整对话历史既token低效又会降低生成质量，需要一种选择性保留可复用上下文的机制。

Method: 提出共享选择性持久记忆架构，识别并保留四类可复用上下文（任务规范、数据模式、工具配置、输出约束），丢弃会话特定推理轨迹。记忆可在用户间共享（基于角色的访问控制）。配套零token数据刷新机制，将生成程序与运行时数据解耦。在部署的协作工作空间平台中实现，支持多源（CSV、SQL、REST API、MCP）的git版本化产物（仪表盘、报告、数据驱动文档）。

Result: 三类企业场景：选择性记忆96%任务完成率 vs 无记忆79% vs 全历史71%。零token刷新减少14倍任务时间，摘要驱动生成降低97倍token成本。四个公开数据集复现验证泛化性，零token刷新12/12成功。全历史持久化因陈旧痕迹偏置反而降低完成率。

Conclusion: 选择性保留可复用上下文、丢弃会话特定推理轨迹是比无记忆和全历史记忆都更优的策略；跨用户共享记忆和零token刷新机制有效支持协作和持续复用。

Abstract: Agentic LLM systems that generate code through multi-turn tool use face a fundamental context problem: each session starts from zero, discarding the configuration choices, domain constraints, data schemas, and tool-use patterns that made previous sessions productive. Naively persisting entire conversation histories is token-inefficient and counterproductive: irrelevant context degrades generation quality. We introduce shared selective persistent memory, an architecture that identifies and retains four categories of reusable context (task specifications, data schemas, tool configurations, and output constraints) while discarding session-specific reasoning traces. Crucially, this memory is shared: workspaces encapsulating selective memory can be transferred across users with role-based access control, enabling collaborative reuse without redundant specification. We implement it in a deployed collaborative workspace platform where LLM agents produce, edit, and maintain git-versioned artifacts (dashboards, reports, and data-driven documents) from heterogeneous sources (CSV, SQL, REST APIs, and MCP servers). A complementary zero-token data refresh mechanism decouples generated programs from runtime data, enabling artifact reuse without re-invocation. Across three enterprise scenarios, shared selective persistent memory achieves 96% task completion (vs. 79% without memory and 71% with full history). Zero-token refresh eliminates LLM re-invocation for recurring updates (14x task-time reduction), while summary-driven generation cuts per-invocation token cost by 97x versus raw data injection. A replication on four public datasets confirms generalizability, with zero-token refresh succeeding in 12/12 trials. Notably, naive full-history persistence actively degrades completion by biasing the agent with stale traces, while selective memory outperforms both extremes.

</details>


### [94] [SAGEAgent: A Self-Evolving Agent for Cost-Aware Modality Acquisition in Multimodal Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09521)
*Chongyu Qu,Can Cui,Zhengyi Lu,Junchao Zhu,Tianyuan Yao,Junlin Guo,Juming Xiong,Yanfan Zhu,Yuechen Yang,Bennett A. Landman,Yuankai Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SAGEAgent，一种自进化的LLM临床代理，通过顺序决策决定每位癌症患者需要哪些诊断手段，在保证生存预测精度的同时将平均诊断负担降低55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态生存预测方法要么假设所有模态可用，要么被动处理缺失数据，无法主动判断对特定患者获取下一模态是否合理。临床实践中诊断手段有明确的顺序性和递增负担，需要一种能主动推理并平衡预测精度与临床侵入性的方法。

Method: 将问题建模为顺序决策问题，提出SAGEAgent框架：1) 利用LLM作为核心推理引擎；2) 临床工具将数值预测转为文本；3) 情景记忆检索相似历史病例；4) 语义记忆从经验中积累可复用的决策模式；5) 代理根据患者当前诊断状态决定是否获取下一模态。

Result: 在整合TCGA-LGG、TCGA-GBM和BraTS的胶质瘤队列上，使用四种诊断模态，SAGEAgent在保持竞争性生存预测精度的同时，将平均诊断获取负担降低了55%。

Conclusion: 针对每位患者个性化地选择必要的诊断模态是可行的，SAGEAgent能够有效平衡预测精度与临床侵入性，大幅减少不必要的诊断检查。

Abstract: Does every cancer patient truly need a complete diagnostic workup for accurate survival prediction? In multimodal clinical oncology, diagnostic modalities follow a clinically mandated order of escalating burden -- from demographics collected at intake to genomic profiling requiring specialized tissue analysis. Current multimodal survival methods either assume all modalities are available or passively handle missing data, but none actively reason about whether acquiring the next modality is justified for a given patient along this ordered workflow. We formulate this as a sequential decision problem and propose SAGEAgent (Sequential Acquisition Guided by Experience), a self-evolving LLM-based clinical agent that decides which diagnostic modalities to acquire for each patient, balancing predictive accuracy against clinical invasiveness. SAGEAgent reasons about each patient's evolving diagnostic state through clinical tools that translate numerical predictions into text, an episodic memory that retrieves similar past cases, and a semantic memory that accumulates reusable decision patterns from experience. Experiments on a glioma cohort combining TCGA-LGG, TCGA-GBM, and BraTS with four diagnostic modalities demonstrate that SAGEAgent achieves competitive survival prediction accuracy while reducing average acquisition burden by 55%.

</details>


### [95] [Beyond Fixed Representations: The Vocabulary and Verifier Gaps in Open-Ended AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09560)
*Yuan Cao,Haiqian Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有AI系统在固定表征框架内操作，缺乏创造、稳定和重用新表征原语的能力。通过"词汇鸿沟"和"验证鸿沟"两个缺口刻画了当前AI与真正开放式智能之间的差距，并提出了一套区分框架内变换与生成性变换的理论，以及创新自主性阶梯和推进开放式AI的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统（如推理、编码、定理证明、工具使用等）虽具备强大能力，但存在结构性局限：它们在一个固定的表征框架内运行，概念词汇、可搜索解空间和成功评判标准都是预先给定的。作者认为，要实现真正的开放式创新，AI需要能够创建、稳定和重用新的表征原语，从而改变被搜索的空间本身。

Method: 通过定义两个关键缺口（词汇鸿沟和验证鸿沟）来刻画问题，并基于"智能即认知差异减少"的统一框架，区分了两种认知变换类型：框架内变换（intra-space transformations）和生成性变换（generative transformations）。在此基础上提出创新自主性阶梯（ladder of innovation autonomy）。

Result: 识别出两个核心障碍：1）词汇鸿沟——发明和稳定新表征原语而非简单重组现有原语的困难；2）验证鸿沟——判断新原语价值时面临未来复用才能显现全部效用的困难。提出了一个从框架内操作到框架修改的创新自主性层级划分。

Conclusion: 推进开放式AI需要：奖励有用表征变化的目标函数、用于存储发明原语的持久记忆架构，以及能够与所评估的表征共同进化的自适应验证机制。

Abstract: Modern AI systems are increasingly being evaluated for their ability to reason, code, prove theorems, use tools, and long-horizon research tasks. These are powerful capabilities, but they share a structural limitation: the representational frame within which the model operates, including its conceptual vocabulary, the space of admissible solutions it can search, and the criteria by which success is evaluated, is typically fixed and supplied in advance. This paper argues that building stronger intelligent systems capable of open-ended innovation requires additional classes of operations: the creation, stabilization, and reuse of new representational primitives, which alter the space being searched rather than simply searching within it.
  We characterize the distance between current AI systems and genuinely open-ended intelligence through two gaps. The first is the vocabulary gap, the difficulty of inventing and stabilizing new representational primitives rather than merely recombining existing ones. The second is the verifier gap, the difficulty of judging the value of a new primitive when its full payoff may be visible only after future reuse. We interpret both gaps through a unified framework of intelligence as cognitive discrepancy reduction. By viewing intelligent behaviors as a sequence of cognitive transformations, we distinguish intra-space transformations which operate within a fixed representational frame, from generative transformations which may modify the frame itself. On this basis, we propose a ladder of innovation autonomy and outline several directions for advancing open-ended AI, including objectives that reward useful representational change, persistent memory architectures for invented primitives, and adaptive verification mechanisms capable of evolving alongside the representations they evaluate.

</details>


### [96] [Knowledge Graphs and Explainable AI as Complementary Resources for Urban Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09578)
*Jan Gronewald,Andreas Emrich,Nijat Mehdiyev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于互补性理论的四种KG-XAI集成模式（提升、约束、类型化、修正），用于增强建筑拆前评估中决策的可辩护性，并通过消防门案例展示了W3C链接建筑数据栈的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献对知识图谱与可解释AI的集成描述丰富但缺乏结构性解释，无法阐明为何特定集成能产生单独资源无法提供的产物，亟需一个结构化的理论框架。

Method: 基于IS资源基础观的互补性理论，提出四种KG-XAI集成模式（Lifting, Constraining, Typing, Revising），每一种模式被定义为对XAI产物和知识图谱底层结构的类型化操作。

Result: 每种集成模式解锁可辩护性的不同属性（可读性、合理性、溯源性和可争议性），并通过城市采矿中的消防门案例展示了W3C链接建筑数据栈的具体应用。

Conclusion: 在建筑拆前评估这一受监管的审计过程中，AI支持的价值核心不是预测精度，而是决策的可辩护性；提出的四种集成模式为构建符合监管要求的可辩护决策支持系统提供了结构化路径。

Abstract: Pre-demolition assessment, the regulated audit process at the heart of urban mining, is an information process in which AI support must serve qualified auditors who remain accountable for the decisions taken. The relevant unit of value is not prediction accuracy alone, but the defensibility of the supported decisions: their legibility, plausibility, sourcing, and contestability. Explainable AI techniques and domain knowledge graphs each address parts of this requirement, and existing taxonomies have catalogued their integration. The literature is descriptively rich but structurally under-specified: what remains less developed is a structural account of why specific integrations produce artefacts neither resource can provide alone. This paper offers a complementarity-theoretic interpretation grounded in the IS resource-based tradition. We propose four consolidated KG-XAI integration modes (Lifting, Constraining, Typing, and Revising), each defined as a typed operation over XAI artefacts and knowledge-graph substrate structures. Each mode unlocks a distinct property of defensibility and contributes to the kind of regulatory artefact pre-demolition assessment demands. A fire-door example from the urban-mining process illustrates the modes using the W3C Linked Building Data stack and valuation extensions.

</details>


### [97] [TrustX Agent Risk Classification Framework (ARC): Risk-Tiering Internally Created Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09586)
*Hannah M. Liu,Rhea Saxena,Shiv Asthana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了TrustX Agent Risk Classification (ARC)框架，这是一个针对七类代理型AI系统的结构化风险评估工具，包含12维评分体系、GPA+IAT分类模型和五级自治框架，最终输出三级治理建议及控制措施，并包含编码助手扩展模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理型AI系统在企业和公共领域的快速部署已超越现有通用AI风险框架的分类与治理能力，亟需一个可重复、结构化的专门化风险评估工具。

Method: 基于已有AI治理框架，构建了包含12维度评分规则的评估体系，结合GPA+IAT分类模型和五级自治等级框架，最终输出三级治理输出与映射控制建议，并针对代码助手类系统设计了专门扩展。

Result: 提出了一个可应用于七类代理型AI系统的结构化风险评估框架，并通过示例展示了其实际应用方式。

Conclusion: ARC框架面向AI治理从业者、风险官员、开发者和监管机构，将定期迭代以提升鲁棒性，社区可通过公开链接访问交互式框架。

Abstract: The proliferation of agentic AI systems across enterprise and public-sector contexts has outpaced the capacity of general-purpose AI risk frameworks to classify and govern them. In this paper, we introduce the TrustX Agent Risk Classification Framework, a structured, repeatable instrument that can be applied to seven types of agentic AI systems and is grounded in foundational pre-existing AI governance frameworks. At the core of the framework is a twelve-dimension scoring rubric that robustly quantifies the risk. This rubric is combined with other components, such as the GPA + IAT classification model and the five-level autonomy framework derived from existing literature. These inputs produce a three-tier governance output with mapped control recommendations. A specialised Coding Assistant extension is also included to account for nuances specific to this type of agentic AI system. We then use an illustrative example to show our framework in practice. ARC is intended for AI governance practitioners, risk officers, developers, and regulators, and it will regularly undergo iteration as we continue to expand it and make it more robust. The community can access the interactive framework here: https://arc.responsible.ai/

</details>


### [98] [Agora: Enhancing LLM Agent Reasoning Via Auction-Based Task Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09600)
*Kaiji Zhou,Ales Leonardis,Yue Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Agora框架，通过激励兼容的拍卖机制动态分配任务给专家模型和工具，将推理步骤作为可交易物品，基于修正能力进行竞标，在五个基准测试中优于现有方法，并实现可控的成本-质量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架通常基于任务与专家模型功能的粗粒度匹配来调用API，忽视了功能相似替代方案之间的性能差异和成本效率问题，导致关键逻辑可能被路由到过度自信而非真正有能力的求解器。

Method: 提出Agora框架，引入激励兼容的拍卖机制，将推理步骤视为可交易物品，专家模型和工具基于修正能力（rectified competence）进行竞标，确保关键逻辑路由到最合适的求解器，通过单一拍卖参数控制成本-质量权衡。

Result: 在五个基准测试上，Agora在可比候选池中优于匹配的单模型、路由和级联基线，同时通过单一拍卖参数实现了可控的成本-质量权衡。

Conclusion: 基于拍卖的动态任务分配机制能有效提升LLM智能体的推理能力，在保证性能的同时优化成本效率，为专家模型和工具的编排提供了新范式。

Abstract: Enhancing the reasoning capabilities of large language model (LLM) agents requires effective orchestration of diverse expert models and tools. However, existing frameworks typically call APIs based on coarse-grained matching between tasks and the functions of expert models or tools, while overlooking critical factors such as performance variability and cost efficiency among functionally similar alternatives. To address this, we propose Agora, a framework that introduces an incentive-compatible auction mechanism for dynamically allocating tasks to expert models and tools. By treating reasoning steps as tradeable items, Agora enables agents to bid based on their rectified competence-ensuring that critical logic is routed to the most capable solver rather than the most overconfident one. Evaluations across five benchmarks show that Agora improves over matched single-model, routing, and cascade baselines under comparable candidate pools, while exposing a controllable cost-quality trade-off through a single auction parameter.

</details>


### [99] [ConceptSMILE: Auditing the Trustworthiness of Concept-Based Explainable AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09649)
*Mohadeseh Mollapour,Koorosh Aslansefat,Zeinab Dehghani,Bhupesh Kumar Mishra,Tejal Shah,Zhibao Mian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ConceptSMILE，一个模型无关的、基于扰动的审计框架，用于评估概念可解释人工智能的可信度。通过在视网膜眼底图像上比较MedSAM视觉概念与VLM语义概念，发现概念可靠性因路径而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于概念的可解释AI虽然能提升模型推理的可理解性，但概念层面的输出并非自动可信，缺乏独立的评估机制来审计概念解释的可靠性。

Method: ConceptSMILE框架通过扰动输入区域、测量概念响应偏移、应用局部加权，并拟合XGBoost代理模型来近似局部概念行为。从归因准确性、代理保真度、忠实性、稳定性和一致性五个维度评估可靠性。

Result: MedSAM路径在空间归因和代理保真度上表现最优（R²=0.8503, R_w²=0.8465）；VLM路径在血管忠实性和特定伪影条件下的稳定性更强。概念可靠性在不同概念和路径间存在差异。

Conclusion: ConceptSMILE为评估基于概念的可解释AI的可信度提供了独立的审计层。

Abstract: Concept-based explainable artificial intelligence (AI) can make model reasoning more human-understandable, but concept-level outputs are not automatically trustworthy. We introduce ConceptSMILE, a model-agnostic perturbation-based auditing framework for evaluating the reliability of concept-based explanations. Rather than replacing SMILE, ConceptSMILE extends its perturbation-based logic from feature- or region-level attribution to the auditing of human-understandable concept explanations. The framework perturbs input regions, measures concept-response shifts, applies locality weighting, and fits an XGBoost surrogate to approximate local concept behaviour. Reliability is assessed through attribution accuracy, surrogate fidelity, faithfulness, stability, and consistency. We evaluate ConceptSMILE on retinal fundus images by comparing MedSAM-derived visual concepts with VLM-based semantic concepts. Results show that reliability varies across concepts and pathways: MedSAM achieves stronger spatial attribution and the highest surrogate fidelity ($R^2 = 0.8503$, $R_w^2 = 0.8465$), while the VLM pathway shows stronger vessel faithfulness and stronger stability under selected artefact conditions. ConceptSMILE provides an independent audit layer for evaluating the trustworthiness of concept-based XAI.

</details>


### [100] [SolarChain-Eval: A Physics-Constrained Benchmark for Trustworthy Economic Agents in Decentralized Energy Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08681)
*Shilin Ou,Yifan Xu,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SolarChain-Eval，一个物理约束的基准测试，用于评估去中心化能源市场中可信赖的经济智能体，实验揭示了效用-安全性权衡，并表明可信赖的评估需要物理约束和透明的干预记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI系统在物理环境中评估任务性能和可信赖性的需求；去中心化能源市场中智能体可能利用无效物理数据、制造人造流动性和产生不稳定的治理决策。

Method: 将市场治理建模为Gymnasium兼容的MDP；从市场效用、物理安全、滑点、动作平滑性、空间公平性和可审计性多维度评估；集成LLM驱动的规划器/审计器层；记录干预日志。

Result: 静态、随机、近视、RL和RL+LLM策略的实验显示效用-安全性权衡；RL智能体提升市场效用但产生不安全行为；去除物理惩罚时奖励最大化智能体会利用无效生成并增加人造流动性；LLM规划器/审计器提升可审计性但无法完全补偿错误指定的奖励函数。

Conclusion: 可信赖的智能体AI评估需要物理约束和透明的干预记录；数据和代码开源以促进可复现性。

Abstract: As agentic AI systems are increasingly applied to cyber-physical environments, their evaluation requires assessment of both task performance and trustworthiness. In decentralized energy markets, autonomous agents may improve market utility, but may also exploit invalid physical data, create artificial liquidity, and produce unstable governance decisions. Therefore, we propose SolarChain-Eval, a physics-constrained benchmark for evaluating trustworthy economic agents. It formulates market governance as a Gymnasium-compatible Markov Decision Process, where agents make hourly decisions. SolarChain-Eval evaluates each policy across multiple dimensions, including market utility, physical safety, slippage, action smoothness, spatial fairness, and auditability. To support agentic evaluation, SolarChain-Eval incorporates an LLM-based Planner/Auditor layer. The Planner defines episode-level action bounds and audit rules, while the Auditor reviews and revises high-risk actions. All interventions are recorded through structured logs, including trigger signals, proposed actions, revised actions, and audit rationales. Experiments with static, random, myopic, RL, and RL+LLM policies reveal a clear utility-safety trade-off. RL agents improve market utility but can still produce unsafe behavior. When the physics penalty is removed, reward-maximizing agents exploit invalid generation and increase artificial liquidity. The LLM Planner/Auditor improves auditability and mitigates selected risks, but it cannot fully compensate for a misspecified reward function. These results indicate that trustworthy agentic AI evaluation requires both physical constraints and transparent intervention traces. We release data and code as open access on GitHub for replicability.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [Programmers Are Poor and Overconfident Judges of LLM-Generated Assertions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08885)
*Zhanna Kaufman,Yuriy Brun,Adithya Murali,Madeline Endres*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过实验发现，程序员判断正确断言准确率74%，判断错误断言仅49%；自然语言解释无益，低质量解释反而降低判断准确性（p=0.037）并增加过度自信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代码生成工具日益普及，代码理解和审查的重要性持续上升，但AI辅助（如断言和自然语言解释）对开发者评估代码行为的效果尚不明确。

Method: 对86名Python程序员进行受控实验，并开展后续出声思维研究，考察开发者判断不同质量断言的正确性与完整性，以及自然语言解释如何影响这些判断。

Result: 正确断言判断准确率74%，错误断言仅49%（OR=2.94，p<0.001）；自然语言解释整体无益；低质量解释降低规范评估准确性（OR=0.58，p=0.037）同时增加开发者信心（3.99/5→4.25/5，p=0.005）。

Conclusion: AI辅助可能不会提高代码理解和审查的可靠性；除生成可靠的人工制品外，帮助开发者评估机器生成的可信度人工制品同样重要。

Abstract: Code comprehension and code review are already critically important software engineering tasks, and the rising use of AI code generation tools is only increasing that importance. Generative AI has the possibility of supporting these activities, for example by augmenting code with assertions and natural-language explanations describing code behavior. However, little is known about how effective such support may be. We conduct a controlled experiment with 86 Python programmers and a follow-up think-aloud study to examine developers' ability to assess the correctness and completeness of generated assertions of varying quality, and to investigate how natural-language explanations influence these assessments. While programmers can somewhat accurately judge correct assertions (74% accuracy), they perform poorly when shown incorrect assertions (49% accuracy), despite reporting similar levels of confidence in both judgments. This difference in judgment accuracy is statistically significant (p < 0.001): the odds of a developer accurately judging a correct assertion was nearly three times higher than the odds of accurately judging an incorrect assertion (OR = 2.94). Surprisingly, natural-language explanations of assertions provided no overall benefit. Furthermore, low-quality explanations could impair specification assessment accuracy (p = 0.037, OR = 0.58) while simultaneously increasing developer confidence (p = 0.005, 3.99/5 vs. 4.25/5). Our findings suggest that, contrary to common assumptions, AI assistance may not improve the reliability of code comprehension and review. More broadly, our findings highlight the importance of helping developers evaluate machine-generated reliability artifacts, in addition to generating them.

</details>


### [102] [Better Harnesses, Smaller Models: Building 90% Cheaper Agents via Automated Harness Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08938)
*Chenyang Yang,Xinran Zhao,Tongshuang Wu,Christian Kästner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过元代理自动发现的适配框架（定制指令、工具和编排循环）能使小语言模型（SLM）代理在常规商业任务中达到与大型语言模型（LLM）代理相近的性能，同时降低90%的推理成本。在7个商业任务和3个SLM系列上，优化框架显著改善了16/21的任务-SLM组合，最佳SLM代理以4%的成本恢复了LLM 89.7%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM代理推理成本高昂，难以大规模部署；直接替换为SLM会导致性能大幅下降。本文旨在探索能否通过适配代理框架（而非替换模型本身）来弥合SLM与LLM之间的性能差距，实现低成本、高性能的SLM代理部署。

Method: 1. 构建任务难度归因框架，将代理故障模式映射到框架适配策略（定制指令、工具、编排循环）。2. 设计框架优化器（元代理），利用故障轨迹自动发现有效的适配方案。3. 在7个商业任务和3个SLM系列上进行系统实验验证。

Result: 优化框架在16/21的任务-SLM组合上显著提升了SLM性能，其中7个组合完全弥合了SLM-LLM性能差距。最佳SLM代理（配合优化框架）以LLM 4%的成本恢复了89.7%的性能。适配效果在工作流更重复的任务和基础能力足够的SLM上更佳。

Conclusion: 框架适配是一种有效且自动化的途径，能够显著扩展SLM代理在常规商业任务中的实际部署范围，实现低成本高性能的代理应用，为SLM在实际业务场景中的大规模落地提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Frontier LLM agents are automating many business tasks, but their high inference cost makes large-scale deployment unsustainable. Small language models (SLMs) offer a cheaper alternative, yet they typically fall short when swapped into a harness designed for a frontier LLM. We show that for many routine business tasks, SLM agents can match LLM performance at 90% lower cost, when paired with an adapted harness that can be automatically discovered by a meta agent. The key insight is that much of the task difficulty is shared across instances and can be lifted from the model into the harness via tailored instructions, tools, and orchestration loops. To study this systematically, we create a framework that maps agent failure modes to harness adaptation strategies, and build a harness optimizer that automatically discovers effective adaptations from failure trajectories. Across seven business-oriented agentic tasks and three SLM families, we found optimized harnesses significantly improve performance on 16 of 21 task-SLM pairs, with seven pairs closing the SLM-LLM performance gap and the best SLM agent recovering 89.7% of LLM performance at 4% of the cost. Our analysis further shows that adaptation works best for tasks with more repetitive workflows and for SLMs with sufficient base capabilities. Together, these results suggest that harness adaptation can expand the practical deployment range of SLM agents in routine business tasks.

</details>


### [103] [Toward Inferring Accurate Context-free Grammars for Big Languages in a Black-box Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08959)
*Mohammad Rifat Arefin,Nuhiat Arefin,Shanto Rahman,Christoph Csallner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Xvada是一种用于黑盒上下文无关文法推断的新方法，在准确性、紧凑性和可扩展性上优于现有方法（如Arvada、TreeVada等），并成功发现了Python Liquid引擎中的CVE及多个漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒上下文无关文法推断方法（如Arvada、TreeVada、Kedavra、Cucio）在处理较大语言时存在可扩展性差、准确性低、文法可读性差等问题，亟需更优的方案。

Method: Xvada引入了多种新技术，用于确定性推断上下文无关文法，并通过避免近期研究中常见陷阱的实证比较进行评估。

Result: 在准确性和紧凑性上均优于最高分的竞品TreeVada；发现了Python Liquid引擎中的一个CVE，并基于Xvada推断的文法进行模糊测试，额外发现5个漏洞（已被开发者修复）。

Conclusion: Xvada在黑盒上下文无关文法推断任务上取得了显著进展，并在实际软件安全中展现了价值，工具和实验数据已开源。

Abstract: Black-box context-free grammar inference is crucial for program analysis, reverse engineering, program understanding, fuzzing, and security. But existing approaches such as Arvada, TreeVada, Kedavra, and Cucio struggle with scalability, accuracy, and grammar readability, especially on larger languages. To address this challenge, Xvada introduces several new techniques for deterministic inference of context-free grammars. In an empirical comparison that avoids several pitfalls of recent studies, Xvada improves on the highest-scoring competitor (TreeVada) both in grammar accuracy and grammar compactness. Xvada also found a CVE in the widely used Python Liquid engine. Fuzzing based on the XVada-inferred grammar found five more bugs, which the Python Liquid developers fixed based on our bug reports. XVADA and all experimental data and scripts are freely available.

</details>


### [104] [The Patchwork Problem in LLM-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08981)
*Viraaji Mothukuri,Reza M. Parizi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLM生成的代码在局部看似正确，但部署后常因结构不一致（如引用未声明的配置、导入不存在的包、遗漏认证守卫等）而失败。本文形式化定义了"拼凑问题"，提出基于图表示的结构一致性验证框架，并揭示了现有CI工具链难以检测此类缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码虽能通过编译和测试，但部署后频繁因结构性问题失败，而现有CI工具链（类型检查、测试、SAST）无法有效检测这类缺陷。随着LLM编码工具广泛采用，这一盲点对软件质量构成日益严重的风险。

Method: 将结构一致性形式化为仓库工件的图表示（导入图、调用图、依赖图、配置图、模式图、资源图、控制流图、路由图）上的一致性不变量，提出八类故障分类法，并构建混合验证框架：对已有成熟工具的分析任务委派给现有工具，对跨领域不变量部署专用检测器，以可证明的约束违反为目标而非启发式模式匹配。

Result: 在两个前沿模型、四种提示策略下的实验表明，绝大多数结构故障完全逃逸了类型检查、测试和SAST检测；不同模型间的故障模式存在定性差异，挑战了模型无关的缓解策略。外部验证确认这些故障并非受控实验的产物，而是LLM在极少人工监督下编写代码时的普遍现象。

Conclusion: LLM生成代码的结构不一致问题是普遍且严重的，现有工具链存在系统性盲点。本文提出的框架和分类法为检测这类缺陷提供了有效途径，但不同模型间的故障模式差异要求更针对性的缓解策略。

Abstract: LLM-generated code often compiles, passes tests, and appears correct, yet breaks once deployed. The root cause is frequently structural rather than logical. A generated endpoint references configuration keys never declared in the project, an import targets a package that does not exist in any registry, or a new route omits the authentication guard applied to every sibling endpoint. Each patch is locally valid but globally incoherent, and standard CI toolchains rarely surface these failures. As LLM-powered coding tools see widespread adoption, this blind spot poses a growing risk to software quality. We call this the \textbf{patchwork problem}. This paper formalizes structural coherence as consistency invariants over graph representations of repository artifacts, including import, call, dependency, configuration, schema, resource, control-flow, and routing graphs, and introduces an eight-category failure taxonomy distinguishing defects specific to LLM generation from those merely amplified by it. We present a hybrid verification framework that delegates to mature static analysis tools where they already excel and deploys purpose-built detectors for cross-cutting invariants underserved by existing toolchains, targeting provable constraint violations rather than heuristic pattern matching. Empirical evaluation across two frontier models under four prompting strategies reveals that the vast majority of structural failures evade type checking, testing, and SAST entirely, and that failure patterns diverge qualitatively between models in ways that challenge model-agnostic mitigation strategies. External validation on real-world AI-generated repositories confirms that these failures are not artifacts of controlled experimentation but are prevalent wherever LLMs write code with minimal human oversight.

</details>


### [105] [SCATE: Learning to Supervise Coding Agents for Cost-Effective Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08983)
*Sijia Gu,Noor Nashid,Ali Mesbah*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SCATE框架，通过将自动化测试生成的监督形式化为上下文赌博机问题，自适应地指导编码智能体测试行为，替代人工干预，显著提升代码覆盖率和测试效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主编码智能体在自动化测试生成中存在"惰性生成"问题——过早终止任务、系统性地回避复杂程序逻辑，导致代码覆盖率不足。当前缓解该问题需要持续的人工监督，这成为效率瓶颈，抵消了自动化生成的优势。

Method: SCATE将监督过程形式化为上下文赌博机问题，基于当前覆盖率和类可测试性指标，学习选择最优的测试动作，在最大化覆盖率增益的同时最小化无效生成开销。该框架可无缝集成不同编码智能体。

Result: 集成GEMINI-CLI后，行覆盖率和分支覆盖率分别比智能体基线提升32.3%和30.9%。与CLAUDE CODE对比验证了框架能动态调整策略以优化每个智能体的独特优势。在所有指标上持续优于最先进的非智能体方法。

Conclusion: SCATE通过自适应自动化监督有效解决了编码智能体的惰性生成问题，消除了对人工干预的依赖，具有与不同智能体的良好兼容性和一致的性能优势。

Abstract: While autonomous coding agents have significantly advanced automated test generation, they remain fundamentally limited by lazy generation, a phenomenon where agents prematurely terminate tasks and systematically avoid complex programmatic logic, resulting in inadequate code coverage. Currently, mitigating this premature termination requires continuous human-in-the-loop supervision. This heavy reliance on human intuition creates a bottleneck that negates the efficiency gains of automated generation. We propose SCATE, a framework for adaptive, automated supervision of coding agents that replaces human intervention during test generation. By formulating supervision as a contextual bandit problem, SCATE learns to select the most promising testing actions based on the current coverage and class testability metrics, maximizing coverage gains while minimizing wasted generation effort. Our empirical evaluation demonstrates that SCATE integrates seamlessly with different coding agents. When applied to GEMINI-CLI, it achieves 32.3% higher line coverage and 30.9% higher branch coverage than the agent-only baseline. A comparison with CLAUDE CODE confirms the framework dynamically adapts its policy to optimize each agent's unique strengths. SCATE also consistently outperforms state-of-the-art non-agentic approaches across all metrics.

</details>


### [106] [Loop-Based Slicing and Input-Driven Concretization: An Empirical Study of Termination and Non-Termination Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08988)
*Negar Fathi,Rahul Purandare,Tachio Terauchi,Hiroshi Unno*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出FocusTNT，一种轻量级、工具无关的C语言源码预处理方法（循环切片+输入具体化），在6个分析器上对117个真实C/C++非终止程序进行实证研究，发现预处理并非普遍有益，其对分析器、任务和程序特征敏感，应作为原始程序分析的补充手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实C程序中循环交互和非确定性输入给现有终止性分析器带来挑战，需探索轻量级预处理方法提升分析效果。

Method: 实现FocusTNT，采用基于循环的切片隔离循环级义务，以及输入驱动具体化将非确定性输入特化为选定输入场景变体；在6个分析器上对117个程序进行系统评估。

Result: 预处理效果因分析器、任务和程序特征而异；切片提供保守的结构隔离与定位；具体化可提升选定场景的可检测性但缩小语义范围；两者组合并非始终叠加增益。

Conclusion: 预处理应作为原始程序分析的互补手段自适应使用，为应用开发者理解验证结果和工具开发者提升分析器鲁棒性提供实践指导。

Abstract: Termination and non-termination are fundamental correctness properties, but verifying them in real-world C programs remains difficult because loop interactions and nondeterministic inputs challenge existing analyzers. This paper presents an empirical study of lightweight, tool-independent source-level preprocessing for (non-)termination analysis. We implement FocusTNT, a C front end that applies loop-based slicing to isolate loop-level obligations and input-driven concretization to specialize nondeterministic inputs into selected input-scenario variants. We evaluate slicing, concretization, and their combination across six analyzers on 117 C/C++ programs derived from real-world non-termination bugs and their fixes. The study examines effects on analyzer correctness, complementarity with original-program analysis, loop-level diagnostics, feature sensitivity, runtime behavior, semantic scope, and integration potential. Results show that preprocessing is not uniformly beneficial: its impact depends on the analyzer, task, and program features. Slicing provides conservative structural isolation and localization, whereas concretization can improve detectability for selected scenarios but narrows semantic scope and may increase analysis effort. Their combination is not consistently additive. Overall, the results support adaptive use of preprocessing as a complement to original-program analysis and provide practical guidance to application developers interpreting verification outcomes and tool developers improving analyzer robustness.

</details>


### [107] [From Generic to Personalized: Exploring Persona-Aware Code Review Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08990)
*Shamse Tasnim Cynthia,Ratnadira Widyasari,Banani Roy,Italo Santos,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探索了人格化代码评审解释（personified code review explanations）的潜力，通过混合方法用户研究发现开发者对风格化评审评论的偏好因问题解决风格、经验水平和角色而异，并提出面向包容性、人本中心的AI辅助代码评审系统愿景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码评审是确保软件质量和促进协作的关键环节，但已有研究表明开发者对代码评审评论的理解存在差异，这种差异阻碍了有效的沟通协作。因此，本文旨在探索人格化代码评审解释以解决这一沟通挑战。

Method: 采用混合方法的用户研究（ongoing mixed-methods user study），让开发者评估多段代码片段上的个性化（persona-aligned）评审评论，收集偏好数据进行分析。

Result: 开发者对解释风格的偏好因问题解决风格、经验水平和角色而异。所有问题解决风格档案的开发者均更重视解释深度、学习支持、实用建议和风险意识，而非简洁性；这表明需要在个性化与清晰度、可信度之间取得平衡。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，本文提出了一个面向包容性、人本中心的AI辅助代码评审系统的愿景，该系统能够根据开发者的问题解决偏好自适应地调整反馈。

Abstract: Code review is essential for ensuring software quality and supporting collaboration, yet prior work shows that developers can interpret code review comments differently. These differences can hinder effective communication, particularly in collaborative settings. To address this challenge, we explore the potential of personified code review explanations. We report initial findings from an ongoing mixed-methods user study in which developers evaluated persona-aligned review comments across multiple code snippets. Our results suggest that preferences for explanation styles vary across problem-solving styles, experience levels, and roles. Across problem-solving style profiles, developers valued explanatory depth, learning support, practical suggestions, and risk awareness over conciseness, highlighting the need to balance personalization with clarity and trust. Based on these findings, we outline a vision for inclusive, human-centered AI-assisted code review systems that adapt feedback to developers' problem-solving preferences.

</details>


### [108] [Shadow-Based Noise Fingerprinting of Simulated Quantum Noise Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08998)
*Vridhi Jain,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的噪声指纹识别流程，结合结构化经典阴影层析成像和物理信息特征工程，使用3量子比特探针电路和279维特征向量，通过随机森林分类器在14,000个样本上实现了84.26%的测试准确率，用于识别近量子处理器的噪声通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有噪声分类方法（如量子过程层析成像）随系统规模指数扩展，不适用于常规校准，需要一种可扩展的噪声分类方案。

Method: 结合结构化经典阴影层析成像与物理信息特征工程，从3量子比特探针电路中提取279维特征向量（随机化泡利测量与可观测导出量），使用随机森林、额外树和多层感知器三种分类器对10种噪声类型进行分类。

Result: 随机森林分类器在14,000个样本上达到最高测试准确率84.26%，宏观F1分数为84.37%，优于其他基线模型。

Conclusion: 许多噪声类型可被高可靠性分类，混淆主要发生在具有相似物理衰变机制的通道之间，未来工作可探索更丰富的探针态和噪声参数估计。

Abstract: Accurate noise classification is essential for operating near-term quantum processors, yet existing approaches, such as quantum process tomography, scale exponentially with system size, limiting their practicality for routine calibration. We propose a scalable noise fingerprinting pipeline that combines structured classical shadow tomography with physics-informed feature engineering to identify noise channels from a fixed set of 3-qubit probe circuits. Each sample is represented by a 279-dimensional feature vector constructed from randomized Pauli measurements and derived observables, designed to resolve physically similar noise channels that produce overlapping signatures under generic measurement sets. We evaluate three classifiers, i.e., random forest, extra trees, and a multilayer perceptron, on a dataset of 14,000 labeled samples spanning 10 noise types. The random forest classifier achieves the highest test accuracy of 0.8426 with a macro F1 score of 0.8437, outperforming both baselines. Confusion analysis reveals that many noise types are classified with high reliability, with the remaining confusions occurring between channels sharing similar physical decay mechanisms, motivating future work on richer probe states and noise parameter estimation.

</details>


### [109] [Benchmarking Large Language Models on Repairing Qiskit Programs using Bugs4Q](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09007)
*Saumya Brahmbhatt,Mitali Hukkeri,Dongchan Kim,M. V. Panduranga Rao,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了量子程序缺陷基准Bugs4Q，发现其标注存在"静默标签反转"问题——基准条目的正确性取决于(基准,版本)组合而非单一基准。在六个Qiskit版本下测试了四个LLM的修复能力，GPT-5.4表现最佳(pass@10=48.8%)，且多数失败源于API不兼容而非错误修复。作者发布了重新验证的版本固定基准，并强调基准验证应先于修复评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子程序缺陷基准Bugs4Q被广泛使用，但其评估假设基准标签始终有效且生成的修复能在目标环境中执行。作者发现基准条目可能因参考修复不再有效或缺陷程序不再复现失败而静默失效，因此需要系统评估基准在不同Qiskit版本下的有效性与LLM修复能力。

Method: 选取Bugs4Q两个版本共67个真实Qiskit缺陷，补全缺失的可执行测试；在六个Qiskit固定版本(0.25.0至2.3.1)下重新运行所有条目；使用四个LLM(GPT-4o-mini、GPT-5o-mini、GPT-5.4、GPT-5.4-mini)每个缺陷生成最多10个修复候选，并在所有版本上测试。

Result: GPT-5.4 pass@10最高(48.8%)，其次GPT-5.4-mini(47.3%)、GPT-5o-mini(30.3%)、GPT-4o-mini(22.6%)；所有模型在Qiskit 0.45.0上表现最佳，Qiskit 1.0过渡后性能下降；64%的成功修复发生在目标版本下无效的条目上。

Conclusion: 量子基准的"静默标签反转"问题损害了评估的有效性，基准验证必须在修复评估之前进行。作者发布了重新验证且版本固定的Bugs4Q基准。

Abstract: In quantum programs, Bugs4Q is a widely used benchmark containing real quantum defects. However, its evaluation assumes that benchmark labels remain valid and that generated fixes execute in the target environment. We evaluate two Bugs4Q versions containing 67 unique real Qiskit defects, adding executable tests where missing, and re-run all entries across six pinned Qiskit releases (0.25.0, 0.45.0, 1.0.0, 1.1.1, 2.0.0, and 2.3.1). We find that quantum benchmarks can suffer from silent label inversion: entries become invalid without errors when reference fixes stop executing or buggy programs no longer reproduce failures. Thus, correctness depends on the (benchmark, version) pair rather than the benchmark alone. We evaluate four LLMs (GPT-4o-mini, GPT-5o-mini, GPT-5.4, and GPT-5.4-mini), generating up to 10 repair candidates per defect and testing them across all versions. GPT-5.4 achieves the highest pass@10 (48.8%), followed by GPT-5.4-mini (47.3%), GPT-5o-mini (30.3%), and GPT-4o-mini (22.6%). All models perform best on Qiskit 0.45.0 and decline after the Qiskit 1.0 transition. Many failures arise from deprecated or incompatible APIs rather than incorrect repairs, and 64\% of successful repairs occur on entries invalid under the target version. We release a re-validated, version-pinned Bugs4Q benchmark and show that benchmark validation must precede repair evaluation.

</details>


### [110] [Automating Just-In-Time Python Type Annotation Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09054)
*Zhipeng Xue,Zhipeng Gao,Xing Hu,Jingyuan Chen,Xin Xia,Shanping Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TypeUp方法，利用LLM在代码变更时自动更新Python类型注解，相比SOTA方法提升41.9%，且20/25的更新已被开发者确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python类型注解在代码变更后常被忽略导致过时不一致，阻碍程序理解、误导开发者甚至引入缺陷，因此需要一种在代码变更时自动更新类型注解的方法。

Method: 提出TypeUp方法，基于LLM通过激发模型知识和逻辑推理能力，并从相似代码变更中学习，根据旧类型注解和对应代码变更自动生成新类型注解。

Result: TypeUp在自动更新类型注解任务上比SOTA类型推断方法TypeGen提升41.9%；在真实世界项目中，20/25的生成更新被开发者确认。

Conclusion: TypeUp能有效实现代码变更时的即时类型注解更新，减少过时注解问题，具备实际应用价值。

Abstract: Type annotations are more and more popular in Python projects to avoid type errors caused by Python's dynamic typing feature. However, when developers change source code, these type annotations are often neglected or overlooked, resulting in outdated and inconsistent type annotations. Such obsolete type annotations can hinder program comprehension, mislead developers, and even introduce bugs in the future. Therefore, it is necessary to avoid and correct these inconsistent type annotations from the very beginning. In this work, we argue that obsolete type annotations can be reduced and even avoided by automatically updating type annotations alongside code changes. We refer to this task as "Just-In-Time (JIT) type annotation updating". To solve this task, we propose a novel LLM-based approach named TypeUp (Type Annotation Updator) to automate this task. TypeUp can automatically generate new type annotations based on the old type annotations and corresponding code changes. Specifically, TypeUp guides LLM to perform type annotation updates by eliciting its knowledge and logical reasoning power and learning from similar code changes. The evaluation results show that TypeUp outperforms state-of-the-art type infer approach (i.e., TypeGen) by 41.9% on our task. Moreover, we conducted an in-the-wild evaluation with real-world software projects, 20 out of 25 type annotation updates generated by our approach have already been confirmed by developers, showing our approach's practical value in real-world environments.

</details>


### [111] [Inside the Skill Market: From Software Engineering Activities to Reusable Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09065)
*Jialun Cao,Xinru Yan,Songqiang Chen,Yaojie Lu,Zhongxin Liu,Shing-Chi Cheung*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对软件工程（SE）技能在公开仓库和市场中作为可复用构件进行了大规模实证研究，分析了技能封装的SE活动、生命周期覆盖、演化特征及评估机制，发现SE活动正越来越多地被转化为可复用的技能构件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理的兴起使得技能（Skills）成为一种新型可复用构件，但现有研究缺乏对SE特定技能及其在软件开发生命周期中覆盖情况的系统理解。

Method: 收集并分析了公开仓库和市场中的大规模SE技能语料库，从封装活动、生命周期覆盖、演化特征和评估机制四个维度进行实证分析。

Result: SE活动正越来越多地通过技能形式成为可复用构件；揭示了技能在生命周期各阶段的覆盖分布；发现了技能推荐和工程化结构化的研究机会；指出了封装高上下文SE活动为可复用技能需要新机制。

Conclusion: 本研究首次提供了以活动为中心的SE技能特征刻画，揭示了SE活动向可复用技能转化的趋势，为技能复用、生态系统发展和代理驱动的SE未来提供了新见解。

Abstract: Software engineering (abbrev. SE) has continuously evolved through increasingly powerful forms of reuse, from source code and libraries to components and services. Recent advances in AI agents have introduced a potentially new reusable artifact: skills. Emerging agent skill repositories and marketplaces enable developers to package, share, and reuse SE expertise as reusable skills. This trend raises a fundamental question: what SE activities are being encapsulated into reusable skills?
