Existing studies primarily focus on a broad range of skills acquisition, safety, or benchmarking, while lacking a systematic understanding of SE-specific skills and their coverage across the software development lifecycle. To address this gap, we conduct the first large-scale empirical study of SE skills in public repositories and marketplaces. We collect and analyze a large corpus of SE skills, examining the activities they encapsulate, lifecycle coverage, evolution characteristics, and evaluation mechanisms. Our findings reveal that SE activities are increasingly becoming reusable artifacts via skills and suggest promising research opportunities for skill recommendation and engineering-oriented structuring, as well as the need for mechanisms to encapsulate high-context SE activities into reusable skills. Overall, our study provides the first activity-centric characterization of SE skills and reveals how SE activities are increasingly being transformed into reusable skills. These findings offer new insights into skill reuse, ecosystem development, and the future of agent-centric SE.

</details>


### [112] [Agentic Proof and Property-Based Testing via Property-Templates in Data-Intensive Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09072)
*Seongmin Lee,Yaoxuan Wu,Miryung Kim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出属性模板(Property Templates)方法，通过形式化证明(Lean 4)与基于属性的测试(PBT)双轨验证框架，解决AI驱动软件开发中意图规约与验证的瓶颈。在Apache Spark上的评估表明，该方法将代理证明工程成功率提升最高2.6倍，减少59%的证明幻觉，并将PBT意图偏差从22降至1，合成成本降低最高5.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI编码成本降低，软件工程的新瓶颈转向意图规约与验证。传统模糊测试不足以应对AI驱动编程的"耐久性危机"，需要形式化证明与基于属性的系统测试互补验证，但这面临候选属性验证和PBT无幻觉操作化两个子问题。

Method: 提出属性模板(Property Templates)——抽象的、参数化的模式模板，将数据分区、计算分解和数据流计算等原则编码为可复用模板。构建双轨验证框架：在Lean 4定理证明器中进行形式化证明，同时实例化PBT模板为可执行的PySpark测试。通过代理(Agentic)方式自动完成模板填充与验证。

Result: 属性模板使代理证明工程成功率提升最高2.6倍(平均1.6倍)，证明幻觉减少59%；模板引导的PBT合成将意图偏差从22降至1，合成成本降低最高5.7倍(平均3.8倍)；代码覆盖率优于SOTA Spark fuzzer，接近无引导的LLM-Based PBT；双轨对比能识别形式模型与实现之间的差距。

Conclusion: 属性模板有效解决了AI驱动编程中属性验证的两个子问题，形式化证明与PBT的双轨验证框架具有互补性，能发现形式模型与实现之间的不一致，为应对AI编码的耐久性危机提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As the cost of code generation becomes cheaper with AI, the new bottleneck in software engineering has shifted to intent specification and validation. Overcoming this durability crisis of AI-driven coding requires more than traditional fuzzing: each candidate property must be proven correct over a model and shown to hold on the real implementation, making formal proof and systematic property-based testing (PBT) complementary. However, validating properties this way at scale requires solving two subproblems: verifying candidate properties and operationalizing PBT without AI hallucination. We hypothesize that recurring property patterns, cast as property templates--abstract, parameterized forms with holes--address both at once.
  This paper investigates recurring property patterns in Apache Spark. In data-intensive scalable computing systems, correctness properties arise from the principles of data partition, computation decomposition, and dataflow computation. For instance, aggregation decomposition relates a global function executed on the entire dataset to a local function followed by a recombiner. We design an agentic, dual-track validation framework that uses property templates to formally verify correctness in the Lean 4 theorem prover and instantiate PBT templates as executable PySpark tests. Our evaluation shows that property templates increase agentic proof engineering success by up to 2.6x (1.6x on average) and reduce proof hallucinations by 59%. Template-guided PBT synthesis reduces intent misalignments from 22 to 1 and cuts synthesis cost by up to 5.7x (3.8x on average). Template-guided synthesis further exceeds a state-of-the-art Spark fuzzer and approaches unguided LLM-based PBT on code coverage. Finally, comparing the two tracks is informative: when a proof succeeds yet a PBT finds a counterexample, the mismatch identifies a gap between the formal model and implementation.

</details>


### [113] [Multi-Agent LLM Collaboration for Unit Test Generation via Human-Testing-Inspired Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09101)
*Quanjun Zhang,Ye Shang,Siqi Gu,Jianyi Zhou,Chunrong Fang,Zhenyu Chen,Liang Xiao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TestAgent提出了一种基于多智能体协作的LLM单元测试生成方法，通过需求规划器、测试生成器和测试审查器三个专门智能体模拟人类测试实践，结合动态工具API调用和测试专用知识图谱，在Java项目上实现了97.46%执行率、92.34%行覆盖率和83.69%变异得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动单元测试生成方法存在两个主要局限：(1)遵循僵化的程序化工作流，未能充分利用LLM的自主推理能力，难以根据实时反馈动态调整测试策略；(2)依赖基于规则的上下文提取，未针对测试生成进行定制，无法捕获细粒度代码依赖和测试特定知识，导致测试需求推导不充分。

Method: TestAgent通过多智能体协作机制模拟人类测试实践：(1)设计三个专门智能体——需求规划器(receipt planner)、测试生成器(test generator)和测试审查器(test reviewer)，分别模拟理解、构建和验证测试的过程；(2)配备一组可动态按需调用的工具API，释放LLM的自主能力；(3)通过静态分析构建测试专用知识图谱，捕获代码实体及其跨项目依赖，并持久化存储测试生成过程中产生的测试工件(如测试报告和失败分析)。

Result: 在六个Java项目上，TestAgent实现了97.46%的执行率、92.34%的行覆盖率、90.24%的分支覆盖率和83.69%的变异得分，在所有指标上均优于基于LLM的基线方法，并取得了显著高于基于搜索的工具的变异得分。

Conclusion: TestAgent通过模拟人类测试实践的多智能体协作机制，有效克服了现有LLM测试生成方法在动态策略调整和上下文理解方面的局限，展示了在自动化单元测试生成任务中的优越性能。

Abstract: Recently, the emergence of Large Language Models (LLMs) has spurred a surge of research into automated unit test generation, yielding impressive performance and reducing manual effort. However, existing LLM-based approaches still suffer from two major limitations: (1) they follow rigid, procedural workflows that underutilize the autonomous reasoning potential of LLMs, making it difficult to dynamically adapt testing strategies based on real-time feedback; and (2) they rely on rule-based context extraction that is not tailored to test generation, failing to capture fine-grained code dependencies and test-specific knowledge required for deriving test requirements. In this paper, we propose TestAgent, an LLM-based test generation approach that addresses the above limitations by emulating human testing practices via a multi-agent collaboration mechanism. Particularly, TestAgent designs three specialized agents, namely a requirement planner, a test generator, and a test reviewer, to simulate how developers understand, construct, and validate unit tests. To unleash the autonomous capabilities of LLMs, we equip TestAgent with a set of tool APIs that can be invoked dynamically in an on-demand and adaptive manner. To further support repository-level reasoning, TestAgent constructs a test-specialized knowledge graph via static analysis, which captures code entities and their dependencies across the project and persistently stores testing artifacts (e.g., test reports and failure analyses) produced during generation. Experimental results show that TestAgent achieves 97.46% execution rate, 92.34% line coverage, 90.24% branch coverage, and 83.69% mutation score on six Java projects, outperforming LLM-based baselines across all metrics and achieving substantially higher mutation scores than search-based tools.

</details>


### [114] [ReProAgent: Tool-Augmented Multi-Stage Agentic Generation of Bug Reproduction Tests from Issue Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09123)
*Quanjun Zhang,Yi Zheng,Ye Shang,Weifeng Sun,Haichuan Hu,Chunrong Fang,Zhenyu Chen,Liang Xiao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ReProAgent，一种多阶段智能体框架，用于从问题报告生成复现测试。该方法将任务分解为缺陷定位、根因分析、测试规划和测试生成四个阶段，集成专用工具进行任务分解、上下文检索和运行时交互。在SWT-bench-lite和SWT-bench-verified上分别达到58.43%和70.30%的复现成功率，平均成本仅$0.14/实例，显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源项目中的问题报告很少包含复现测试，而现有基于大语言模型的提示词流水线方法受限于理解仓库级代码库中问题行为的能力，且缺乏灵活组织复现测试构建的机制。

Method: 提出ReProAgent多阶段智能体框架，包含四个阶段：(1) 缺陷定位——定位相关代码区域；(2) 根因分析——分析问题根本原因；(3) 测试规划——规划复现测试方案；(4) 测试生成——生成具体测试代码。集成三方面工具：任务分解与反思工具、文本来源与仓库图的双重上下文检索工具、执行环境的运行时交互工具。

Result: 在SWT-bench-lite和SWT-bench-verified上分别达到58.43%和70.30%的复现成功率，全面超越所有基线。使用GPT-5-mini时，比同骨干网络的OpenHands分别高出20.43和7.90个百分点。平均成本仅$0.14/实例。在多个骨干LLM上具有泛化能力，且与现有修复方法集成时可提升下游问题修复性能。

Conclusion: ReProAgent通过多阶段智能体架构有效解决了从问题报告生成复现测试的难题，其模块化设计不仅提升了复现测试生成的成功率，还具有良好的成本效益、跨模型泛化能力，并能增强下游的缺陷修复效果。

Abstract: Reproduction tests help developers confirm reported issues and provide executable feedback for issue resolution, yet issue reports in open-source projects rarely include such tests. Recent studies have explored generating issue reproduction tests from issue reports with large language models, but existing approaches largely rely on prompt-based pipelines that retrieve textual context and generate tests. This limits their ability to understand how reported issues behave in repository-scale codebases and to flexibly organize the construction of reproduction tests. In this paper, we propose ReProAgent, a multi-stage agent framework for reproduction test generation from issue reports. ReProAgent decomposes the task into four agent stages: bug localization, root cause analysis, test planning, and test generation. To support these stages, ReProAgent integrates task-specific tools for task decomposition and reflection, context retrieval from both textual sources and repository graphs, and runtime interaction with the execution environment. Experiments on SWT-bench-lite and SWT-bench-verified show that ReProAgent successfully reproduces 58.43% and 70.30% of issues, outperforming all baselines, with an average cost of $0.14 per instance. For example, when equipped with GPT-5-mini, ReProAgent exceeds OpenHands with the same backbone by 20.43 and 7.90 percentage points, respectively. ReProAgent also generalizes across multiple backbone LLMs and improves downstream issue resolution performance when integrated with existing repair approaches.

</details>


### [115] [Exploring the Potential of Program Flowcharts on Code Generation Using Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09146)
*Yuki Toi,Tao Xiao,Kazushi Tomoto,Masanari Kondo,Yasutaka Kamei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探究了向多模态大语言模型（GPT-4o）提供流程图对代码生成性能的影响，发现结合流程图与问题描述可将性能提升最高10%，且流程图详细程度与性能呈正相关趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究探讨视觉输入对LLM的影响以及软件图表的应用，但流程图对多模态LLM代码生成性能的潜在影响仍未被充分探索。

Method: 从AtCoder问题的示例解决方案代码生成流程图，将其与问题描述一同提供给GPT-4o进行代码生成，并对比不同详细程度的流程图及Few-Shot Learning方法的效果。

Result: 结合流程图可将性能提升最高10%；流程图详细程度越高，性能提升趋势越明显；一次学习提供持续改进，而两次学习仅带来微小提升。

Conclusion: 软件图表在多模态LLM驱动的代码生成中具有重要作用，流程图能够有效提升代码生成性能。

Abstract: In recent years, Large Language Models (LLMs) have made significant strides, leading to the emergence of multimodal LLMs capable of processing diverse inputs such as images and audio. Previous research indicates that the supply of multimodal LLMs with combined textual and visual information improves the automatic code generation capabilities. In software development, diagrams such as flowcharts are widely employed to facilitate tasks like code comprehension. While existing studies investigated the impact of visual inputs on LLMs and the usage of software diagrams, the potential influence of providing flowcharts on multimodal LLM performance remains underexplored. In this study, we generated flowcharts from example solution code for AtCoder problems and provided these visual aids alongside problem statements to GPT-4o for code generation. Our findings demonstrate that integrating flowcharts with problem statements yields performance improvements of up to 10%. Furthermore, when employing abstracted flowcharts, we observed a trend indicating that increasing levels of flowchart detail correlate with enhanced performance. Additionally, we compared the effectiveness of flowchart provision to Few-Shot Learning approaches. The findings suggest that one-shot learning provides sustainable improvements, whereas two-shot learning results in only minor improvements. Our work highlights the importance of software diagrams in supporting multimodal LLM-driven code generation.

</details>


### [116] [Evaluating Semantic and Quality-Aware Retrieval for Source Code Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09161)
*Marek Horváth,Emília Pietriková*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究评估了一个结合函数级碎片化、文本与代码嵌入、ChromaDB向量存储、LLM质量元数据和四种检索模式的源代码检索原型系统。在C语言教育语料库上，语义检索在15个手工判定查询中达到nDCG@5 0.820、Success@5 0.800、MRR 0.644，自动路由选择准确率100%，LLM质量评分在9/12个片段上与人工评估相差1分以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于关键词的源代码检索在应对自然语言查询、实现意图以及代码质量等非精确匹配场景时存在局限，需要更先进的检索方法来弥补这一不足。

Method: 构建了一个原型检索系统，整合了函数级碎片化、文本与代码嵌入、ChromaDB向量存储、LLM生成的质量元数据，并实现了语义检索、质量过滤检索、混合检索和自动路由四种检索模式。使用包含563个匿名标识符、8,951个C文件的完整教育语料库进行评估，可复现的10%索引样本包含56个标识符、847个文件和3,839个代码片段。

Result: 在15个人工判定查询上，语义检索的nDCG@5为0.820，Success@5为0.800，MRR为0.644。自动路由在所有15个查询中均选择了预期模式。在小型人工审计中，12个片段中有9个的LLM质量评分与人工评估相差1分以内。语义检索是整体最强的模式，显式质量元数据在明确的面向质量查询中最有用。

Conclusion: 该原型系统在代码检索方面表现出色，语义检索模式整体最优，自动路由准确率100%，LLM生成的质量元数据具有一定的可靠性，为后续改进提供了参考基础。

Abstract: Keyword-based retrieval is limited for source-code repositories when queries are expressed in natural language or concern implementation intent and code quality rather than exact tokens. This study evaluates a prototype retrieval system that combines function-level fragmentation, text-and-code embeddings, ChromaDB vector storage, LLM-derived quality metadata, and four retrieval modes: semantic, quality-filtered, hybrid, and automatic routing. The concrete evaluation uses an educational C-code corpus. The full corpus contains 563 anonymized programmer identifiers and 8,951 C files; a reproducible 10% indexed sample contains 56 programmer identifiers, 847 files, and 3,839 fragments. Across 15 manually judged queries, semantic retrieval achieved nDCG@5 of 0.820, Success@5 of 0.800, and MRR of 0.644. The automatic router selected the expected mode for all 15 queries. In a small manual audit, LLM-derived quality scores were within one point of the manual assessment for 9 of 12 fragments. Within the reported query set, semantic retrieval was the strongest overall mode, while explicit quality metadata was most useful for explicitly quality-oriented queries.

</details>


### [117] [Attention to Detail: Evaluating Energy, Performance, and Accuracy Trade-offs Across vLLM Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09172)
*Nada Zine,Tristan Coignion,Vincenzo Stoico,Clément Quinton,Romain Rouvoy,Patricia Lago*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模对照实验（9000次运行，93600次测量），系统研究了vLLM推理引擎的三种配置选项（注意力核类型、前缀缓存、分块预填充）对5个开源大语言模型在5种推理任务上的能耗、延迟和准确率的影响。结果表明，配置选项显著影响能耗和性能，主要受注意力类型和前缀缓存驱动，分块预填充影响有限；效果高度依赖模型和工作负载，无通用最优配置；模型选择主导全局权衡，配置调优提供局部帕累托改进；推理配置还会意外影响模型准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注模型架构和硬件加速，但推理引擎配置（如vLLM）对能耗、性能和输出质量的影响尚不明确，缺乏系统性的理解。

Method: 对vLLM的三种配置选项（注意力核类型、前缀缓存、分块预填充）的所有组合进行全面评估，涉及5个开源LLM和5种推理任务，总计数千次运行和数万次测量，分析主效应和交互效应。

Result: 注意力类型和前缀缓存对能耗和性能影响显著，分块预填充影响有限；效果高度依赖模型和工作负载；无通用最优配置；模型选择主导全局权衡，配置调优提供局部改进；推理配置可影响模型准确性。

Conclusion: 推理引擎配置对LLM部署的能耗和性能有显著影响，但需根据具体模型和任务进行针对性调优，无法一刀切；模型选择比配置调优对全局权衡的影响更大。

Abstract: Large Language Models are reshaping how software is developed and maintained. They are typically deployed in production using inference engines such as vLLM, which can efficiently serve pre-trained, highly configurable models. While prior work has focused on model architectures and hardware acceleration, the impact of inference engine configuration on energy consumption, performance, and output quality remains poorly understood.
  In this paper, we present a large-scale controlled study of three selected vLLM configuration options: attention kernel type, prefix caching, and chunked prefill. We evaluate all combinations of these configurations across 5 open-weight LLMs and 5 diverse inference tasks, totaling $9,000$ runs and $93,600$ measures. We analyze energy consumption, latency, and accuracy, and examine both main effects and interaction effects between configuration options and tasks.
  Our results show that the studied configuration options significantly impact energy and performance, mainly driven by attention type and prefix caching, while chunked prefill has a limited effect under the default vLLM serving configuration and evaluated workloads. These effects are highly model- and workload-dependent, and no configuration is universally optimal. We further show that model choice dominates global trade-offs, while configuration tuning provides local improvements along the Pareto frontier. Unexpectedly, inference options can also affect model accuracy.

</details>


### [118] [Git-Assistant: Planning-Based Support for Updating Git Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09224)
*Alfredo Garrachón Ruiz,Tomás de la Rosa,Daniel Borrajo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 Git-Assistant，一种结合大语言模型与自动规划技术的混合 AI 助手，用于辅助开发者执行非平凡的 git 操作。实验表明，将形式推理与 LLM 集成可提高仓库管理的可靠性和减少错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 版本控制系统（如 git）对许多从业者仍具挑战性，现有 LLM 虽能理解开发者意图，但在仓库管理任务中因缺乏形式推理能力而效果有限。

Method: 提出 Git-Assistant 框架，结合 LLM 与自动规划技术：分析仓库上下文，将自然语言请求转化为可执行的命令序列，并通过规划技术确保操作的正确性与安全性。采用合成和随机化的 git 环境进行系统评估，对比纯 LLM 变体与规划增强变体的性能。

Result: 集成形式推理与 LLM 的方法在仓库管理任务中显著提高了可靠性，减少了错误率。

Conclusion: 混合 AI 方法（LLM + 自动规划）在智能开发者辅助方面具有巨大潜力，能够有效弥补纯 LLM 在需要形式推理的 git 操作中的不足。

Abstract: Version control systems are essential for collaborative software development, yet tools like git remain challenging for many practitioners. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) offer promising capabilities for interpreting developer intent, but their effectiveness in repository management tasks is limited by the need for formal reasoning. This work introduces Git-Assistant, an AI-based assistant that combines LLMs with automated planning to support developers in executing non-trivial git operations. The assistant analyzes repository context, translates natural language requests into actionable command sequences, and incorporates planning techniques to ensure correctness and safety. We present a systematic evaluation methodology using synthetic and randomized git environments, comparing the performance of LLM-only and planning-augmented variants across multiple metrics. Experimental results demonstrate that integrating formal reasoning with LLMs improves reliability and reduces errors in repository management, highlighting the potential of hybrid AI approaches for intelligent developer assistance.

</details>


### [119] [How Far Are We from Detecting Flaky Tests? On the Limits of Code-Based Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09345)
*Ömer Oktay Gültekin,Alexander Berndt,Jonathan Bell,Thomas Bach,Sebastian Baltes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现有代码级测试不稳定预测方法存在标签捷径和评估协议缺陷，导致性能被高估。作者构建了两个去偏数据集（C-IDoFT和FlakeBench子集），证明模型在项目分离协议下不如常数基线。重新将问题定位为：观察到的失败是否不稳定，以及给定执行环境下的失败概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于代码的不稳定检测器在基准测试中表现良好，但实际应用有限。作者认为这是由于该领域研究的是错误问题：不稳定不是测试代码的静态属性，仅凭测试代码常常缺乏判断所需的信息。

Method: 1) 分析三种代码级检测器，发现广泛使用的基准存在捷径（shortcuts），标签泄漏导致F1分数虚高，评估方案夸大泛化性。2) 构建C-IDoFT数据集（54,468个单元测试，57个GitHub项目），保留开发者确认的不稳定测试子集，用重复执行重建非稳定类。3) 在C-IDoFT上复现CodeBERT版本检测器，对比交叉验证协议与项目分离协议下的表现。4) 在FlakeBench（限制为可从测试代码识别的不稳定类型）上使用项目分离协议评估。5) 从CI日志中挖掘第二个数据集（86个不稳定端到端测试）。

Result: 1) 交叉验证协议下模型远高于常数基线，但项目分离后与常数基线无差异，高F1分数依赖于标签捷径和评估协议。2) 在FlakeBench上，项目分离协议下模型识别出几乎所有不稳定测试。3) 从CI日志挖掘的数据集中，42%的不稳定测试可从代码和日志找到原因，58%需要进一步执行证据。

Conclusion: 不应放弃不稳定预测，而应重新框架为：观察到的失败是否由不稳定引起，以及测试在特定执行环境下的失败概率。提供的数据集和CI挖掘方法支持这一新方向。

Abstract: Flaky tests pass and fail on the same code version, weakening the signal of test results and disrupting continuous integration (CI) pipelines. Code-based flakiness detectors report strong benchmark results, yet their use in practice remains limited. We argue that the field is studying the wrong problem: Flakiness is not a static property of test code, which often lacks the information needed to decide whether a test is flaky. Analyzing three code-based detectors operating on test code, we found that widely used benchmarks contain shortcuts that inflate reported F1 scores and that evaluation protocols overstate generalizability. To control for these shortcuts, we curated two datasets. The first, C-IDoFT (54,468 unit tests from 57 GitHub projects), keeps a developer-confirmed subset of IDoFT's flaky tests and rebuilds only the non-flaky class from repeated executions instead of fixed versions of flaky tests. C-IDoFT is a controlled counterfactual, not a benchmark for reuse. Our CodeBERT reimplementations of two published detectors scored far above its constant baselines under the published cross-validation protocol but no better than them once projects were separated. The high scores rested on the labeling shortcut and the evaluation protocol, not on the test code. On FlakeBench, a benchmark restricted to flakiness types typically recognizable from test code, and the same project-disjoint protocol, the models identified nearly all flaky tests. The second dataset, mined from CI logs, contains 86 flaky end-to-end tests that passed and failed on the same commit. The test code and CI log yielded a cause for 42% of them; the other 58% required further execution evidence. Rather than abandoning flakiness prediction, we reframe it around whether an observed failure is flaky and how likely a test is to fail given its execution environment. Our datasets and CI-mining method support this direction.

</details>


### [120] [Diversifying to Verify: When Task-Equivalent Programs Differ in Verifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09366)
*Shirley Yu,Ruben Martins*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Diversify2Verify，一个基于LLM的分阶段Why3验证流水线，通过生成多样化的程序实现并利用有界验证器引导的注释修复，将工件级验证率从32.9%提升至52.7%，任务级成功率达67.1%，证明实现多样性有助于发现更易验证的程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序验证对软件正确性至关重要，但生成完全验证的程序在实践中仍然困难。本文研究当多个生成程序意图满足相同任务级语义时，实现结构是否影响自动化可验证性。

Method: 提出Diversify2Verify，一个分阶段的LLM-based Why3流水线：1) 推断表示特定合约；2) 生成并测试多样化的递归式与命令式数组/列表实现；3) 使用有界验证器引导的注释修复进行验证。构建了包含73个任务（整数、数组、列表）的验证基准，产生292个实现变体。

Result: 初始验证96个工件，两次修复后达154个；工件级验证率从32.9%提升至52.7%；73个任务中有49个至少有一个变体通过验证，任务级成功率67.1%。

Conclusion: 任务等价的实现在可验证性上存在显著差异，实现多样性有助于找到更易验证的工件。

Abstract: Program verification is crucial for software correctness, but producing fully verified programs remains difficult in practice. This paper studies whether implementation structure affects automated verifiability when multiple generated programs are intended to satisfy the same task-level semantics.
  We present Diversify2Verify, a staged LLM-based pipeline for Why3 that infers representation-specific contracts, generates and tests diverse recursive and imperative array/list implementations, and attempts verification with bounded verifier-guided annotation repair. We also construct a verification-oriented benchmark of 73 tasks over integers, arrays, and lists, yielding 292 implementation variants. Diversify2Verify verifies 96 artifacts initially and 154 after two repair passes, improving artifact-level verification from 32.9% to 52.7%. At the task level, at least one variant verifies for 49 of 73 tasks, a 67.1% success rate. These results show that task-equivalent implementations can differ substantially in verifiability and that implementation diversity helps find verification-friendly artifacts.

</details>


### [121] [How Do Software Professionals Evaluate AI-Generated Code? (Registered Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09434)
*Samuli Määttä,Hera Arif,Burak Turhan,Paul Ralph,Markus Kelanti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文采用建构主义扎根理论方法，通过调查、半结构化访谈和阶梯访谈，研究软件专业人员如何评估AI生成代码，旨在建立相关理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成式AI工具（如GitHub Copilot、ChatGPT、Claude）在软件开发中广泛使用，但软件专业人员如何评估这些工具生成的代码尚不明确。

Method: 采用建构主义扎根理论方法，结合调查、半结构化访谈和阶梯访谈，计划对20-50名软件专业人员进行迭代访谈直至理论饱和。

Result: 初步调查数据已完成收集，研究正在进行中。

Conclusion: 旨在建立关于软件专业人员如何评估AI生成代码的理论，该理论植根于他们的评估实践、认知和偏好的实际叙述中。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI tools have significantly changed how software professionals write, evaluate, and interact with code. Generative AI tools such as GitHub Copilot, ChatGPT, and Claude are increasingly being integrated into everyday workflows. Despite the growing adoption of and reliance on these tools, it remains unclear as to how software professionals evaluate the code they generate. To explore this topic, we will conduct a constructivist grounded theory study that incorporates a survey, semi-structured interviews, and laddering interviews. With the initial survey data collection complete, we aim to interview 20--50 software professionals iteratively until theoretical saturation is achieved. This research aims to build a theory of how software professionals evaluate AI-generated code, grounded in their accounts of evaluative practices, perceptions, and preferences.

</details>


### [122] [Practical Source Code Recovery from Binary Functions Using Anchor-Based Retrieval and LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09452)
*Charles Edward Gagnon,Steven H. H. Ding,Philippe Charland,Benjamin C. M. Fung*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合逆向工程、基于锚点的源码检索和大语言模型推理的实用流水线，用于从 stripped 二进制函数中恢复源代码。该方法通过提取字符串、常量、外部调用等锚点，利用倒排索引检索候选文件，再用大语言模型对反汇编、反编译代码和源码元数据进行重排序，最终实现对二进制到源码的精确匹配。在高质量源码数据库上对 tcpdump 二进制文件进行测试，汇编指令覆盖率达到 95.2%；在基于 GitHub 的检索数据库上平均指令覆盖率为 35.5%，主要受限于检索遗漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从 stripped 二进制文件中恢复源代码是逆向工程中的关键挑战，现有方法多生成近似伪代码而非直接匹配原始源码，准确率有限。作者希望利用高质量源码数据库和大语言模型推理能力，实现更精确的二进制到源码的匹配。

Method: 使用 Ghidra 提取锚点（字符串、常量、外部调用、可用函数名），通过倒排索引数据库检索候选文件，缩小候选范围至可能的函数片段，再利用大语言模型基于反汇编、反编译代码和源码元数据进行重排序。高置信度匹配可作为后续轮次的锚点。

Result: 在高质量源码数据库上对 stripped 且优化的 tcpdump 二进制进行评估，汇编指令覆盖率达 95.2%；在基于 GitHub 的检索数据库上平均指令覆盖率为 35.5%，主要因检索遗漏导致性能下降。

Conclusion: 源码级二进制恢复在高质量数据库上表现出色，即使在噪声环境中也是一个有用的工具。

Abstract: We present a practical pipeline for recovering source code from stripped binary functions by combining reverse engineering, anchor-based source code retrieval, and large language model reasoning. Our binary-to-source-code retrieval method attempts to identify the source function from a source code database, rather than generating approximate decompiled pseudocode. It extracts anchors such as strings, constants, external calls, and available function names using Ghidra, retrieves candidate files via an inverted-index search database, narrows candidates to likely function snippets, and re-ranks them with a large language model (LLM) based on disassembly, decompiled code, and source metadata. Confident matches can also serve as anchors in later passes. In an evaluation backed by our high-fidelity source code database on a stripped, optimized tcpdump binary, our proposed binary-to-source matching method achieves 95.2% assembly instruction coverage. Experiments on a GitHub-based retrieval database showed lower performance with 35.5% instruction coverage on average, mainly due to retrieval misses. These results show that source-level binary recovery excels with high-quality databases and remains a useful tool in noisy environments.

</details>


### [123] [Failure as a Process: An Anatomy of CLI Coding Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09510)
*Xiangxin Zhao,Han Li,Shuaiting Li,Tianyi Zhao,Earl T. Barr,Federica Sarro,He Ye*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对CLI编码智能体的失败轨迹进行大规模实证研究，通过分析7个前沿模型在3种编码智能体框架下生成的1,794条完整轨迹（超过63,000步），提出了面向过程的失败分析框架，揭示了失败主要在早期步骤由认知错误引发，且往往在无法恢复时才被发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将失败视为最终结果而非时间过程，缺乏对失败如何产生、演变和变得不可恢复的深入理解。本文旨在通过过程导向的框架分析CLI编码智能体失败轨迹的全过程。

Method: 收集7个前沿模型在3种编码智能体框架（OpenHands、MiniSWE、Terminus2）上生成的3,843条执行轨迹，筛选出1,794条完整有效轨迹进行人工标注（超过63,000步），从失败发生、根本原因、恢复和跨系统一致性四个维度进行分析。

Result: 编码智能体的失败主要由认知错误驱动，通常在前几个执行步骤内开始出现，且往往在恢复已不再可能时才被发现。研究得出了覆盖失败发生、根本原因、恢复和跨系统一致性的14项发现。

Conclusion: 提高编码智能体的可靠性需要在早期进行验证和干预，而非仅依赖最终结果的评估。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) coding agents are increasingly deployed to autonomously perform software engineering tasks in terminal-based environments, making their reliability a growing concern. Existing empirical studies investigate why coding agents fail, yet they largely treat failure as a final outcome rather than a temporal process, providing limited insight into how failures emerge, evolve, and become unrecoverable. We present the first large-scale empirical study of CLI coding-agent failure trajectories, introducing a process-oriented framework that analyzes failure through its onset, evolution, and recovery across execution trajectories. We first collect 3,843 execution trajectories generated by seven frontier models across three coding-agent scaffolds (OpenHands, MiniSWE, and Terminus2) on Terminal-Bench, then carefully filter them to obtain 1,794 complete and valid trajectories for manual annotation (over 63,000 execution steps), from which we derive 14 findings spanning failure occurrence, root causes, recovery, and cross-system consistency. Our findings show that coding-agent failures are predominantly driven by epistemic errors, typically begin within the first few execution steps, and often remain hidden until recovery is no longer possible, suggesting that improving coding-agent reliability requires earlier validation and intervention rather than relying solely on final-outcome evaluation.

</details>


### [124] [Balancing Usefulness and Naturalness: An LLM-based Curation Pipeline for Code Review Comments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09524)
*Oussama Ben Sghaier,Martin Weyssow,Houari Sahraoui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出两种面向代码评审数据集的质量优化管道：CuREV通过LLM系统改写所有评论提升清晰度与文明性，改进版则基于质量评估分离高低质量评论，用高质量评论作为上下文示例引导低质量评论改写，使生成的评论更真实多样，最终提升下游LLM任务（评论生成与代码优化）的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码评审数据集存在噪声大、不一致、结构差等问题，限制了LLM在自动化代码评审任务中的学习效果。

Method: 1) CuREV管道：利用LLM对所有评审评论进行系统性改写，提升清晰性、简洁性和文明性，同时保留语义意图；2) 改进管道：先基于评估框架将数据集分为高质量与低质量评论，保留高质量评论原文，并将其作为上下文示例指导低质量评论的改写，通过变化示例实现风格多样性。

Result: CuREV数据集提供更干净、更高质量的评论，在下游任务（评论生成与代码优化）中带来可测量的性能提升；改进管道生成的评论不仅更清晰、更具可操作性，还展现出更广泛的写作风格，更接近真实人工评论。

Conclusion: 通过系统性数据质量控制与质量感知的改写策略，可以有效提升代码评审数据集的质量，进而增强LLM在自动化代码评审任务中的表现。

Abstract: Code review is a cornerstone of software development, where reviewers provide feedback through written comments to ensure code quality, maintainability, and correctness. The effectiveness of this process hinges on the quality of review comments. As large language models (LLMs) gain traction in automating code review tasks, the utility of these systems is directly limited by the quality of the datasets on which they are trained. Unfortunately, existing code review datasets are often noisy, inconsistent, or poorly structured, which hinders the ability of LLMs to learn to generate accurate, helpful, and human-like review comments.
  To overcome these limitations, we propose two different curation pipelines designed to improve both the quality and the utility of large-scale code review datasets. In the first pipeline, all review comments are systematically reformulated by an LLM to improve their clarity, conciseness, and civility while preserving their semantic intent. The curated dataset resulting from this approach, called CuREV, offers cleaner, higher-quality, and easier-to-learn-from comments that lead to measurable improvements in downstream automation tasks, namely review comment generation and code refinement. Building on this, we propose an improved pipeline, guided by high-quality exemplars, that enhances the realism and diversity of curated review comments. This method first separates the dataset into high-quality and low-quality reviews, based on a systematic quality assessment using an evaluation framework. High-quality comments are preserved in their original form and further used as in-context exemplars to inspire the reformulation of low-quality comments. By varying the exemplars provided, the reformulated comments are not only clearer and more actionable but also exhibit a broader range of writing styles, making them more realistic and human-like.

</details>


### [125] [Writing Bug Reports for Software Repair Agents: What Information Matters Most?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09553)
*Vincenzo Luigi Bruno,Alessandro Giagnorio,Daniele Bifolco,Leon Wienges,Massimiliano Di Penta,Gabriele Bavota*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了在智能体优先的软件开发流程中，如何撰写错误报告以最大化AI代理生成正确修复的概率。基于SWE-bench Verified基准中的441个真实错误报告，通过人工标注信息类型（如观察行为、期望行为、复现步骤、定位线索、建议修复等），并使用三种LLM后端运行修复代理，建立二项回归模型分析各信息类型与修复成功率的关联。结果表明：定位线索和建议修复（代码或自然语言形式）与修复成功率最强正相关，而传统上对人类有用的复现步骤则贡献较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着软件开发向智能体优先的工作流转变，AI代理开始负责根据问题报告生成修复。然而，现有研究缺乏对"如何撰写错误报告才能让AI代理更好地理解和修复问题"的系统性理解。该研究旨在填补这一空白，探究哪些信息类型能使错误报告具备"代理就绪"（agent-ready）特性。

Method: 基于SWE-bench Verified基准中的500个真实仓库问题，人工分类变更类型并标注每条句子的信息类型（观察行为、期望行为、复现步骤、定位线索、建议修复）。聚焦441个错误报告，使用mini-swe-agent搭配三种LLM后端（GPT-5-mini、MiniMax M2.5、Gemini 3 Flash）运行修复实验，构建二项回归模型估计各信息类型与修复成功率的增量关联，并控制混淆因素。同时进行消融实验验证结论。

Result: 定位线索（如受影响代码区域引用）与修复成功正相关；建议修复（代码或自然语言形式）与通过概率呈现最强的正相关。消融实验表明，传统上对人类有用的信息（如复现步骤）对代理帮助较小，而能够暴露修复方向的信息（定位线索或建议修复）对代理更有价值。

Conclusion: 智能体优先的错误报告应重点包含能缩小代理搜索和修复空间的信息，特别是定位线索和建议修复，而非传统人类友好的复现步骤。这为编写面向AI代理的错误报告提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Software development is increasingly moving toward agentic-first workflows. This includes AI agents responsible for generating initial fixes for submitted issue reports. In this setting, issue reports are no longer merely documentation for human maintainers; they become the primary task specification for the agent. However, little is known about how such reports should be written to maximize the agent's chances of producing a correct fix. We study what makes a bug report agent-ready. Starting from the SWE-bench Verified benchmark (i.e., a collection of 500 real repository issues with human-written gold patches and test suites for evaluating generated fixes) we manually classify each issue by change type (e.g., bug fix vs refactoring) and annotate each sentence with its information type, such as observed behavior, expected behavior, reproduction steps, localization cues, and suggested fixes. We focus on the 441 issues representing bug reports, and we run on them mini-swe-agent using three LLM backbones (i.e., GPT-5-mini, MiniMax M2.5, and Gemini 3 Flash). We then fit a binomial regression model to estimate the incremental association between each information type and agent success, controlling for confounding factors. Our results suggest that agentic-first reports benefit most from information that narrows the agent's search and repair space. Localization cues, such as references to affected code areas, are positively associated with successful repairs, while suggested fixes, expressed either in code or natural language, show some of the strongest positive associations with pass probability. An ablation study removing selected information types confirms that agents benefit less from information traditionally useful to humans, such as reproduction steps, and more from sentences that expose a repair direction, either through bug localization or a suggested fix.

</details>


### [126] [REFORGE: A Method for Benchmarking LLMs' Reverse Engineering Capabilities in Decompiled Binary Function Naming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07738)
*Nicolas Koller,Andreas u. Schmidt*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Reforge流程，揭示编译器优化导致的二进制与源码对齐不可靠性是LLM辅助逆向工程评估的主要障碍，高置信度产量从87.2%降至65.9%，并证明未配对比较会因幸存者偏差高估性能衰退。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM辅助二进制分析基准将函数级ground truth构建视为已解决的预处理步骤，报告准确率却不披露可评估函数数量，导致能力评估存在偏差。

Method: 构建Reforge流程：从C源码经编译、DWARF和语法提取、对齐、反编译到函数级ground truth，通过八门置信度漏斗和三层次分层来操作化对齐不确定性。

Result: 在受控微基准上，高置信度产量随优化级别从87.2%降至65.9%；未配对比较因幸存者偏差夸大优化导致的性能衰退。

Conclusion: 公平评估LLM逆向工程能力的关键障碍不是模型能力，而是编译器优化下二进制到源码对齐的可靠性，应采纳不确定性感知的基准测试实践。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to reverse-engineering tasks, and recent threat-intelligence reporting shows them operating inside live offensive-security workflows. Claims about their capability, however, outpace our ability to measure it. Existing benchmarks for LLM-assisted binary analysis treat the construction of function-level ground truth as a solved pre-processing step and report accuracy without disclosing how many functions were reliably evaluable. We argue that the principal obstacle to fair evaluation is not model capability but the reliability of binary-to-source alignment under compiler optimization. This paper presents Reforge, a provenance-tracked pipeline that constructs function-level ground truth from C source through compilation, DWARF and syntactic extraction, alignment, and decompilation, and that operationalizes alignment uncertainty as an eight-gate confidence funnel with three-tier stratification. On a controlled micro-benchmark, high-confidence yield falls from 87.2% to 65.9% across optimization levels, and unpaired comparisons overstate optimization-induced performance decay through survivorship bias. A proof-of-concept evaluation of seven contemporary LLMs on function naming demonstrates the substrate and motivates uncertainty-aware benchmarking practice

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Beyond Topicality: A Conceptual Analysis of Societal Relevance and Its Application to Search Results and AI Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09264)
*Dirk Lewandowski*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Haider和Sundin提出的"社会相关性"概念，该概念旨在弥补传统相关性模型在处理网络有害内容（如 misinformation 和歧视）方面的不足。通过分析系统、用户和社会相关性的组合，论文研究了社会相关性的定义、实际应用及其与信息质量测量的区别，为构建价值驱动的搜索引擎提供了理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主题相关性和用户相关性模型在处理网络中的有害内容（如错误信息和歧视）时存在局限性，亟需一种新的相关性框架来指导搜索引擎产出符合"更大利益"的结果。

Method: 通过分析系统相关性、用户相关性和社会相关性的不同组合方式，研究社会相关性的三个分析性问题：定义、实际应用以及与信息质量测量的区别。

Result: 社会相关性概念虽在理论上尚未充分发展，但为构建价值驱动型搜索引擎提供了重要框架，能够优先考虑伦理结果和公共利益，而非仅仅依赖关键词匹配。

Conclusion: 社会相关性是信息科学中一个有价值的新概念，尽管仍需理论完善，但它为开发注重伦理和社会利益的搜索引擎奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper examines "societal relevance," a concept introduced by Haider and Sundin to address the limitations of traditional relevance models in web search. While topical and user relevance are foundational to information science, they are insufficient for managing harmful content such as misinformation or discrimination found on the uncontrolled web. This study investigates three analytical questions: the definition of societal relevance, its practical application in search systems, and its distinction from information quality measures. By analyzing various combinations of system, user, and societal relevance, the paper explores how search outputs can be optimized for the "greater good". Although the concept remains theoretically underdeveloped, it provides a vital framework for developing value-driven search engines that prioritize ethical outcomes and societal interests over mere keyword matching.

</details>


### [128] [From Raw IDs to Semantic Planning: How Recommender Systems Utilize Information at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09540)
*Changhong Jin,Shiqiu Yang,Roger Zhe Li,Yingjie Niu,Aghiles Salah,Mete Sertkan,Zheng Ju,Xingsheng Guo,Huifeng Guo,Ruihai Dong,Barry Smyth*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了推荐系统从基于原始ID（离散、全局唯一、语义不透明）到语义ID（封装更丰富信息）的演变过程，并提出未来可能走向语义规划阶段——系统先预测下一次曝光的语义目标，再将其实例化为具体物品或生成内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解推荐系统在大规模工业约束下如何利用信息的历史演变规律，并前瞻性地提出未来发展方向，尤其是语义ID逐渐取代原始ID成为核心标识符的趋势。

Method: 通过对推荐系统过去二十年发展的历史性回顾与分析，提出三个递进问题（原始ID为何主导、语义信息为何被封装进ID、超越语义检索后下一步是什么），并引入语义规划作为未来范式。

Result: 识别出推荐系统信息利用的三个阶段：原始ID主导阶段、语义ID兴起阶段、以及未来的语义规划阶段；指出这一转变不仅关乎生成式推荐，更反映了信息利用方式的根本性变革。

Conclusion: 推荐系统正从以原始ID为核心的精确查找范式，转向以语义ID为载体的结构化信息利用范式，未来可能进一步演变为语义规划范式，这要求模型设计、评估方式以及用户-平台-提供者三方目标协调机制都做出相应改变。

Abstract: The evolution of recommender systems can be explored by asking how they utilize information at scale. Throughout most of the historical period under consideration during the past two decades, industrial systems have relied on raw IDs, which are discrete, globally unique, and semantically opaque identifiers that enable exact lookup, logging, and item-specific memorization at scale. Over time, however, recommender systems have sought to utilize richer sources of information, including item content, context, multimodal signals, and cross-domain structure. This development has led to a new stage in which part of such information is no longer used solely as auxiliary features around item identity, but is increasingly encapsulated in semantic IDs that provide a more structured, model-facing form of identity. We argue that this shift goes beyond the rise of generative recommendation over traditional methods. Indeed, it reflects a broader evolution in how recommender systems utilize information under industrial-scale constraints. This paper looks at the past, present, and future to examine three connected questions: why raw IDs dominated the early development of recommender systems, why semantic information is increasingly being encapsulated in IDs today, and what may come next once recommendations move beyond semantic retrieval. In particular, we introduce semantic planning as a possible future direction in which the system first predicts the semantic target of the next exposure, and only then instantiates that target as a specific item or generated creative. We further argue that such a shift may require changes not only in model design but also in evaluation and in the way recommender systems coordinate the objectives of users, platforms, and providers.

</details>
